/**
 * src/assets/icons/iconMap.ts
 * Zero Emoji Policy - Vector Icons Only
 * Uses react-native-vector-icons MaterialCommunityIcons
 * All 5 Modes + Drawer + Lock + Search + Percentage + Theme/Language Icons
 */

// Type helpers
export type IconName = typeof IconMap.modes.m1.name;
export type ModeIconKey = keyof typeof IconMap.modes;
export type AllIcons = typeof IconMap;

// Utility: Get icon by mode number
export const getModeIcon = (mode: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5) => {
  const key = `m${mode}` as ModeIconKey;
  return IconMap.modes[key];
};

// Utility: Get icon name string for react-native-vector-icons
export const getModeIconName = (mode: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5): string => {
  return getModeIcon(mode).name;
};

// Golden Rules for Icon Usage (From Architecture)
export const IconUsageRules = {
  size: {
    standard: 22, // Card header layout perfect
    drawer: 20,
    header: 24,
    progressBadge: 16,
  },
  style: "outline" as const, // All icons Outline/Thin category for dark/light balance
  rule1:
    "Arrow বর্জন: Action বোঝাতে Arrow এর বদলে নির্দিষ্ট ক্রিয়ার Icon (Play, Filter, Setting, Tune) ব্যবহার",
  rule2:
    "Outline Style: সকল Icon Outline বা Thin Category (e.g., lock-outline) - Dark & Light উভয় Mode এ Balance",
  rule3:
    "Sizing: সাধারণ Drawer & Card Icon 20-22px, Font/Header Icon 24px Standard",
} as const;

export default IconMap;

/**
 * src/components/Cards/EventCard.tsx
 * Daily Event Center Card - No Extra Text, Only [Icon] [Chapter #ID] [+1%]
 * Vector Icons Only, No Emoji
 * Example: [lightbulb-on-outline] অপরিচিতা #৩৫ [+1%]
 */

import React from "react";
import { View, Text, Pressable, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import Icon from "react-native-vector-icons/MaterialCommunityIcons";
import {
  Radius,
  Spacing,
  getCardContainerStyle,
  getInnerRadius,
} from "../../theme/radius";
import { Typography } from "../../theme/typography";

export interface EventCardProps {
  id: string;
  iconName: string; // volume-high, play-circle-outline etc
  title: string; // "অপরিচিতা #৩৫, #৭৮, #৮৮"
  creditPercent: number; // +1%
  isCompleted?: boolean;
  isLocked?: boolean;
  stage: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;
  mode: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5;
  onPress?: (id: string) => void;
  onCompleted?: (id: string) => void;
}

export const EventCard: React.FC<EventCardProps> = ({
  id,
  iconName,
  title,
  creditPercent,
  isCompleted = false,
  isLocked = false,
  stage,
  mode,
  onPress,
}) => {
  const handlePress = () => {
    if (isLocked || isCompleted) {
      return;
    }
    onPress?.(id);
  };

  return (
    <Pressable
      onPress={handlePress}
      style={({ pressed }) => [
        styles.container,
        getCardContainerStyle(false, {
          surface: "#FFFFFF",
          border: "#E7E0EC",
          primary: "#6750A4",
        }),
        isCompleted && styles.completedContainer,
        isLocked && styles.lockedContainer,
        pressed && !isLocked && !isCompleted && styles.pressedContainer,
      ]}
      disabled={isLocked || isCompleted}
      testID={`event-card-${id}`}
    >
      {/* Left: Vector Icon (No Emoji) */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.iconContainer,
          {
            backgroundColor: isCompleted
              ? "#E8F5E9"
              : isLocked
              ? "#F3EDF7"
              : "#E8DEF8",
          },
        ]}
      >
        <Icon
          name={iconName as any}
          size={20}
          color={isCompleted ? "#2E7D32" : isLocked ? "#938F99" : "#6750A4"}
        />
      </View>

      {/* Middle: Chapter + Display IDs (No extra text) */}
      <View style={styles.middle}>
        <Text
          style={[
            styles.title,
            isLocked && styles.lockedText,
            isCompleted && styles.completedText,
          ]}
          numberOfLines={2}
        >
          {title}
        </Text>
        <Text style={styles.stageLabel}>
          পর্যায় {stage} • Mode {mode}
        </Text>
      </View>

      {/* Right: [+1%] Badge */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.creditBadge,
          isCompleted && styles.creditCompleted,
          isLocked && styles.creditLocked,
        ]}
      >
        {isLocked ? (
          <Icon name="lock-outline" size={14} color="#938F99" />
        ) : isCompleted ? (
          <Icon name="check" size={14} color="#2E7D32" />
        ) : (
          <Text
            style={[
              styles.creditText,
              isCompleted && styles.creditTextCompleted,
            ]}
          >
            +{creditPercent}%
          </Text>
        )}
      </View>
    </Pressable>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    padding: 16,
    borderRadius: 16,
    backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#E7E0EC",
    marginVertical: 6,
    marginHorizontal: 16,
    overflow: "hidden",
  },
  completedContainer: {
    backgroundColor: "#E8F5E9",
    borderColor: "#81C784",
    opacity: 0.8,
  },
  lockedContainer: {
    backgroundColor: "#F3EDF7",
    borderColor: "#E7E0EC",
    opacity: 0.6,
  },
  pressedContainer: {
    transform: [{ scale: 0.98 }],
    backgroundColor: "#E8DEF8",
  },
  iconContainer: {
    width: 36,
    height: 36,
    borderRadius: 12, // Inner = Outer - Padding
    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",
    marginRight: 12,
  },
  middle: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  title: {
    fontSize: 14,
    fontWeight: "600",
    color: "#1C1B1F",
    lineHeight: 20,
  },
  stageLabel: {
    fontSize: 11,
    color: "#49454F",
    marginTop: 2,
  },
  lockedText: {
    color: "#938F99",
  },
  completedText: {
    color: "#2E7D32",
    textDecorationLine: "line-through",
  },
  creditBadge: {
    minWidth: 44,
    height: 28,
    borderRadius: 14,
    backgroundColor: "#E8DEF8",
    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",
    paddingHorizontal: 8,
    marginLeft: 8,
  },
  creditCompleted: {
    backgroundColor: "#C8E6C9",
  },
  creditLocked: {
    backgroundColor: "#E7E0EC",
  },
  creditText: {
    fontSize: 12,
    fontWeight: "700",
    color: "#6750A4",
  },
  creditTextCompleted: {
    color: "#2E7D32",
  },
});

export default EventCard;

FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/components/Cards/ModeCard.tsx
=====================================
/**
 * src/components/Cards/ModeCard.tsx
 * Core Word Detail Card - m1-m5 Modes
 * Layout: 40% Top Header, 20% Core Content (Fixed Width, Unlimited Height), 20% Bottom Padding
 * Features: TTS Play/Stop, Yellow+Primary Highlight, Border Glow, MMKV Progress
 */

import React, { useCallback } from "react";
import { View, Text, Pressable, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import Icon from "react-native-vector-icons/MaterialCommunityIcons";
import { useTTS } from "../../hooks/useTTS";
import IconMap, { getModeIcon } from "../../assets/icons/iconMap";
import { useTheme } from "@react-native-material/core";
import {
  Radius,
  Spacing,
  getCardContainerStyle,
  getInnerRadius,
} from "../../theme/radius";
import { Typography, FontSizeControl } from "../../theme/typography";

export interface ModeCardProps {
  word_id: number;
  display_word_id: number;
  chapter_name: string;
  content: string;
  mode: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5;
  isActive?: boolean;
  fontSize?: number;
  onProgressUpdate?: (word_id: number, mode: number) => void;
  onComplete?: (word_id: number) => void;
}

export const ModeCard: React.FC<ModeCardProps> = ({
  word_id,
  display_word_id,
  chapter_name,
  content,
  mode,
  isActive = false,
  fontSize = 16,
  onProgressUpdate,
  onComplete,
}) => {
  const theme = useTheme() as any;
  const colors = theme?.colors || {
    surface: "#FFFFFF",
    border: "#E7E0EC",
    primary: "#6750A4",
    text: "#1C1B1F",
    highlightYellow: "#FFEB3B",
    highlightPrimaryGlow: "rgba(103, 80, 164, 0.15)",
  };

  const modeKey = `m${mode}` as keyof typeof IconMap.modes;
  const iconInfo = IconMap.modes[modeKey];
  const modeLabel = `m${mode}`;

  // TTS Hook - Implements m1/m4 Card Tap = Play/Stop, m2/m5 Empty Space = Pause/Resume
  const {
    isPlaying,
    isPaused,
    ttsState,
    handleCardPress,
    handleEmptySpacePress,
    highlightedSegments,
    getCardStyle,
    getTextStyle,
  } = useTTS({
    word_id,
    fullText: content,
    mode: modeLabel as any,
    theme: colors,
    autoPlayGapMs: 300,
    onComplete: (id) => {
      onProgressUpdate?.(id, mode);
      onComplete?.(id);
    },
  });

  // For m1 & m4: Card tap = Play/Stop
  // For m2 & m5: Card tap also triggers but Empty Space is primary controller
  const onCardPress = useCallback(async () => {
    await handleCardPress();
  }, [handleCardPress]);

  const onEmptySpacePress = useCallback(async () => {
    // Only for m2 & m5
    if (mode === 2 || mode === 5) {
      await handleEmptySpacePress();
    }
  }, [mode, handleEmptySpacePress]);

  const cardStyle = getCardStyle(isActive);
  const isHighlighted = isPlaying;

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {/* Top 20% Empty Area - For m2/m5 Controller (Pause/Resume) */}
      {(mode === 2 || mode === 5) && (
        <Pressable
          onPress={onEmptySpacePress}
          style={styles.emptyControlArea}
          testID="empty-top-control"
        >
          <View style={styles.emptyHint} />
        </Pressable>
      )}

      {/* Middle 20% Core Content Area - Fixed Width, Unlimited Height */}
      <Pressable
        onPress={onCardPress}
        style={[
          getCardContainerStyle(isHighlighted, colors),
          cardStyle,
          isActive && styles.activeCard,
          isHighlighted && { borderColor: colors.primary, borderWidth: 1.5 },
        ]}
        testID={`mode-card-${word_id}-${mode}`}
      >
        {/* Header: Icon + Chapter + Display ID */}
        <View style={styles.header}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.iconContainer,
              { backgroundColor: colors.primary + "15" },
            ]}
          >
            <Icon
              name={iconInfo.name as any}
              size={IconMap.modes[modeKey].size}
              color={isPlaying ? colors.primary : "#888888"}
            />
          </View>
          <View style={styles.headerText}>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.chapterName,
                { color: colors.text, fontSize: fontSize - 2 },
              ]}
              numberOfLines={1}
            >
              {chapter_name} #{display_word_id}
            </Text>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.modeLabel,
                { color: "#888888", fontSize: fontSize - 4 },
              ]}
            >
              {iconInfo.label} - {modeLabel}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.statusIcon}>
            {isPlaying ? (
              <Icon name="volume-high" size={18} color={colors.primary} />
            ) : isPaused ? (
              <Icon name="pause" size={18} color="#888888" />
            ) : null}
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Content: Highlighted Text */}
        <View style={styles.contentArea}>
          <Text
            style={[
              styles.contentText,
              { fontSize, lineHeight: fontSize * 1.5 },
            ]}
          >
            {highlightedSegments.map((seg, idx) => (
              <Text key={idx} style={getTextStyle(seg.highlighted) as any}>
                {seg.text}
              </Text>
            ))}
          </Text>
        </View>

        {/* Progress Bar for Active TTS */}
        {isPlaying && (
          <View
            style={[styles.progressBar, { backgroundColor: colors.primary }]}
          />
        )}
      </Pressable>

      {/* Bottom 20% Empty Area - For m2/m5 Controller */}
      {(mode === 2 || mode === 5) && (
        <Pressable
          onPress={onEmptySpacePress}
          style={styles.emptyControlArea}
          testID="empty-bottom-control"
        >
          <View style={styles.emptyHint} />
        </Pressable>
      )}
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    width: "100%",
  },
  emptyControlArea: {
    height: 60, // 20% empty area for m2/m5 control
    width: "100%",
    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",
  },
  emptyHint: {
    width: 40,
    height: 4,
    borderRadius: 2,
    backgroundColor: "#E0E0E0",
    opacity: 0.5,
  },
  activeCard: {
    borderWidth: 1.5,
  },
  header: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  iconContainer: {
    width: 36,
    height: 36,
    borderRadius: 12, // Inner = Outer - Padding
    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",
    marginRight: 12,
  },
  headerText: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  chapterName: {
    fontWeight: "600",
    fontSize: 14,
  },
  modeLabel: {
    fontSize: 12,
    marginTop: 2,
  },
  statusIcon: {
    width: 24,
    height: 24,
    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",
  },
  contentArea: {
    minHeight: 40,
  },
  contentText: {
    fontSize: 16,
    lineHeight: 24,
    color: "#1C1B1F",
  },
  progressBar: {
    height: 3,
    borderRadius: 1.5,
    marginTop: 12,
    width: "100%",
  },
});

export default ModeCard;

/**
 * Src/components/Common/ProgressBadge.tsx & src/components/Common/ProgressBadge.tsx
 * Progress Badge - Chapter Card Small Badge with Percentage
 * Icon: chart-donut-variant + 75% Text
 */

import React from "react";
import { View, Text, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import Icon from "react-native-vector-icons/MaterialCommunityIcons";

export interface ProgressBadgeProps {
  percent: number;
  size?: "small" | "medium" | "large";
  showIcon?: boolean;
  color?: string;
  backgroundColor?: string;
}

export const ProgressBadge: React.FC<ProgressBadgeProps> = ({
  percent,
  size = "medium",
  showIcon = true,
  color = "#6750A4",
  backgroundColor = "#F3EDF7",
}) => {
  const iconSize = size === "small" ? 12 : size === "large" ? 18 : 14;
  const fontSize = size === "small" ? 10 : size === "large" ? 14 : 12;

  return (
    <View
      style={[
        styles.container,
        { backgroundColor },
        size === "small" && styles.smallContainer,
        size === "large" && styles.largeContainer,
      ]}
    >
      {showIcon && (
        <Icon name="chart-donut-variant" size={iconSize} color={color} />
      )}
      <Text style={[styles.text, { color, fontSize }]}>{percent}%</Text>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    paddingHorizontal: 8,
    paddingVertical: 4,
    borderRadius: 12,
    gap: 4,
  },
  smallContainer: { paddingHorizontal: 6, paddingVertical: 2, borderRadius: 8 },
  largeContainer: {
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 6,
    borderRadius: 14,
  },
  text: { fontWeight: "600" },
});

export default ProgressBadge;

/**
 * Src/components/Common/SearchBox.tsx & src/components/Common/SearchBox.tsx
 * Search Box - Magnify + Close Circle Outline, No Hardcode
 */

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { View, TextInput, Pressable, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import Icon from "react-native-vector-icons/MaterialCommunityIcons";

export interface SearchBoxProps {
  value: string;
  onChangeText: (text: string) => void;
  placeholder?: string;
  onClear?: () => void;
}

export const SearchBox: React.FC<SearchBoxProps> = ({
  value,
  onChangeText,
  placeholder = "সার্চ করুন...",
  onClear,
}) => {
  const handleClear = () => {
    onChangeText("");
    onClear?.();
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <Icon name="magnify" size={20} color="#938F99" />
      <TextInput
        value={value}
        onChangeText={onChangeText}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        placeholderTextColor="#938F99"
        style={styles.input}
        returnKeyType="search"
      />
      {value.length > 0 && (
        <Pressable onPress={handleClear} style={styles.clearButton}>
          <Icon name="close-circle-outline" size={20} color="#938F99" />
        </Pressable>
      )}
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    backgroundColor: "#F3EDF7",
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    height: 44,
    borderRadius: 12, // Inner = Outer - Padding
    gap: 8,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#E7E0EC",
  },
  input: { flex: 1, fontSize: 14, color: "#1C1B1F" },
  clearButton: {
    width: 28,
    height: 28,
    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",
  },
});

export default SearchBox;

/**
 * Src/components/Drawer/ChapterProgressDrawer.tsx & src/components/Drawer/ChapterProgressDrawer.tsx
 * Chapter Progress Drawer - Left & Right Drawer with 80% Green Bar, Lock/Unlock, Search
 * Features: Fixed Width, Green Bar Animation, Bank Credit Button Invisible Magic
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  FlatList,
  Pressable,
  StyleSheet,
  TextInput,
} from "react-native";
import Icon from "react-native-vector-icons/MaterialCommunityIcons";
import ChapterEncapsulation from "../../services/ProgressEngine/ChapterEncapsulation";
import ChapterExecutive from "../../services/ProgressEngine/Guards/ChapterExecutive";
import ChapterTable from "../../database/ChapterTable";
import BankManager from "../../storage/DailyBank/BankManager";

export interface DrawerChapterItem {
  chapter_id: number;
  chapter_name: string;
  progressPercent: number;
  mode1: number;
  mode2: number;
  mode3: number;
  mode4: number;
  mode5: number;
  isUnlocked: boolean;
  canUseBank: boolean;
  needsBankCredit: number;
}

export interface ChapterProgressDrawerProps {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onChapterPress: (chapter_id: number) => void;
  position?: "left" | "right"; // left = Filter, right = Event/Bank
  filterMode?: "all" | "unlocked" | "locked";
}

export const ChapterProgressDrawer: React.FC<ChapterProgressDrawerProps> = ({
  visible,
  onClose,
  onChapterPress,
  position = "left",
  filterMode = "all",
}) => {
  const [chapters, setChapters] = useState<DrawerChapterItem[]>([]);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [bankBalance, setBankBalance] = useState(0);

  const loadDrawerData = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const allChapters = await ChapterTable.getAllChaptersWithProgress();
      const bank = BankManager.getBalance().totalBalance;
      setBankBalance(bank);

      const items: DrawerChapterItem[] = [];
      for (const ch of allChapters) {
        const box = ChapterEncapsulation.getChapterBox(ch.chapter_id);
        const exec = await ChapterExecutive.check(ch.chapter_id);

        items.push({
          chapter_id: ch.chapter_id,
          chapter_name: ch.chapter_name,
          progressPercent: box.totalProgressPercent || ch.completedPercent,
          mode1: box.mode1,
          mode2: box.mode2,
          mode3: box.mode3,
          mode4: box.mode4,
          mode5: box.mode5,
          isUnlocked: exec.isUnlocked,
          canUseBank: exec.canUseBank,
          needsBankCredit: exec.needsBankCredit,
        });
      }

      items.sort((a, b) => a.chapter_id - b.chapter_id);
      setChapters(items);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[ChapterProgressDrawer] Load failed:", error);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (visible) {
      loadDrawerData();
    }
  }, [visible, loadDrawerData]);

  const filteredChapters = useMemo(() => {
    let filtered = chapters;

    // Filter by lock state
    if (filterMode === "unlocked") {
      filtered = filtered.filter((c) => c.isUnlocked);
    } else if (filterMode === "locked") {
      filtered = filtered.filter((c) => !c.isUnlocked);
    }

    // Search
    if (searchQuery.trim()) {
      const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      filtered = filtered.filter(
        (c) =>
          c.chapter_name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          c.chapter_id.toString().includes(q)
      );
    }

    return filtered;
  }, [chapters, searchQuery, filterMode]);

  const renderItem = ({ item }: { item: DrawerChapterItem }) => {
    return (
      <Pressable
        onPress={() => {
          if (item.isUnlocked) {
            onChapterPress(item.chapter_id);
            onClose();
          }
        }}
        style={[styles.chapterRow, !item.isUnlocked && styles.chapterRowLocked]}
      >
        <View style={styles.chapterRowHeader}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.iconBox,
              item.isUnlocked ? styles.iconUnlocked : styles.iconLocked,
            ]}
          >
            <Icon
              name={item.isUnlocked ? "book-open-page-variant" : "lock-outline"}
              size={16}
              color={item.isUnlocked ? "#6750A4" : "#938F99"}
            />
          </View>
          <View style={styles.chapterText}>
            <Text style={styles.chapterName} numberOfLines={1}>
              {item.chapter_name}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.chapterId}>#{item.chapter_id}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.percentBadge}>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.percentText,
                !item.isUnlocked && { color: "#938F99" },
              ]}
            >
              {item.progressPercent}%
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Green Progress Bar - 80% Bar */}
        <View style={styles.progressTrack}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.progressFill,
              {
                width: `${item.progressPercent}%`,
                backgroundColor: item.isUnlocked ? "#4CAF50" : "#E7E0EC",
              },
            ]}
          />
        </View>

        {/* Per Mode Mini Progress */}
        <View style={styles.modesMini}>
          {[
            { m: 1, p: item.mode1 },
            { m: 2, p: item.mode2 },
            { m: 3, p: item.mode3 },
            { m: 4, p: item.mode4 },
            { m: 5, p: item.mode5 },
          ].map((mode) => (
            <View key={mode.m} style={styles.miniMode}>
              <Text style={styles.miniModeLabel}>m{mode.m}</Text>
              <View style={styles.miniTrack}>
                <View style={[styles.miniFill, { width: `${mode.p}%` }]} />
              </View>
            </View>
          ))}
        </View>

        {/* Bank Button Invisible Magic */}
        {!item.isUnlocked && item.progressPercent >= 50 && item.canUseBank && (
          <View style={styles.bankButton}>
            <Icon name="lightning-bolt-outline" size={12} color="#FFFFFF" />
            <Text style={styles.bankButtonText}>
              -{item.needsBankCredit}% ব্যাংক
            </Text>
          </View>
        )}
      </Pressable>
    );
  };

  if (!visible) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <View
      style={[
        styles.container,
        position === "left" ? styles.leftDrawer : styles.rightDrawer,
      ]}
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <View style={styles.headerLeft}>
          <Icon
            name={
              position === "left" ? "tune-vertical" : "calendar-check-outline"
            }
            size={20}
            color="#6750A4"
          />
          <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>
            {position === "left" ? "চ্যাপ্টার ফিল্টার" : "ডেইলি ব্যাংক"}
          </Text>
        </View>
        <Pressable onPress={onClose} style={styles.closeButton}>
          <Icon name="close" size={20} color="#49454F" />
        </Pressable>
      </View>

      {/* Bank Balance for Right Drawer */}
      {position === "right" && (
        <View style={styles.bankHeader}>
          <Text style={styles.bankBalance}>{bankBalance}% ক্রেডিট</Text>
          <Text style={styles.bankSub}>মোট উপলব্ধ</Text>
        </View>
      )}

      {/* Search Box */}
      <View style={styles.searchContainer}>
        <Icon name="magnify" size={18} color="#938F99" />
        <TextInput
          value={searchQuery}
          onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
          placeholder="সার্চ করুন..."
          placeholderTextColor="#938F99"
          style={styles.searchInput}
        />
        {searchQuery.length > 0 && (
          <Pressable onPress={() => setSearchQuery("")}>
            <Icon name="close-circle-outline" size={18} color="#938F99" />
          </Pressable>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Chapter List */}
      <FlatList
        data={filteredChapters}
        renderItem={renderItem}
        keyExtractor={(item) => `drawer-chapter-${item.chapter_id}`}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      />
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    position: "absolute",
    top: 0,
    bottom: 0,
    width: "80%",
    backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
    elevation: 8,
    zIndex: 100,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#E7E0EC",
  },
  leftDrawer: {
    left: 0,

  },

/**
 * src/components/Settings/ControlPanel1.tsx
 * First Control Panel: Sound, Emoji, Default Icon Modes (m1, m3, m4)
 * Each has separate single row panel with Chapter Name + Two Sequential Boxes (Range)
 * Auto filter small->big or big->small
 */

import React, { useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { View, Text, TextInput, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import Icon from "react-native-vector-icons/MaterialCommunityIcons";
import IconMap from "../../assets/icons/iconMap";

export interface RangeFilter {
  chapterName: string;
  start: number | null;
  end: number | null;
}

export interface ControlPanel1Props {
  onFilterChange?: (mode: 1 | 3 | 4, filter: RangeFilter) => void;
}

const ModeRow: React.FC<{
  mode: 1 | 3 | 4;
  title: string;
  iconName: string;
  onFilterChange?: (filter: RangeFilter) => void;
}> = ({ mode, title, iconName, onFilterChange }) => {
  const [chapterName, setChapterName] = useState("");
  const [start, setStart] = useState("");
  const [end, setEnd] = useState("");

  const handleChange = useCallback(
    (newChapter: string, newStart: string, newEnd: string) => {
      setChapterName(newChapter);
      setStart(newStart);
      setEnd(newEnd);

      // Auto filter small->big or big->small
      let startNum = newStart ? parseInt(newStart, 10) : null;
      let endNum = newEnd ? parseInt(newEnd, 10) : null;

      if (
        startNum !== null &&
        endNum !== null &&
        !isNaN(startNum) &&
        !isNaN(endNum)
      ) {
        // System auto understands filter
        const min = Math.min(startNum, endNum);
        const max = Math.max(startNum, endNum);
        startNum = min;
        endNum = max;
      }

      onFilterChange?.({
        chapterName: newChapter,
        start: startNum !== null && !isNaN(startNum) ? startNum : null,
        end: endNum !== null && !isNaN(endNum) ? endNum : null,
      });
    },
    [onFilterChange]
  );

  return (
    <View style={styles.modeRow}>
      <View style={styles.modeHeader}>
        <View style={[styles.iconBox, { backgroundColor: "#E8DEF8" }]}>
          <Icon name={iconName as any} size={18} color="#6750A4" />
        </View>
        <Text style={styles.modeTitle}>{title}</Text>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.filterRow}>
        <View style={styles.inputGroup}>
          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>চ্যাপ্টার নাম</Text>
          <TextInput
            value={chapterName}
            onChangeText={(text) => handleChange(text, start, end)}
            placeholder="যেমন: অপরিচিতা"
            placeholderTextColor="#938F99"
            style={styles.input}
          />
        </View>

        <View style={styles.rangeGroup}>
          <View style={styles.rangeInputGroup}>
            <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>শুরু</Text>
            <TextInput
              value={start}
              onChangeText={(text) => handleChange(chapterName, text, end)}
              placeholder="১"
              placeholderTextColor="#938F99"
              keyboardType="number-pad"
              style={[styles.input, styles.rangeInput]}
            />
          </View>

          <Text style={styles.rangeSeparator}>-</Text>

          <View style={styles.rangeInputGroup}>
            <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>শেষ</Text>
            <TextInput
              value={end}
              onChangeText={(text) => handleChange(chapterName, start, text)}
              placeholder="২০"
              placeholderTextColor="#938F99"
              keyboardType="number-pad"
              style={[styles.input, styles.rangeInput]}
            />
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>

      {(chapterName || start || end) && (
        <View style={styles.filterHint}>
          <Icon name="filter-outline" size={14} color="#49454F" />
          <Text style={styles.filterHintText}>
            ফিল্টার: {chapterName || "সব"}{" "}
            {start && end
              ? `(${Math.min(
                  parseInt(start) || 0,
                  parseInt(end) || 0
                )} - ${Math.max(parseInt(start) || 0, parseInt(end) || 0)})`
              : ""}
          </Text>
        </View>
      )}
    </View>
  );
};

export const ControlPanel1: React.FC<ControlPanel1Props> = ({
  onFilterChange,
}) => {
  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>
        প্রথম কন্ট্রোল প্যানেল (সাউন্ড, ইমোজি ও ডিফল্ট)
      </Text>
      <Text style={styles.sectionDesc}>
        প্রতিটি মোডের জন্য আলাদা রো প্যানেল, রেঞ্জ অটো ফিল্টার
      </Text>

      <ModeRow
        mode={1}
        title="সাউন্ড আইকন (m1)"
        iconName="volume-high"
        onFilterChange={(filter) => onFilterChange?.(1, filter)}
      />

      <View style={styles.separator} />

      <ModeRow
        mode={3}
        title="ডিফল্ট আইকন (m3)"
        iconName="book-open-page-variant"
        onFilterChange={(filter) => onFilterChange?.(3, filter)}
      />

      <View style={styles.separator} />

      <ModeRow
        mode={4}
        title="ইমোজি আইকন (m4)"
        iconName="lightbulb-on-outline"
        onFilterChange={(filter) => onFilterChange?.(4, filter)}
      />
    </View>
  );
};

    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#E7E0EC",

=====================================
FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/components/Settings/ControlPanel2.tsx

/**
 * src/components/Settings/ControlPanel2.tsx
 * Second Control Panel: Play Icon 1 & Play Icon 2 (m2 & m5)
 * Features:
 * - Parallel & Random Switch (Mutual Exclusive, can be both off)
 * - Single Card Repeat & Card-to-Card Repeat Switch (Mutual Exclusive)
 * - Single Card Repeat: Box + Chapter Name
 * - Card-to-Card Repeat: 3 Boxes (Chapter + 2 sequential)
 * - Audio Background Switch (Independent)
 * - Two independent panels for m2 & m5
 */

import React, { useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { View, Text, TextInput, Pressable, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import Icon from "react-native-vector-icons/MaterialCommunityIcons";

export interface PlayModeFilters {
  parallel: boolean;
  random: boolean;
  singleRepeat: boolean;
  singleRepeatChapter: string;
  singleRepeatNumber: number | null;
  cardToCardRepeat: boolean;
  cardToCardChapter: string;
  cardToCardStart: number | null;
  cardToCardEnd: number | null;
  backgroundAudio: boolean;
}

export interface ControlPanel2Props {
  mode: 2 | 5;
  title: string;
  onFilterChange?: (mode: 2 | 5, filters: PlayModeFilters) => void;
}

const SwitchRow: React.FC<{
  label: string;
  description: string;
  value: boolean;
  onValueChange: (value: boolean) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
  icon: string;
}> = ({ label, description, value, onValueChange, disabled = false, icon }) => {
  return (
    <Pressable
      onPress={() => !disabled && onValueChange(!value)}
      style={({ pressed }) => [
        styles.switchRow,
        disabled && styles.switchDisabled,
        pressed && !disabled && { opacity: 0.7 },
      ]}
      disabled={disabled}
    >
      <View style={styles.switchIcon}>
        <Icon
          name={icon as any}
          size={18}
          color={value ? "#6750A4" : "#938F99"}
        />
      </View>
      <View style={styles.switchText}>
        <Text style={[styles.switchLabel, value && styles.switchLabelActive]}>
          {label}
        </Text>
        <Text style={styles.switchDesc}>{description}</Text>
      </View>
      <View style={[styles.switchTrack, value && styles.switchTrackActive]}>
        <View style={[styles.switchThumb, value && styles.switchThumbActive]} />
      </View>
    </Pressable>
  );
};

const PlayModePanel: React.FC<{
  mode: 2 | 5;
  onFilterChange?: (filters: PlayModeFilters) => void;
}> = ({ mode, onFilterChange }) => {
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<PlayModeFilters>({
    parallel: false,
    random: false,
    singleRepeat: false,
    singleRepeatChapter: "",
    singleRepeatNumber: null,
    cardToCardRepeat: false,
    cardToCardChapter: "",
    cardToCardStart: null,
    cardToCardEnd: null,
    backgroundAudio: false,
  });

  const updateFilters = useCallback(
    (updates: Partial<PlayModeFilters>) => {
      const newFilters = { ...filters, ...updates };

      // Mutual Exclusive: Parallel & Random cannot be both ON
      if (updates.parallel === true) {
        newFilters.random = false;
      }
      if (updates.random === true) {
        newFilters.parallel = false;
      }

      // Mutual Exclusive: Single Repeat & Card-to-Card Repeat cannot be both ON
      if (updates.singleRepeat === true) {
        newFilters.cardToCardRepeat = false;
      }
      if (updates.cardToCardRepeat === true) {
        newFilters.singleRepeat = false;
      }

      setFilters(newFilters);
      onFilterChange?.(newFilters);
    },
    [filters, onFilterChange]
  );

  return (
    <View style={styles.panel}>
      {/* Parallel & Random Switches */}
      <View style={styles.switchGroup}>
        <Text style={styles.groupTitle}>ক্রমিক নিয়ন্ত্রণ</Text>
        <SwitchRow
          label="সমান্তরাল"
          description="ক্রমিক স্বাভাবিক নিয়মে আসবে"
          value={filters.parallel}
          onValueChange={(v) => updateFilters({ parallel: v })}
          icon="sort-numeric-ascending"
        />
        <SwitchRow
          label="অসমান্তরাল"
          description="ক্রমিক সংখ্যা রেন্ডম হিসেবে আসবে"
          value={filters.random}
          onValueChange={(v) => updateFilters({ random: v })}
          icon="shuffle-variant"
        />
        <Text style={styles.mutualNote}>
          * দুটি সুইচ একসাথে অন করা যাবে না, তবে একসাথে বন্ধ রাখা যাবে
        </Text>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.divider} />

      {/* Single Card Repeat & Card-to-Card Repeat Switches */}
      <View style={styles.switchGroup}>
        <Text style={styles.groupTitle}>রিপিট নিয়ন্ত্রণ</Text>
        <SwitchRow
          label="একক কার্ড রিপিট"
          description="একটি নির্দিষ্ট কার্ড বারবার"
          value={filters.singleRepeat}
          onValueChange={(v) => updateFilters({ singleRepeat: v })}
          icon="repeat-once"
        />

        {filters.singleRepeat && (
          <View style={styles.repeatInputs}>
            <View style={styles.inputGroup}>
              <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>চ্যাপ্টার নাম</Text>
              <TextInput
                value={filters.singleRepeatChapter}
                onChangeText={(text) =>
                  updateFilters({ singleRepeatChapter: text })
                }
                placeholder="অপরিচিতা"
                style={styles.input}
                placeholderTextColor="#938F99"
              />
            </View>
            <View style={styles.inputGroup}>
              <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>কার্ড নম্বর (যেমন ১৬)</Text>
              <TextInput
                value={filters.singleRepeatNumber?.toString() || ""}
                onChangeText={(text) => {
                  const num = text ? parseInt(text, 10) : null;
                  updateFilters({
                    singleRepeatNumber: isNaN(num as number) ? null : num,
                  });
                }}
                placeholder="১৬"
                keyboardType="number-pad"
                style={styles.input}
                placeholderTextColor="#938F99"
              />
            </View>
          </View>
        )}

        <SwitchRow
          label="কার্ড টু কার্ড রিপিট"
          description="একটি রেঞ্জের কার্ডগুলো রিপিট"
          value={filters.cardToCardRepeat}
          onValueChange={(v) => updateFilters({ cardToCardRepeat: v })}
          icon="repeat-variant"
        />

        {filters.cardToCardRepeat && (
          <View style={styles.repeatInputs}>
            <View style={styles.inputGroup}>
              <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>চ্যাপ্টার নাম</Text>
              <TextInput
                value={filters.cardToCardChapter}
                onChangeText={(text) =>
                  updateFilters({ cardToCardChapter: text })
                }
                placeholder="অপরিচিতা"
                style={styles.input}
                placeholderTextColor="#938F99"
              />
            </View>
            <View style={styles.rangeRow}>
              <View style={[styles.inputGroup, { flex: 1 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>শুরু</Text>
                <TextInput
                  value={filters.cardToCardStart?.toString() || ""}
                  onChangeText={(text) => {
                    const num = text ? parseInt(text, 10) : null;
                    updateFilters({
                      cardToCardStart: isNaN(num as number) ? null : num,
                    });
                  }}
                  placeholder="১"
                  keyboardType="number-pad"
                  style={styles.input}
                  placeholderTextColor="#938F99"
                />
              </View>
              <Text style={styles.rangeSep}>-</Text>
              <View style={[styles.inputGroup, { flex: 1 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>শেষ</Text>
                <TextInput
                  value={filters.cardToCardEnd?.toString() || ""}
                  onChangeText={(text) => {
                    const num = text ? parseInt(text, 10) : null;
                    updateFilters({
                      cardToCardEnd: isNaN(num as number) ? null : num,
                    });
                  }}
                  placeholder="২০"
                  keyboardType="number-pad"
                  style={styles.input}
                  placeholderTextColor="#938F99"
                />
              </View>
            </View>
            <Text style={styles.autoNote}>
              ছোট থেকে বড় বা বড় থেকে ছোট সিস্টেম স্বয়ংক্রিয়ভাবে বুঝে ফিল্টার
              করবে
            </Text>
          </View>
        )}

        <Text style={styles.mutualNote}>
          * দুটি সুইচ একসাথে চালু করা যাবে না, তবে একসাথে বন্ধ রাখা যাবে
        </Text>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.divider} />

      {/* Audio Background Switch - Separate Below Repeats */}
      <View style={styles.switchGroup}>
        <Text style={styles.groupTitle}>ব্যাকগ্রাউন্ড অডিও</Text>
        <SwitchRow
          label="অডিও ব্যাকগ্রাউন্ড"
          description="অ্যাপের বাইরে বা হোম স্ক্রিনেও অটো-প্লে চলবে"
          value={filters.backgroundAudio}
          onValueChange={(v) => updateFilters({ backgroundAudio: v })}
          icon="music-box-outline"
        />
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

export const ControlPanel2: React.FC<ControlPanel2Props> = ({

  title,
  onFilterChange,

          </Text>
        </View>
      </View>

        onFilterChange={(filters) => onFilterChange?.(mode, filters)}
      />
    </View>
  );
};

/**
 * src/components/Settings/ManifestoDownloader.tsx
 * Manifesto JSON Download List
 * Row: Left Serial No | Middle Chapter Name | Right Download/Delete Icon Toggle
 * Vector Icons Only
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  Pressable,
  FlatList,
  StyleSheet,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
} from "react-native";
import Icon from "react-native-vector-icons/MaterialCommunityIcons";
import SQLiteManager from "../../database/SQLiteManager";
import WordTable from "../../database/WordTable";
import ChapterTable from "../../database/ChapterTable";

export interface ManifestoItem {
  manifesto_id: number;
  chapter_id: number;
  chapter_name: string;
  is_downloaded: 0 | 1;
  json_data?: string | null;
}

export interface ManifestoDownloaderProps {
  manifestData: ManifestoItem[]; // Fixed manifest JSON list
  onDownloadComplete?: (chapter_id: number) => void;
  onDeleteComplete?: (chapter_id: number) => void;
}

export const ManifestoDownloader: React.FC<ManifestoDownloaderProps> = ({
  manifestData,
  onDownloadComplete,
  onDeleteComplete,
}) => {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<ManifestoItem[]>(manifestData);
  const [loadingId, setLoadingId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadDownloadStatus();
  }, []);

  const loadDownloadStatus = async () => {
    try {
      const [result] = await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
        "SELECT manifesto_id, is_downloaded FROM ManifestoTable;"
      );
      const statusMap = new Map<number, number>();
      for (let i = 0; i < result.rows.length; i++) {
        const row = result.rows.item(i);
        statusMap.set(row.manifesto_id, row.is_downloaded);
      }

      setItems((prev) =>
        prev.map((item) => ({
          ...item,
          is_downloaded: (statusMap.get(item.manifesto_id) as 0 | 1) || 0,
        }))
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[ManifestoDownloader] Load status failed:", error);
    }
  };

  const handleDownload = useCallback(async (item: ManifestoItem) => {
    setLoadingId(item.manifesto_id);
    try {
      // Simulate JSON data parsing - in real app, item.json_data is the chapter words JSON
      // For demo, we create mock words
      const mockWords = Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, i) => ({
        word_id: item.chapter_id * 1000 + i,
        display_word_id: i + 1,
        chapter_id: item.chapter_id,
        chapter_name: item.chapter_name,
        content: `Content for ${item.chapter_name} word ${i + 1}`,
        view_id: 0,
      }));

      // Bulk upsert to WordTable
      await WordTable.bulkUpsert(mockWords);

      // Upsert to ChapterTable
      await ChapterTable.upsertChapter({
        chapter_id: item.chapter_id,
        chapter_name: item.chapter_name,
        total_words: mockWords.length,
        completed_words: 0,
        unlocked_id: 0,
        target_percentage: 50,
      });

      // Save to ManifestoTable as downloaded
      await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
        `INSERT OR REPLACE INTO ManifestoTable (manifesto_id, chapter_id, chapter_name, is_downloaded, downloaded_at)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, 1, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);`,
        [item.manifesto_id, item.chapter_id, item.chapter_name]
      );

      setItems((prev) =>
        prev.map((p) =>
          p.manifesto_id === item.manifesto_id ? { ...p, is_downloaded: 1 } : p
        )
      );
      onDownloadComplete?.(item.chapter_id);
      console.log(
        `[ManifestoDownloader] Downloaded chapter ${item.chapter_id}`
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(
        `[ManifestoDownloader] Download failed for ${item.chapter_id}:`,
        error
      );
      Alert.alert("ডাউনলোড ব্যর্থ", "আবার চেষ্টা করুন");
    } finally {
      setLoadingId(null);
    }
  }, []);

  const handleDelete = useCallback(async (item: ManifestoItem) => {
    Alert.alert(
      "ডিলিট করুন?",
      `${item.chapter_name} ডিলিট করলে সব প্রোগ্রেস মুছে যাবে`,
      [
        { text: "বাতিল", style: "cancel" },
        {
          text: "ডিলিট",
          style: "destructive",
          onPress: async () => {
            setLoadingId(item.manifesto_id);
            try {
              await WordTable.deleteByChapter(item.chapter_id);
              await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
                "DELETE FROM ManifestoTable WHERE manifesto_id = ?;",
                [item.manifesto_id]
              );
              await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
                "DELETE FROM ChapterTable WHERE chapter_id = ?;",
                [item.chapter_id]
              );

              setItems((prev) =>
                prev.map((p) =>
                  p.manifesto_id === item.manifesto_id
                    ? { ...p, is_downloaded: 0 }
                    : p
                )
              );
              onDeleteComplete?.(item.chapter_id);
              console.log(
                `[ManifestoDownloader] Deleted chapter ${item.chapter_id}`
              );
            } catch (error) {
              console.error("[ManifestoDownloader] Delete failed:", error);
            } finally {
              setLoadingId(null);
            }
          },
        },
      ]
    );
  }, []);

  const renderItem = ({
    item,
    index,
  }: {
    item: ManifestoItem;
    index: number;
  }) => {
    const isDownloaded = item.is_downloaded === 1;
    const isLoading = loadingId === item.manifesto_id;

    return (
      <View style={styles.row}>
        {/* Left: Serial No */}
        <View style={styles.left}>
          <Text style={styles.serial}>{index + 1}</Text>
        </View>

        {/* Middle: Chapter Name */}
        <View style={styles.middle}>
          <Text style={styles.chapterName} numberOfLines={1}>
            {item.chapter_name}
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.chapterId}>Chapter #{item.chapter_id}</Text>
        </View>

        {/* Right: Download/Delete Icon Toggle */}
        <View style={styles.right}>
          {isLoading ? (
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#6750A4" />
          ) : (
            <Pressable
              onPress={() =>
                isDownloaded ? handleDelete(item) : handleDownload(item)
              }
              style={({ pressed }) => [
                styles.iconButton,
                pressed && { opacity: 0.6 },
              ]}
            >
              <Icon
                name={
                  isDownloaded
                    ? "delete-circle-outline"
                    : "download-circle-outline"
                }
                size={24}
                color={isDownloaded ? "#BA1A1A" : "#6750A4"}
              />
            </Pressable>
          )}
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>ম্যানিফেস্টো ডেটা ম্যানেজমেন্ট</Text>
        <Text style={styles.headerSubtitle}>
          ফিক্সড ম্যানিফেস্টো JSON থেকে ডাউনলোড
        </Text>
      </View>

      <FlatList
        data={items}
        renderItem={renderItem}
        keyExtractor={(item) => `manifesto-${item.manifesto_id}`}
        scrollEnabled={false}
        ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={styles.separator} />}
      />
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
    borderRadius: 16,
    padding: 16,
    marginHorizontal: 16,
    marginVertical: 8,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#E7E0EC",
  },
  header: {
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  headerTitle: {
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: "600",
    color: "#1C1B1F",
  },
  headerSubtitle: {
    fontSize: 12,
    color: "#49454F",
    marginTop: 2,
  },
  row: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    paddingVertical: 12,
  },
  left: {
    width: 32,
    height: 32,
    borderRadius: 16,
    backgroundColor: "#E7E0EC",
    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",
    marginRight: 12,
  },
  serial: {
    fontSize: 12,
    fontWeight: "600",
    color: "#49454F",
  },
  middle: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  chapterName: {
    fontSize: 14,
    fontWeight: "500",
    color: "#1C1B1F",
  },
  chapterId: {
    fontSize: 11,
    color: "#79747E",
    marginTop: 2,
  },
  right: {
    width: 40,
    height: 40,
    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",
  },
  iconButton: {
    width: 40,
    height: 40,
    borderRadius: 12,
    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",
  },
  separator: {
    height: 1,
    backgroundColor: "#F3EDF7",
  },
});

export default ManifestoDownloader;

/**
 * src/components/Settings/TTSControlSlider.tsx
 * TTS Control: Speech Rate Slider + Time Gap Input Box
 * No Lag, Smooth Drag, Save Button Clears Box Manually
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import { View, Text, TextInput, Pressable, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import Icon from "react-native-vector-icons/MaterialCommunityIcons";
import { ttsBrainMMKV } from "../../storage/MMKVInstance";
import TTSController from "../../services/TTSEngine/TTSController";
import TTSStateKey from "../../storage/keys/TTSStateKey";

export const TTSControlSlider: React.FC = () => {
  const [speechRate, setSpeechRate] = useState<number>(0.5);
  const [gapInput, setGapInput] = useState<string>("");
  const [currentGap, setCurrentGap] = useState<number>(300);
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const savedRate = TTSStateKey.getSpeechRate();
    const savedGap = TTSStateKey.getTimeGap();
    setSpeechRate(savedRate);
    setCurrentGap(savedGap);
  }, []);

  // Slider Logic - Long line with marker, drag left/right smoothly no lag
  const handleRateChange = useCallback(async (newRate: number) => {
    const clamped = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(1.0, newRate));
    setSpeechRate(clamped);
    TTSStateKey.setSpeechRate(clamped);

    try {
      await TTSController.initialize();
      const Tts = (await import("react-native-tts")).default;
      await Tts.setDefaultRate(clamped);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[TTSControlSlider] Set rate failed:", error);
    }
  }, []);

  // Gap Input Box + Save Button - Box manually clears after save
  const handleGapSave = useCallback(() => {
    const parsed = parseInt(gapInput, 10);
    if (isNaN(parsed) || parsed < 0 || parsed > 5000) {
      return;
    }

    TTSStateKey.setTimeGap(parsed);
    ttsBrainMMKV.set("tts_time_gap", parsed);
    setCurrentGap(parsed);
    setGapInput(""); // Manually clear box as per architecture
    console.log(`[TTSControlSlider] Gap saved: ${parsed}ms`);
  }, [gapInput]);

  // Slider rendered as custom draggable (no external slider lib to avoid lag)
  const SliderTrack = () => {
    const trackWidth = 280; // Fixed track width
    const markerPosition = ((speechRate - 0.1) / 0.9) * trackWidth; // 0.1-1.0 range

    return (
      <View style={styles.sliderContainer}>
        <View style={styles.sliderTrack}>
          <View style={[styles.sliderFill, { width: markerPosition }]} />
          <View style={[styles.sliderMarker, { left: markerPosition - 10 }]} />
        </View>

        {/* Touch areas for drag simulation - 3 buttons for simplicity but smooth */}
        <View style={styles.sliderControls}>
          <Pressable
            onPress={() => handleRateChange(speechRate - 0.05)}
            style={styles.sliderButton}
          >
            <Icon name="minus" size={18} color="#49454F" />
          </Pressable>

          <Text style={styles.rateText}>{speechRate.toFixed(2)}x</Text>

          <Pressable
            onPress={() => handleRateChange(speechRate + 0.05)}
            style={styles.sliderButton}
          >
            <Icon name="plus" size={18} color="#49454F" />
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>টিটিএস কন্ট্রোল</Text>

      {/* First Control: Speech Rate / Pitch Slider */}
      <View style={styles.controlRow}>
        <View style={styles.controlHeader}>
          <Icon name="speedometer" size={20} color="#6750A4" />
          <Text style={styles.controlLabel}>স্পিচ রেট / পিচ</Text>
        </View>
        <Text style={styles.controlDesc}>
          ডান থেকে বামে ড্র্যাগ করুন - কোনো ল্যাগ ছাড়া
        </Text>
        <SliderTrack />
      </View>

      {/* Second Control: Time Gap Input Box */}
      <View style={styles.controlRow}>
        <View style={styles.controlHeader}>
          <Icon name="timer-outline" size={20} color="#6750A4" />
          <Text style={styles.controlLabel}>টাইম গ্যাপ (Delay)</Text>
        </View>
        <Text style={styles.controlDesc}>
          একটি কন্টেন্ট শেষ হওয়ার পর পরবর্তী কন্টেন্টের গ্যাপ
        </Text>

        <View style={styles.gapContainer}>
          <View style={styles.inputContainer}>
            <TextInput
              value={gapInput}
              onChangeText={setGapInput}
              placeholder={`${currentGap}`}
              placeholderTextColor="#938F99"
              keyboardType="number-pad"
              style={styles.input}
            />
            <Text style={styles.inputUnit}>ms</Text>
          </View>

          <Pressable
            onPress={handleGapSave}
            style={({ pressed }) => [
              styles.saveButton,
              pressed && { opacity: 0.7 },
            ]}
          >
            <Icon name="content-save-outline" size={18} color="#FFFFFF" />
            <Text style={styles.saveButtonText}>সেভ</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <Text style={styles.currentValue}>বর্তমান গ্যাপ: {currentGap}ms</Text>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

/**
 * src/components/Settings/ThemeSelector.tsx
 * Theme / Appearance Selection + Language Selection
 * No Switch, Direct Click, 3 Options Visible Side-by-Side
 * Dark, Light, System Default + Bengali, English, System
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { View, Text, Pressable, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import Icon from "react-native-vector-icons/MaterialCommunityIcons";
import { ttsBrainMMKV } from "../../storage/MMKVInstance";

export type ThemeMode = "light" | "dark" | "system";
export type LanguageMode = "bengali" | "english" | "system";

export interface ThemeSelectorProps {
  onThemeChange?: (theme: ThemeMode) => void;
  onLanguageChange?: (lang: LanguageMode) => void;
}

export const ThemeSelector: React.FC<ThemeSelectorProps> = ({
  onThemeChange,
  onLanguageChange,
}) => {
  const [selectedTheme, setSelectedTheme] = useState<ThemeMode>("system");
  const [selectedLang, setSelectedLang] = useState<LanguageMode>("system");

  useEffect(() => {
    const savedTheme = ttsBrainMMKV.getString("app_theme") as ThemeMode;
    const savedLang = ttsBrainMMKV.getString("app_language") as LanguageMode;
    if (savedTheme) {
      setSelectedTheme(savedTheme);
    }
    if (savedLang) {
      setSelectedLang(savedLang);
    }
  }, []);

  const handleThemeSelect = (theme: ThemeMode) => {
    setSelectedTheme(theme);
    ttsBrainMMKV.set("app_theme", theme);
    onThemeChange?.(theme);
  };

  const handleLangSelect = (lang: LanguageMode) => {
    setSelectedLang(lang);
    ttsBrainMMKV.set("app_language", lang);
    onLanguageChange?.(lang);
  };

  const ThemeOption: React.FC<{
    mode: ThemeMode;
    icon: string;
    label: string;
    selected: boolean;
    onPress: () => void;
  }> = ({ icon, label, selected, onPress }) => (
    <Pressable
      onPress={onPress}
      style={({ pressed }) => [
        styles.optionCard,
        selected && styles.optionSelected,
        pressed && { opacity: 0.8 },
      ]}
    >
      <View style={[styles.optionIcon, selected && styles.optionIconSelected]}>
        <Icon
          name={icon as any}
          size={22}
          color={selected ? "#FFFFFF" : "#49454F"}
        />
      </View>
      <Text
        style={[styles.optionLabel, selected && styles.optionLabelSelected]}
      >
        {label}
      </Text>
      {selected && (
        <View style={styles.checkMark}>
          <Icon name="check-circle" size={18} color="#6750A4" />
        </View>
      )}
    </Pressable>
  );

  const LangOption: React.FC<{
    mode: LanguageMode;
    icon: string;
    label: string;
    subLabel: string;
    selected: boolean;
    onPress: () => void;
  }> = ({ icon, label, subLabel, selected, onPress }) => (
    <Pressable
      onPress={onPress}
      style={({ pressed }) => [
        styles.optionCard,
        selected && styles.optionSelected,
        pressed && { opacity: 0.8 },
      ]}
    >
      <View style={[styles.optionIcon, selected && styles.optionIconSelected]}>
        <Icon
          name={icon as any}
          size={20}
          color={selected ? "#FFFFFF" : "#49454F"}
        />
      </View>
      <Text
        style={[styles.optionLabel, selected && styles.optionLabelSelected]}
      >
        {label}
      </Text>
      <Text style={styles.optionSubLabel}>{subLabel}</Text>
      {selected && (
        <View style={styles.checkMark}>
          <Icon name="check-circle" size={18} color="#6750A4" />
        </View>
      )}
    </Pressable>
  );

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {/* Theme Selection - 3 options side by side, no switch, direct click */}
      <View style={styles.section}>
        <View style={styles.sectionHeader}>
          <Icon name="theme-light-dark" size={20} color="#6750A4" />
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>থিম বা অ্যাপিয়ারেন্স</Text>
        </View>
        <Text style={styles.sectionDesc}>
          কার্ডের ভেতরে সরাসরি ক্লিক - কোনো সুইচ ছাড়া
        </Text>

        <View style={styles.optionsRow}>
          <ThemeOption
            mode="light"
            icon="weather-sunny"
            label="Light"
            selected={selectedTheme === "light"}
            onPress={() => handleThemeSelect("light")}
          />
          <ThemeOption
            mode="dark"
            icon="weather-night"
            label="Dark"
            selected={selectedTheme === "dark"}
            onPress={() => handleThemeSelect("dark")}
          />
          <ThemeOption
            mode="system"
            icon="cellphone-cog"
            label="System"
            selected={selectedTheme === "system"}
            onPress={() => handleThemeSelect("system")}
          />
        </View>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.divider} />

      {/* Language Selection - 3 options */}
      <View style={styles.section}>
        <View style={styles.sectionHeader}>
          <Icon name="translate" size={20} color="#6750A4" />
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>ভাষা পরিবর্তন</Text>
        </View>
        <Text style={styles.sectionDesc}>
          বাংলা / ইংরেজি সিলেক্ট - পাশে সিলেক্টেড মার্ক
        </Text>

        <View style={styles.optionsRow}>
          <LangOption
            mode="bengali"

            label="Bengali"
            subLabel="বাংলা"
            selected={selectedLang === "bengali"}
            onPress={() => handleLangSelect("bengali")}
          />
          <LangOption

/**
 * src/database/ChapterTable.ts
 * Chapter Unlock Logic with Two Guards: 50% Default Guard & 80% Executive Manager
 * Handles: Dynamic Target from 30-Day Algo, Bank Credit Logic, unlocked_id
 */

import SQLiteManager from "./SQLiteManager";
import WordTable from "./WordTable";

export interface ChapterRow {
  chapter_id: number;
  chapter_name: string;
  total_words: number;
  completed_words: number;
  unlocked_id: number; // 0=Locked, 1=Unlocked
  target_percentage: number; // 50-100 from 30-Day Dynamic Engine
  created_at?: string;
}

export interface ChapterUnlockStatus {
  chapter_id: number;
  chapter_name: string;
  total: number;
  completed: number;
  completedPercent: number; // completed/total*100
  targetPercent: number; // From dynamic engine (50-100)
  is50GuardPassed: boolean; // Default Guard
  is80ManagerPassed: boolean; // Executive Manager (or targetPercent)
  isUnlocked: boolean; // unlocked_id=1
  needsBankCredit: number; // How much bank needed to reach target
  canUseBank: boolean; // is50GuardPassed && bankBalance >= needsBankCredit
}

class ChapterTableManager {
  /**
   * Upsert Chapter (On Manifesto Download)
   */
  async upsertChapter(
    chapter: Partial<ChapterRow> & { chapter_id: number; chapter_name: string }
  ): Promise<void> {
    const query = `
      INSERT OR REPLACE INTO ChapterTable
      (chapter_id, chapter_name, total_words, completed_words, unlocked_id, target_percentage)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?);
    `;
    const total = chapter.total_words || 0;
    await SQLiteManager.executeSql(query, [
      chapter.chapter_id,
      chapter.chapter_name,
      total,
      chapter.completed_words || 0,
      chapter.unlocked_id || 0,
      chapter.target_percentage || 50,
    ]);
  }

  /**
   * Update total_words after bulk word import
   */
  async syncTotalWords(chapter_id: number): Promise<void> {
    const total = await WordTable.getTotalCount(chapter_id);
    await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
      "UPDATE ChapterTable SET total_words = ? WHERE chapter_id = ?;",
      [total, chapter_id]
    );
  }

  /**
   * Set Dynamic Target from 30-Day Algorithm (50-100%)
   * Called by generateComplexDynamicTarget()
   */
  async setDynamicTarget(
    chapter_id: number,
    targetPercentage: number
  ): Promise<void> {
    const safeTarget = Math.max(
      50,
      Math.min(100, Math.round(targetPercentage))
    );
    await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
      "UPDATE ChapterTable SET target_percentage = ? WHERE chapter_id = ?;",
      [safeTarget, chapter_id]
    );
    console.log(
      `[ChapterTable] Chapter ${chapter_id} target set to ${safeTarget}%`
    );
  }

  /**
   * Get Chapter Row
   */
  async getChapter(chapter_id: number): Promise<ChapterRow | null> {
    const [result] = await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
      "SELECT * FROM ChapterTable WHERE chapter_id = ?;",
      [chapter_id]
    );
    if (result.rows.length > 0) {
      return result.rows.item(0) as ChapterRow;
    }
    return null;
  }

  /**
   * Core: Check Two Guards Logic (50% Guard & 80% Manager)
   * This is the Heart of Invisible UI Magic Trigger
   */
  async checkChapterUnlockStatus(
    chapter_id: number,
    bankBalancePercent: number = 0
  ): Promise<ChapterUnlockStatus> {
    const chapter = await this.getChapter(chapter_id);
    const progress = await WordTable.getChapterProgress(chapter_id);

    if (!chapter) {
      // If chapter not in ChapterTable, create temp status from WordTable
      return {
        chapter_id,
        chapter_name: `Chapter ${chapter_id}`,
        total: progress.total,
        completed: progress.completed,
        completedPercent: progress.percentage,
        targetPercent: 80, // Default as per UI: "80% প্রয়োজন"
        is50GuardPassed: progress.percentage >= 50,
        is80ManagerPassed: progress.percentage >= 80,
        isUnlocked: false,
        needsBankCredit: Math.max(0, 80 - progress.percentage),
        canUseBank:
          progress.percentage >= 50 &&
          bankBalancePercent >= Math.max(0, 80 - progress.percentage),
      };
    }

    const completedPercent = progress.percentage;
    const targetPercent = chapter.target_percentage || 80;

    // Two Guards
    const is50GuardPassed = completedPercent >= 50;
    const is80ManagerPassed = completedPercent >= targetPercent;

    // Bank Logic: User's own + Bank Credit
    const needsBankCredit = Math.max(0, targetPercent - completedPercent);
    const canUseBank = is50GuardPassed && bankBalancePercent >= needsBankCredit;

    // Final Unlock Check: Either natural 80% OR 50%+Bank = 80%
    const isUnlockedByProgress = is80ManagerPassed;
    const isUnlockedByBank =
      is50GuardPassed &&
      completedPercent + Math.min(bankBalancePercent, needsBankCredit) >=
        targetPercent;
    const isUnlocked =
      chapter.unlocked_id === 1 || isUnlockedByProgress || isUnlockedByBank;

    // Sync completed_words
    if (chapter.completed_words !== progress.completed) {
      await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
        "UPDATE ChapterTable SET completed_words = ? WHERE chapter_id = ?;",
        [progress.completed, chapter_id]
      );
    }

    return {
      chapter_id,
      chapter_name: chapter.chapter_name,
      total: progress.total,
      completed: progress.completed,
      completedPercent,
      targetPercent,
      is50GuardPassed,
      is80ManagerPassed,
      isUnlocked,
      needsBankCredit,
      canUseBank,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Unlock Chapter (view_id=1 for Chapter) - Called when 80% condition met
   * From Architecture: Chapter Executive says "চমৎকার! ৮০% পূরণ হয়েছে"
   */
  async unlockChapter(chapter_id: number): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
        "UPDATE ChapterTable SET unlocked_id = 1 WHERE chapter_id = ?;",
        [chapter_id]
      );
      console.log(
        `[ChapterTable] Chapter ${chapter_id} UNLOCKED (unlocked_id=1)`
      );
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[ChapterTable] Unlock failed for ${chapter_id}:`, error);
      return false;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Unlock via Bank Credit (Manual Click on -30% Button)
   * User Clicks [ -30% ব্যাংক ক্রেডিট কেটে আনলক করুন ]
   */
  async unlockViaBank(
    chapter_id: number,
    bankCreditToDeduct: number
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; remainingNeeds: number }> {
    const status = await this.checkChapterUnlockStatus(
      chapter_id,
      bankCreditToDeduct
    );

    if (!status.is50GuardPassed) {
      console.log("[ChapterTable] Bank unlock denied: 50% Guard not passed");
      return { success: false, remainingNeeds: status.needsBankCredit };
    }

    if (!status.canUseBank) {
      console.log(
        "[ChapterTable] Bank unlock denied: Insufficient bank balance"
      );
      return { success: false, remainingNeeds: status.needsBankCredit };
    }

    // Deduct and unlock
    const unlocked = await this.unlockChapter(chapter_id);
    return {
      success: unlocked,
      remainingNeeds: 0,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Get All Chapters with Progress (For Home Screen & Drawer)
   */
  async getAllChaptersWithProgress(): Promise<ChapterUnlockStatus[]> {
    const [result] = await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
      "SELECT * FROM ChapterTable ORDER BY chapter_id ASC;"
    );
    const statuses: ChapterUnlockStatus[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < result.rows.length; i++) {
      const row = result.rows.item(i) as ChapterRow;
      const status = await this.checkChapterUnlockStatus(row.chapter_id, 0);
      statuses.push(status);
    }

    return statuses;
  }

  /**
   * Reset Chapter (For Testing)
   */
  async resetChapter(chapter_id: number): Promise<void> {
    await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
      "UPDATE ChapterTable SET unlocked_id = 0, completed_words = 0 WHERE chapter_id = ?;",
      [chapter_id]
    );
    // Also reset all words view_id=0
    await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
      "UPDATE WordTable SET view_id = 0 WHERE chapter_id = ?;",
      [chapter_id]
    );
  }
}

export const ChapterTable = new ChapterTableManager();
export default ChapterTable;

FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/database/SQLiteManager.ts
=====================================
/**
 * src/database/SQLiteManager.ts
 * Offline-First SQLite Core Manager
 * Handles: DB Init, Transaction Wrapper, Safe Query Execution
 * Library: react-native-sqlite-storage
 */

import SQLite from "react-native-sqlite-storage";
type SQLiteDatabase = any;
type Transaction = any;
type ResultSet = any;

// Enable Promise
SQLite.enablePromise(true);

const DATABASE_NAME = "myapp_offline.db";
const DATABASE_VERSION = "1.0";
const DATABASE_DISPLAY_NAME = "MyApp Offline DB";
const DATABASE_SIZE = 200000; // 200KB initial

class SQLiteManagerSingleton {
  private static instance: SQLiteManagerSingleton;
  private db: SQLiteDatabase | null = null;
  private isInitialized = false;
  private initPromise: Promise<SQLiteDatabase> | null = null;

  private constructor() {}

  static getInstance(): SQLiteManagerSingleton {
    if (!SQLiteManagerSingleton.instance) {
      SQLiteManagerSingleton.instance = new SQLiteManagerSingleton();
    }
    return SQLiteManagerSingleton.instance;
  }

  /**
   * Initialize DB (Offline-First)
   * Called once at App Launch
   */
  async init(): Promise<SQLiteDatabase> {
    if (this.isInitialized && this.db) {
      return this.db;
    }

    if (this.initPromise) {
      return this.initPromise;
    }

    this.initPromise = new Promise(async (resolve, reject) => {
      try {
        console.log("[SQLiteManager] Initializing DB...");
        const database = await SQLite.openDatabase({
          name: DATABASE_NAME,
          version: DATABASE_VERSION,
          displayName: DATABASE_DISPLAY_NAME,
          size: DATABASE_SIZE,
          createFromLocation: "~myapp_offline.db", // Pre-populated if needed
        });

        this.db = database;
        await this.createTables(database);
        await this.enableForeignKeys(database);

        this.isInitialized = true;
        console.log("[SQLiteManager] DB Initialized Successfully");
        resolve(database);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("[SQLiteManager] Init Failed:", error);
        reject(error);
      } finally {
        this.initPromise = null;
      }
    });

    return this.initPromise;
  }

  private async enableForeignKeys(db: SQLiteDatabase): Promise<void> {
    await db.executeSql("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;");
    await db.executeSql("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;"); // Better concurrency
  }

  /**
   * Create Tables - WordTable & ChapterTable + DailyBank + Settings
   */
  private async createTables(db: SQLiteDatabase): Promise<void> {
    const queries = [
      // WordTable - Core Content
      `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS WordTable (
        word_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
        display_word_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
        chapter_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
        chapter_name TEXT NOT NULL,
        content TEXT,
        view_id INTEGER DEFAULT 0, -- 0=Locked/Incomplete, 1=Unlocked/Complete (5 Modes Done)
        created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
      );`,

      // ChapterTable - Chapter Unlock Logic (80% Manager)
      `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ChapterTable (
        chapter_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
        chapter_name TEXT NOT NULL,
        total_words INTEGER NOT NULL,
        completed_words INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        unlocked_id INTEGER DEFAULT 0, -- 0=Locked, 1=Unlocked (80% + Bank)
        target_percentage INTEGER DEFAULT 50, -- Dynamic from 30-Day Algo (50-100)
        created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
      );`,

      // DailyBank - Credit Wallet
      `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS DailyBank (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        total_balance INTEGER DEFAULT 0, -- e.g., 490%
        today_earned INTEGER DEFAULT 0, -- e.g., +20%
        last_updated DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
      );`,

      // Manifesto Download List
      `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ManifestoTable (
        manifesto_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
        chapter_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
        chapter_name TEXT NOT NULL,
        is_downloaded INTEGER DEFAULT 0, -- 0=Download Icon, 1=Delete Icon
        json_data TEXT,
        downloaded_at TIMESTAMP
      );`,

      // Indexes for Performance (Batch Query Optimization)
      "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_word_chapter ON WordTable(chapter_id);",
      "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_word_view ON WordTable(view_id);",
      "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_word_display ON WordTable(display_word_id);",
    ];

    for (const query of queries) {
      await db.executeSql(query);
    }

    console.log("[SQLiteManager] Tables Created / Verified");
  }

  /**
   * Get DB Instance
   */
  async getDB(): Promise<SQLiteDatabase> {
    if (this.db && this.isInitialized) {
      return this.db;
    }
    return this.init();
  }

  /**
   * Safe Execute SQL with Error Handling
   */
  async executeSql(query: string, params: any[] = []): Promise<[ResultSet]> {
    const db = await this.getDB();
    try {
      const result = await db.executeSql(query, params);
      return result as unknown as [ResultSet];
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[SQLiteManager] Query Failed: ${query}`, error);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Transaction Wrapper - For Batch Operations
   */
  async transaction(
    callback: (tx: Transaction) => Promise<void> | void
  ): Promise<void> {
    const db = await this.getDB();
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      db.transaction(
        async (tx: any) => {
          try {
            await callback(tx);
          } catch (error) {
            console.error("[SQLiteManager] Transaction Callback Error:", error);
            throw error;
          }
        },
        (error: any) => {
          console.error("[SQLiteManager] Transaction Failed:", error);
          reject(error);
        },
        () => {
          resolve();
        }
      );
    });
  }

  /**
   * Close DB (On App Exit)
   */
  async close(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.db) {
      await this.db.close();
      this.db = null;
      this.isInitialized = false;
      console.log("[SQLiteManager] DB Closed");
    }
  }

  /**
   * For Debugging - Clear All
   */
  async clearAll(): Promise<void> {
    const db = await this.getDB();
    await db.executeSql("DELETE FROM WordTable;");
    await db.executeSql("DELETE FROM ChapterTable;");
    await db.executeSql("DELETE FROM DailyBank;");
    console.log("[SQLiteManager] All Tables Cleared");
  }
}

export const SQLiteManager = SQLiteManagerSingleton.getInstance();
export default SQLiteManager;

/**
 * src/database/WordTable.ts
 * Core Content Table - view_id = 1 when all 5 modes completed
 * Offline-First, Primary Key is word_id (Internal), display_word_id is User Visible
 */

import SQLiteManager from "./SQLiteManager";

export interface WordRow {
  word_id: number; // Internal Primary Key (e.g., 67, 896, 156)
  display_word_id: number; // User Visible # (e.g., 35, 78, 15)
  chapter_id: number;
  chapter_name: string; // e.g., "অপরিচিতা"
  content: string | null;
  view_id: number; // 0=Locked/Incomplete, 1=Unlocked/Complete (5 Modes >0)
  created_at?: string;
  updated_at?: string;
}

class WordTableManager {
  /**
   * Insert or Replace Word (For Manifesto Download)
   */
  async upsertWord(word: WordRow): Promise<void> {
    const query = `
      INSERT OR REPLACE INTO WordTable 
      (word_id, display_word_id, chapter_id, chapter_name, content, view_id, updated_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
    `;
    await SQLiteManager.executeSql(query, [
      word.word_id,
      word.display_word_id,
      word.chapter_id,
      word.chapter_name,
      word.content || null,
      word.view_id || 0,
    ]);
  }

  /**
   * Bulk Upsert - For Manifesto JSON Import
   */
  async bulkUpsert(words: WordRow[]): Promise<void> {
    if (words.length === 0) {
      return;
    }

    await SQLiteManager.transaction(async (tx) => {
      for (const word of words) {
        const query = `
          INSERT OR REPLACE INTO WordTable 
          (word_id, display_word_id, chapter_id, chapter_name, content, view_id, updated_at)
          VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
        `;
        tx.executeSql(query, [
          word.word_id,
          word.display_word_id,
          word.chapter_id,
          word.chapter_name,
          word.content || null,
          word.view_id || 0,
        ]);
      }
    });
    console.log(`[WordTable] Bulk Upsert ${words.length} words`);
  }

  /**
   * Get Word by word_id (Primary Key)
   */
  async getWordById(word_id: number): Promise<WordRow | null> {
    const [result] = await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
      "SELECT * FROM WordTable WHERE word_id = ?;",
      [word_id]
    );
    if (result.rows.length > 0) {
      return result.rows.item(0) as WordRow;
    }
    return null;
  }

  /**
   * Get All Words in Chapter
   */
  async getWordsByChapter(chapter_id: number): Promise<WordRow[]> {
    const [result] = await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
      "SELECT * FROM WordTable WHERE chapter_id = ? ORDER BY display_word_id ASC;",
      [chapter_id]
    );
    const words: WordRow[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < result.rows.length; i++) {
      words.push(result.rows.item(i) as WordRow);
    }
    return words;
  }

  /**
   * Get view_id = 1 Count for a Chapter (For 50% Guard & 80% Manager)
   */
  async getCompletedCount(chapter_id: number): Promise<number> {
    const [result] = await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
      "SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM WordTable WHERE chapter_id = ? AND view_id = 1;",
      [chapter_id]
    );
    return result.rows.item(0).count || 0;
  }

  /**
   * Get Total Words Count for Chapter
   */
  async getTotalCount(chapter_id: number): Promise<number> {
    const [result] = await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
      "SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM WordTable WHERE chapter_id = ?;",
      [chapter_id]
    );
    return result.rows.item(0).count || 0;
  }

  /**
   * Update view_id to 1 (Core Unlock Logic from checkWordCompletion)
   * Called when m1>0 && m2>0 && m3>0 && m4>0 && m5>0
   */
  async markAsCompleted(word_id: number): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      // First check if already completed to avoid unnecessary write
      const existing = await this.getWordById(word_id);
      if (existing && existing.view_id === 1) {
        return false; // Already completed
      }

      await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
        "UPDATE WordTable SET view_id = 1, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE word_id = ?;",
        [word_id]
      );
      console.log(`[WordTable] Word ${word_id} marked view_id=1`);
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[WordTable] Failed to mark ${word_id}:`, error);
      return false;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Reset view_id to 0 (For Re-study)
   */
  async resetWord(word_id: number): Promise<void> {
    await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
      "UPDATE WordTable SET view_id = 0, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE word_id = ?;",
      [word_id]
    );
  }

  /**
   * Get Chapter Progress Percentage (For Drawer)
   * Returns: { completed, total, percentage }
   */
  async getChapterProgress(
    chapter_id: number
  ): Promise<{ completed: number; total: number; percentage: number }> {
    const [result] = await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
      `SELECT 
        COUNT(*) as total,
        SUM(CASE WHEN view_id = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as completed
       FROM WordTable WHERE chapter_id = ?;`,
      [chapter_id]
    );
    const row = result.rows.item(0);
    const total = row.total || 0;
    const completed = row.completed || 0;
    const percentage = total > 0 ? Math.round((completed / total) * 100) : 0;

    return { completed, total, percentage };
  }

  /**
   * Delete words of a chapter (When Manifesto Deleted)
   */
  async deleteByChapter(chapter_id: number): Promise<void> {
    await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
      "DELETE FROM WordTable WHERE chapter_id = ?;",
      [chapter_id]
    );
  }
}

export const WordTable = new WordTableManager();
export default WordTable;

FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/database/queries/BatchFetch.ts
