/**
 * src/database/queries/BatchFetch.ts
 * High-Performance Batch Query for Mapping Pipeline
 * MMKV Primary Keys (word_id) -> SQLite Display Data (display_word_id, chapter_name)
 * Uses IN clause optimization, No Loop Query
 */

import SQLiteManager from "../SQLiteManager";
import { WordRow } from "../WordTable";

export interface EnrichedDisplayData {
  wordId: number; // Internal Primary Key (67, 896)
  displayWordId: number; // User Visible (15, 42, #৩৫)
  chapterId: number;
  chapterName: string; // অপরিচিতা
  viewId: number;
  content?: string | null;
}

export interface BatchFetchResult {
  enriched: EnrichedDisplayData[];
  notFound: number[]; // word_ids that were not found in DB
  chapterGrouped: Record<string, EnrichedDisplayData[]>; // Grouped by chapter_name for Event Card
}

/**
 * Core Mapping Pipeline Function
 * @param primaryKeyArray - MMKV Raw Material e.g., [67, 896, 765, 985, 096]
 * @returns Enriched data for Event Card rendering
 */
export async function fetchDisplayDataFromPrimaryKeys(
  primaryKeyArray: number[]
): Promise<BatchFetchResult> {
  if (!primaryKeyArray || primaryKeyArray.length === 0) {
    return { enriched: [], notFound: [], chapterGrouped: {} };
  }

  // Deduplicate and sanitize
  const uniqueIds = Array.from(
    new Set(primaryKeyArray.filter((id) => Number.isFinite(id) && id > 0))
  );

  if (uniqueIds.length === 0) {
    return { enriched: [], notFound: [], chapterGrouped: {} };
  }

  // Chunking for SQLite IN clause limit (SQLite max ~999 variables, we use 500 for safety)
  const CHUNK_SIZE = 500;
  const chunks: number[][] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < uniqueIds.length; i += CHUNK_SIZE) {
    chunks.push(uniqueIds.slice(i, i + CHUNK_SIZE));
  }

  const allEnriched: EnrichedDisplayData[] = [];

  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    // Build placeholders: ?,?,?
    const placeholders = chunk.map(() => "?").join(",");
    const query = `
      SELECT word_id, display_word_id, chapter_id, chapter_name, view_id, content
      FROM WordTable
      WHERE word_id IN (${placeholders})
      ORDER BY chapter_name ASC, display_word_id ASC;
    `;

    try {
      const [result] = await SQLiteManager.executeSql(query, chunk);
      for (let i = 0; i < result.rows.length; i++) {
        const row = result.rows.item(i) as WordRow;
        allEnriched.push({
          wordId: row.word_id,
          displayWordId: row.display_word_id,
          chapterId: row.chapter_id,
          chapterName: row.chapter_name,
          viewId: row.view_id,
          content: row.content,
        });
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(
        `[BatchFetch] Chunk failed for IDs ${chunk.slice(0, 3)}...:`,
        error
      );
      // Continue with other chunks
    }
  }

  // Find not found IDs
  const foundIds = new Set(allEnriched.map((e) => e.wordId));
  const notFound = uniqueIds.filter((id) => !foundIds.has(id));

  // Group by chapter_name for Event Card (e.g., অপরিচিতা #৩৫, #৭৮)
  const chapterGrouped: Record<string, EnrichedDisplayData[]> = {};
  for (const item of allEnriched) {
    if (!chapterGrouped[item.chapterName]) {
      chapterGrouped[item.chapterName] = [];
    }
    chapterGrouped[item.chapterName].push(item);
  }

  console.log(
    `[BatchFetch] Mapped ${uniqueIds.length} PKs -> ${allEnriched.length} enriched, ${notFound.length} not found`
  );

  return {
    enriched: allEnriched,
    notFound,
    chapterGrouped,
  };
}

/**
 * Helper: Convert Enriched to Event Card Display String
 * e.g., "অপরিচিতা #৩৫, #৭৮, #৮৮"
 */
export function toEventCardDisplay(enriched: EnrichedDisplayData[]): string {
  if (enriched.length === 0) {
    return "";
  }

  // Group by chapter (usually same chapter)
  const grouped = enriched.reduce((acc, cur) => {
    if (!acc[cur.chapterName]) {
      acc[cur.chapterName] = [];
    }
    acc[cur.chapterName].push(cur.displayWordId);
    return acc;
  }, {} as Record<string, number[]>);

  // Format: "অপরিচিতা #৩৫, #৭৮, #৮৮, #৮৯"
  return Object.entries(grouped)
    .map(([chapterName, ids]) => {
      const idStr = ids.map((id) => `#${id}`).join(", ");
      return `${chapterName} ${idStr}`;
    })
    .join(" | ");
}

/**
 * Specialized Fetch for 4-Stage Weakness Engine
 * Input: { m1: [67,896], m2: [65,86] ... }
 * Output: { m1: EnrichedDisplayData[], ... }
 */
export async function fetchWeaknessStages(
  stagesRaw: Record<string, number[]> // e.g., { m1: [67,896], m2: [65,86] }
): Promise<Record<string, EnrichedDisplayData[]>> {
  const result: Record<string, EnrichedDisplayData[]> = {};

  // Process in parallel for speed
  const entries = Object.entries(stagesRaw);
  const promises = entries.map(async ([mode, ids]) => {
    const { enriched } = await fetchDisplayDataFromPrimaryKeys(ids);
    return { mode, enriched };
  });

  const resolved = await Promise.all(promises);
  for (const { mode, enriched } of resolved) {
    result[mode] = enriched;
  }

  return result;
}

export default fetchDisplayDataFromPrimaryKeys;

/**
 * src/database/queries/UnlockQuery.ts
 * Core Unlock Logic: Per-Word (view_id=1) and Chapter (unlocked_id=1)
 * Implements: MMKV 5 Modes >0 Check, Batch Update, Zero Unnecessary Writes
 */

import SQLiteManager from "../SQLiteManager";
import WordTable from "../WordTable";
import ChapterTable from "../ChapterTable";
import { MMKV } from "react-native-mmkv";

// MMKV Instance for Progress Check
const progressMMKV = new MMKV({
  id: "word-progress-mmkv",
});

export interface WordCompletionCheck {
  word_id: number;
  m1: number;
  m2: number;
  m3: number;
  m4: number;
  m5: number;
  isCompleted: boolean; // All >0
}

/**
 * Check if a single word has completed all 5 modes
 * Logic from Architecture: word_156_mode_1 >0 && ... && mode_5>0 => view_id=1
 */
export function checkWordCompletionFromMMKV(
  word_id: number
): WordCompletionCheck {
  const m1 = progressMMKV.getNumber(`word_${word_id}_mode_1`) || 0;
  const m2 = progressMMKV.getNumber(`word_${word_id}_mode_2`) || 0;
  const m3 = progressMMKV.getNumber(`word_${word_id}_mode_3`) || 0;
  const m4 = progressMMKV.getNumber(`word_${word_id}_mode_4`) || 0;
  const m5 = progressMMKV.getNumber(`word_${word_id}_mode_5`) || 0;

  const isCompleted = m1 > 0 && m2 > 0 && m3 > 0 && m4 > 0 && m5 > 0;

  return {
    word_id,
    m1,
    m2,
    m3,
    m4,
    m5,
    isCompleted,
  };
}

/**
 * Background Trigger - Called on every mode click
 * Architecture: checkWordCompletionAndTrigger(wordId)
 */
export async function checkWordCompletionAndTrigger(
  wordId: number
): Promise<boolean> {
  const check = checkWordCompletionFromMMKV(wordId);

  if (!check.isCompleted) {
    return false;
  }

  // Condition met! Call SQLite update
  const updated = await updateDatabaseViewId(wordId, 1);

  if (updated) {
    console.log(
      `[UnlockQuery] Word ${wordId} 5 modes complete! SQLite view_id=1`
    );
    // Also trigger chapter progress re-check
    await onWordCompletedTriggerChapterCheck(wordId);
  }

  return updated;
}

/**
 * Direct DB Update: UPDATE WordTable SET view_id = 1 WHERE word_id = ?
 * Safe with existence check to avoid overload
 */
export async function updateDatabaseViewId(
  wordId: number,
  viewId: 0 | 1
): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    // Prevent unnecessary write if already 1
    if (viewId === 1) {
      const [existing] = await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
        "SELECT view_id FROM WordTable WHERE word_id = ?;",
        [wordId]
      );
      if (existing.rows.length > 0 && existing.rows.item(0).view_id === 1) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
      "UPDATE WordTable SET view_id = ?, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE word_id = ?;",
      [viewId, wordId]
    );

    return true;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      `[UnlockQuery] updateDatabaseViewId failed for ${wordId}:`,
      error
    );
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * After word completes, check chapter guards (50% & 80%)
 * Emits event for UI Magic Trigger
 */
async function onWordCompletedTriggerChapterCheck(
  wordId: number
): Promise<void> {
  try {
    // Get chapter_id from word
    const [result] = await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
      "SELECT chapter_id FROM WordTable WHERE word_id = ?;",
      [wordId]
    );
    if (result.rows.length === 0) {
      return;
    }

    const chapter_id = result.rows.item(0).chapter_id as number;

    // Check guards - this will also sync completed_words
    const status = await ChapterTable.checkChapterUnlockStatus(chapter_id, 0);

    // If natural 80% reached, auto unlock chapter
    if (status.is80ManagerPassed && !status.isUnlocked) {
      await ChapterTable.unlockChapter(chapter_id);
      console.log(
        `[UnlockQuery] Chapter ${chapter_id} auto-unlocked via 80% Manager`
      );
    }

    // If 50% guard passed, emit signal for Invisible UI Magic
    if (status.is50GuardPassed) {
      // This signal is listened by Chapter Card to show [-30% Bank Credit] button
      // Using MMKV as event bus (fast)
      const eventMMKV = new MMKV({ id: "chapter-events" });
      eventMMKV.set(`chapter_${chapter_id}_50_guard_passed`, true);
      eventMMKV.set(`chapter_${chapter_id}_guard_event_at`, Date.now());
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "[UnlockQuery] onWordCompletedTriggerChapterCheck failed:",
      error
    );
  }
}

/**
 * Batch Check for Daily Event Completion
 * When user finishes event cards, check all
 */
export async function batchCheckWordCompletion(
  wordIds: number[]
): Promise<{ newlyCompleted: number[]; alreadyCompleted: number[] }> {
  const newlyCompleted: number[] = [];
  const alreadyCompleted: number[] = [];

  for (const wordId of wordIds) {
    const check = checkWordCompletionFromMMKV(wordId);
    if (!check.isCompleted) {
      continue;
    }

    const [existing] = await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
      "SELECT view_id FROM WordTable WHERE word_id = ?;",
      [wordId]
    );
    const viewId = existing.rows.length > 0 ? existing.rows.item(0).view_id : 0;

    if (viewId === 1) {
      alreadyCompleted.push(wordId);
    } else {
      const updated = await updateDatabaseViewId(wordId, 1);
      if (updated) {
        newlyCompleted.push(wordId);
        await onWordCompletedTriggerChapterCheck(wordId);
      }
    }
  }

  return { newlyCompleted, alreadyCompleted };
}

/**
 * Chapter Unlock via Bank Credit (Manual -30% Button Click)
 * Implements: 50% + 30% Bank = 80% Unlock
 */
export async function unlockChapterViaBank(
  chapterId: number,
  bankBalance: number,
  creditToDeduct: number
): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string; newBankBalance?: number }> {
  const status = await ChapterTable.checkChapterUnlockStatus(
    chapterId,
    bankBalance
  );

  if (!status.is50GuardPassed) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: `50% Guard not passed. Completed: ${status.completedPercent}%, Need 50%`,
    };
  }

  if (status.isUnlocked) {
    return { success: true, message: "Chapter already unlocked" };
  }

  if (bankBalance < status.needsBankCredit) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: `Insufficient bank. Need ${status.needsBankCredit}%, You have ${bankBalance}%`,
    };
  }

  // Deduct from DailyBank Table
  await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
    "UPDATE DailyBank SET total_balance = total_balance - ? WHERE id = (SELECT id FROM DailyBank ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1);",
    [creditToDeduct]
  );

  const unlocked = await ChapterTable.unlockChapter(chapterId);

  if (unlocked) {
    // Also update MMKV Bank (for Drawer instant show)
    const bankMMKV = new MMKV({ id: "daily-bank" });
    const current = bankMMKV.getNumber("total_balance") || 0;
    bankMMKV.set("total_balance", Math.max(0, current - creditToDeduct));

    return {
      success: true,
      message: `Chapter unlocked! ${creditToDeduct}% deducted. ${
        status.completedPercent
      }% + ${creditToDeduct}% = ${status.completedPercent + creditToDeduct}%`,
      newBankBalance: bankBalance - creditToDeduct,
    };
  }

  return { success: false, message: "Unlock failed due to DB error" };
}

export default {
  checkWordCompletionFromMMKV,
  checkWordCompletionAndTrigger,
  updateDatabaseViewId,
  batchCheckWordCompletion,
  unlockChapterViaBank,
};

FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/hooks/useProgress.ts

/**
 * Src/hooks/useProgress.ts & src/hooks/useProgress.ts
 * Progress Hook - Single Loop 20*5=100 Calculator + Chapter Box + Guards
 * Provides instant MMKV read + auto recalculation
 */

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo } from "react";
import WordProgressKey from "../storage/keys/WordProgressKey";
import ChapterEncapsulation from "../services/ProgressEngine/ChapterEncapsulation";
import PerModeCalculator, {
  PerModeResult,
} from "../services/ProgressEngine/PerModeCalculator";
import ChapterTable from "../database/ChapterTable";
import ChapterExecutive, {
  ExecutiveResult,
} from "../services/ProgressEngine/Guards/ChapterExecutive";
import { WordModeProgress } from "../types/progress.types";

export interface UseProgressOptions {
  chapterId: number;
  wordIds: number[];
  autoRefresh?: boolean;
}

export interface UseProgressReturn {
  perModeResult: PerModeResult;
  chapterBox: ReturnType<typeof ChapterEncapsulation.getChapterBox>;
  executiveResult: ExecutiveResult | null;
  wordProgresses: WordModeProgress[];
  totalProgress: number;
  refresh: () => Promise<void>;
  isLoading: boolean;
}

export const useProgress = ({
  chapterId,
  wordIds,
  autoRefresh = true,
}: UseProgressOptions): UseProgressReturn => {
  const [perModeResult, setPerModeResult] = useState<PerModeResult>({
    mode1: 0,
    mode2: 0,
    mode3: 0,
    mode4: 0,
    mode5: 0,
    totalCompletedWords: 0,
    totalWords: wordIds.length,
    totalProgressPercent: 0,
    perModeCompleted: { m1Done: 0, m2Done: 0, m3Done: 0, m4Done: 0, m5Done: 0 },
  });
  const [chapterBox, setChapterBox] = useState(() =>
    ChapterEncapsulation.getChapterBox(chapterId)
  );
  const [executiveResult, setExecutiveResult] =
    useState<ExecutiveResult | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);

  const wordProgresses = useMemo(() => {
    return WordProgressKey.getBulkProgress(wordIds);
  }, [wordIds, perModeResult]);

  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      // Single Loop Calculation
      const result = PerModeCalculator.calculateAllModesProgress(wordIds);
      setPerModeResult(result);

      // Save to Chapter Box (Encapsulation)
      const box = ChapterEncapsulation.saveChapterProgress(chapterId, wordIds);
      setChapterBox(box);

      // Check Executive (80% Manager)
      const exec = await ChapterExecutive.check(chapterId);
      setExecutiveResult(exec);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[useProgress] Refresh failed:", error);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [chapterId, wordIds]);

  useEffect(() => {
    refresh();
  }, [refresh]);

  // Auto refresh on focus if enabled
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!autoRefresh) {
      return;
    }
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      refresh();
    }, 2000); // Poll every 2s for live tracking
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [autoRefresh, refresh]);

  return {
    perModeResult,
    chapterBox,
    executiveResult,
    wordProgresses,
    totalProgress: perModeResult.totalProgressPercent,
    refresh,
    isLoading,
  };
};

// Hook for single word progress
export const useWordProgress = (wordId: number) => {
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(() =>
    WordProgressKey.getWordProgress(wordId)
  );

  const refresh = useCallback(() => {
    setProgress(WordProgressKey.getWordProgress(wordId));
  }, [wordId]);

  const increment = useCallback(
    async (mode: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5, by: number = 1) => {
      const newProgress = await WordProgressKey.incrementMode(wordId, mode, by);
      setProgress(newProgress);
      return newProgress;
    },
    [wordId]
  );

  return { progress, refresh, increment };
};

export default useProgress;

/**
 * Src/hooks/useSafeArea.ts & src/hooks/useSafeArea.ts
 * Safe Area Hook - Wrapper with 40/20/20 Layout Support
 */

import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useMemo } from "react";
import { Spacing } from "../theme/radius";

export interface SafeAreaStyle {
  paddingTop: number;
  paddingBottom: number;
  paddingLeft: number;
  paddingRight: number;
}

export const useSafeArea = () => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

  const safeStyle: SafeAreaStyle = useMemo(
    () => ({
      paddingTop: Math.max(insets.top, Spacing.padding.sm),
      paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, Spacing.padding.sm),
      paddingLeft: Math.max(insets.left, Spacing.screen.horizontal),
      paddingRight: Math.max(insets.right, Spacing.screen.horizontal),
    }),
    [insets]
  );

  // Layout Proportions for TTS (40/20/20)
  const layout = useMemo(
    () => ({
      topHeaderHeight: "12%" as const, // Compact version of 40% conceptual
      coreContentMinHeight: 80,
      bottomPaddingHeight: 120, // 20% security space
      emptyControlHeight: 60, // 20% empty for m2/m5 pause/resume
      screenHorizontalMargin: Spacing.screen.horizontal,
      cardVerticalMargin: Spacing.screen.vertical,
    }),
    []
  );

  return {
    insets,
    safeStyle,
    layout,
    top: insets.top,
    bottom: insets.bottom,
    left: insets.left,
    right: insets.right,
  };
};

export default useSafeArea;

FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/hooks/useTTS.ts

/**
 * src/hooks/useTTS.ts
 * Production-ready Hook for TTS Control with MMKV Central Brain
 * Implements: m1/m4 Card Tap, m2/m5 Empty Space Control, Auto Gap 300ms, Background Audio
 * No Emoji, No Hardcode Theme, Zero Loss Mechanics
 */

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { AppState, AppStateStatus } from "react-native";
import TTSController, {
  TTSMode,
  TTSState,
  ResumePosition,
} from "../services/TTSEngine/TTSController";
import HighlightingEngine, {
  HighlightState,
  ThemeColors,
} from "../services/TTSEngine/HighlightingEngine";
import { ttsMMKV, TTS_KEYS } from "../services/TTSEngine/TTSController";

export interface UseTTSOptions {
  word_id: number;
  fullText: string;
  mode: TTSMode;
  theme?: Partial<ThemeColors>;
  autoPlayGapMs?: number; // Default 300ms as per architecture
  onComplete?: (word_id: number) => void; // For MMKV progress + view_id logic
  onProgress?: (charIndex: number) => void;
}

export interface UseTTSReturn {
  // State
  ttsState: TTSState;
  highlightState: HighlightState;
  isPlaying: boolean;
  isPaused: boolean;
  currentCharIndex: number;
  resumePosition: ResumePosition | null;

  // Controls for m1 & m4 (Sound & Emoji)
  handleCardPress: () => Promise<void>; // Card Tap = Play/Stop

  // Controls for m2 & m5 (Play Modes - Empty Space Control)
  handleEmptySpacePress: () => Promise<void>; // Empty Area 20% Top/Bottom = Pause/Resume

  // Global Controls
  stop: () => Promise<void>;
  pause: () => Promise<void>;
  resume: () => Promise<void>;

  // Styles (No Hardcode Theme)
  getCardStyle: (isActive: boolean) => any;
  getTextStyle: (isHighlighted: boolean) => any;
  highlightedSegments: Array<{ text: string; highlighted: boolean }>;
}

export const useTTS = (options: UseTTSOptions): UseTTSReturn => {
  const {
    word_id,
    fullText,
    mode,
    theme,
    autoPlayGapMs = 300,
    onComplete,
    onProgress,
  } = options;

  const [ttsState, setTtsState] = useState<TTSState>("STOPPED");
  const [highlightState, setHighlightState] = useState<HighlightState>({
    isHighlighted: false,
    word_id: null,
    startChar: 0,
    endChar: 0,
    mode: null,
  });
  const [currentCharIndex, setCurrentCharIndex] = useState<number>(0);
  const [resumePosition, setResumePosition] = useState<ResumePosition | null>(
    null
  );

  const appStateRef = useRef<AppStateStatus>(AppState.currentState);
  const gapTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);

  // --- Initialize ---
  useEffect(() => {
    TTSController.initialize();
    if (theme) {
      HighlightingEngine.setTheme(theme);
    }

    // Load existing resume position
    const pos = TTSController.getResumePosition();
    if (pos && pos.word_id === word_id) {
      setResumePosition(pos);
      setCurrentCharIndex(pos.char_index);
    }

    // Subscribe to MMKV state changes (for cross-mode sync)
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const state = TTSController.getState() as TTSState;
      setTtsState(state);

      const savedPos = TTSController.getResumePosition();
      if (savedPos && savedPos.word_id === word_id) {
        setResumePosition(savedPos);
      }
    }, 200); // Polling for MMKV sync (MMKV has no built-in listener in JS, but super fast)

    // Subscribe to highlighting
    const unsubscribeHighlight = HighlightingEngine.subscribe((state) => {
      if (state.word_id === word_id || state.word_id === null) {
        setHighlightState(state);
      }
    });

    // AppState listener for Background Audio Logic
    const subscription = AppState.addEventListener("change", (nextAppState) => {
      if (
        appStateRef.current.match(/inactive|background/) &&
        nextAppState === "active"
      ) {
        // Came back to foreground
      } else if (nextAppState.match(/inactive|background/)) {
        // Going to background
        const bgEnabled = TTSController.isBackgroundEnabled();
        const activeMode = TTSController.getActiveMode();
        if (!bgEnabled || (activeMode !== "m2" && activeMode !== "m5")) {
          // If not m2/m5 or background disabled, stop
          TTSController.onNavigationExit();
        }
      }
      appStateRef.current = nextAppState;
    });

    return () => {
      clearInterval(interval);
      unsubscribeHighlight();
      subscription.remove();
      if (gapTimeoutRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(gapTimeoutRef.current);
      }
    };
  }, [word_id, theme]);

  // --- Derived ---
  const isPlaying = ttsState === "PLAYING";
  const isPaused = ttsState === "PAUSED";

  const highlightedSegments = HighlightingEngine.getHighlightedSegments(
    fullText,
    highlightState
  );

  // --- m1 & m4 Logic: Card Press = Play/Stop ---
  const handleCardPress = useCallback(async () => {
    const currentMode = TTSController.getActiveMode();
    const currentState = TTSController.getState();

    // If same card is playing, stop it
    if (currentState === "PLAYING" && currentMode === mode) {
      const pos = TTSController.getResumePosition();
      if (pos && pos.word_id === word_id) {
        // Pause and save char position
        await TTSController.pause(word_id, currentCharIndex);
        HighlightingEngine.endHighlight();
        setTtsState("PAUSED");
      } else {
        await TTSController.stopImmediately("MANUAL_STOP");
        HighlightingEngine.endHighlight();
      }
      return;
    }

    // If paused on same word, resume
    if (
      currentState === "PAUSED" &&
      resumePosition &&
      resumePosition.word_id === word_id
    ) {
      await TTSController.resume(fullText, word_id);
      HighlightingEngine.startHighlight(
        word_id,
        resumePosition.char_index,
        resumePosition.char_index + 10,
        mode
      );
      return;
    }

    // Conflict: Auto (m2/m5) running, manual (m1/m4) interrupt
    if (
      currentState === "PLAYING" &&
      (currentMode === "m2" || currentMode === "m5")
    ) {
      await TTSController.handleManualInterruptDuringAuto();
    }

    // Start new playback
    TTSController.setActiveMode(mode);
    HighlightingEngine.startHighlight(
      word_id,
      currentCharIndex,
      currentCharIndex + 5,
      mode
    );
    await TTSController.play(fullText, word_id, mode);

    // MMKV Progress Tracking for view_id Logic (m1>0 etc)
    // This is handled outside, but we trigger onComplete callback on finish via polling
  }, [word_id, fullText, mode, currentCharIndex, resumePosition]);

  // --- m2 & m5 Logic: Empty Space Press = Pause/Resume ---
  const handleEmptySpacePress = useCallback(async () => {
    // Only for m2 & m5
    if (mode !== "m2" && mode !== "m5") {
      return;
    }

    const state = TTSController.getState();

    if (state === "PLAYING") {
      // Pause
      await TTSController.pause(word_id, currentCharIndex);
      HighlightingEngine.endHighlight();
      setTtsState("PAUSED");
    } else if (state === "PAUSED") {
      // Resume from same point
      await TTSController.resume(fullText, word_id);
      setTtsState("PLAYING");
    } else {
      // If stopped, start
      await handleCardPress();
    }
  }, [mode, word_id, currentCharIndex, fullText, handleCardPress]);

  const stop = useCallback(async () => {
    await TTSController.stopImmediately("MANUAL_STOP");
    HighlightingEngine.endHighlight();
    setTtsState("STOPPED");
  }, []);

  const pause = useCallback(async () => {
    await TTSController.pause(word_id, currentCharIndex);
    setTtsState("PAUSED");
  }, [word_id, currentCharIndex]);

  const resume = useCallback(async () => {
    await TTSController.resume(fullText, word_id);
    setTtsState("PLAYING");
  }, [word_id, fullText]);

  // --- Auto-Play Gap Logic for m2/m5 Sequence ---
  useEffect(() => {
    // When TTS finishes, if in m2/m5 auto mode, wait gap then trigger next
    if (
      ttsState === "STOPPED" &&
      highlightState.word_id === word_id &&
      highlightState.isHighlighted === false
    ) {
      const wasPlaying = resumePosition !== null || currentCharIndex > 0;
      if (wasPlaying && (mode === "m2" || mode === "m5")) {
        // Auto sequence: wait 300ms gap as per architecture
        gapTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
          onComplete?.(word_id);
        }, autoPlayGapMs);
      }
    }
  }, [
    ttsState,
    word_id,
    mode,
    autoPlayGapMs,
    onComplete,
    highlightState,
    resumePosition,
    currentCharIndex,
  ]);

  // --- Style Getters (No Hardcode) ---
  const getCardStyle = useCallback(
    (isActive: boolean) => {
      return HighlightingEngine.getCardContainerStyle(
        isActive,
        highlightState.isHighlighted && highlightState.word_id === word_id
      );
    },
    [highlightState]
  );

  const getTextStyle = useCallback((isHighlighted: boolean) => {
    return HighlightingEngine.getHighlightedTextStyle(isHighlighted);
  }, []);

  return {
    ttsState,
    highlightState,
    isPlaying,
    isPaused,
    currentCharIndex,
    resumePosition,
    handleCardPress,
    handleEmptySpacePress,
    stop,
    pause,
    resume,
    getCardStyle,
    getTextStyle,
    highlightedSegments,
  };
};

export default useTTS;

/**
 * src/screens/ChapterScreen.tsx
 * Chapter Detail - Word List with Mode Progress
 * Shows: Display IDs with view_id status, Mode progress per word
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import { View, Text, FlatList, Pressable, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import Icon from "react-native-vector-icons/MaterialCommunityIcons";
import { useRoute, useNavigation } from "@react-navigation/native";
import WordTable, { WordRow } from "../database/WordTable";
import WordProgressKey from "../storage/keys/WordProgressKey";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";

export const ChapterScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const route = useRoute() as any;
  const navigation = useNavigation() as any;
  const { chapter_id, chapter_name } = route.params;
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

  const [words, setWords] = useState<
    (WordRow & {
      progress: ReturnType<typeof WordProgressKey.getWordProgress>;
    })[]
  >([]);
  const [totalProgress, setTotalProgress] = useState(0);

  const loadWords = useCallback(async () => {
    const wordList = await WordTable.getWordsByChapter(chapter_id);
    const enriched = wordList.map((w) => ({
      ...w,
      progress: WordProgressKey.getWordProgress(w.word_id),
    }));
    setWords(enriched);

    const completed = enriched.filter((w) => w.progress.isCompleted).length;
    setTotalProgress(
      enriched.length > 0 ? Math.round((completed / enriched.length) * 100) : 0
    );
  }, [chapter_id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadWords();
  }, [loadWords]);

  const handleWordPress = (word: WordRow) => {
    navigation.navigate("WordDetail", {
      word_id: word.word_id,
      display_word_id: word.display_word_id,
      chapter_name,
      chapter_id,
    });
  };

  const renderWordItem = ({
    item,
    index,
  }: {
    item: (typeof words)[0];
    index: number;
  }) => {
    const { m1, m2, m3, m4, m5, isCompleted, completedModesCount } =
      item.progress;
    const modes = [
      { key: "m1", done: m1 > 0, icon: "volume-high" },
      { key: "m2", done: m2 > 0, icon: "play-circle-outline" },
      { key: "m3", done: m3 > 0, icon: "book-open-page-variant" },
      { key: "m4", done: m4 > 0, icon: "lightbulb-on-outline" },
      { key: "m5", done: m5 > 0, icon: "play-speed" },
    ];

    return (
      <Pressable
        onPress={() => handleWordPress(item)}
        style={[styles.wordCard, isCompleted && styles.wordCompleted]}
      >
        <View style={styles.wordHeader}>
          <View style={styles.wordIdBox}>
            <Text style={styles.wordIdText}>#{item.display_word_id}</Text>
          </View>
          <Text style={styles.wordContent} numberOfLines={1}>
            {item.content?.substring(0, 50) || `Word ${item.display_word_id}`}
          </Text>
          <View style={styles.completedBadge}>
            <Text style={styles.completedText}>{completedModesCount}/5</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.modesRow}>
          {modes.map((mode) => (
            <View
              key={mode.key}
              style={[styles.modeDot, mode.done && styles.modeDotDone]}
            >
              <Icon
                name={mode.icon as any}
                size={12}
                color={mode.done ? "#FFFFFF" : "#938F99"}
              />
            </View>
          ))}
          {isCompleted && (
            <Icon
              name="check-circle"
              size={18}
              color="#4CAF50"
              style={{ marginLeft: 8 }}
            />
          )}
        </View>
      </Pressable>
    );
  };

  return (
    <View style={[styles.container, { paddingTop: insets.top }]}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <Pressable
          onPress={() => navigation.goBack()}
          style={styles.backButton}
        >
          <Icon name="arrow-left" size={22} color="#1C1B1F" />
        </Pressable>
        <View style={styles.headerCenter}>
          <Text style={styles.headerTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
            {chapter_name}
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.headerSub}>
            {words.length} শব্দ • {totalProgress}% সম্পন্ন
          </Text>
        </View>
        <View style={styles.progressCircle}>
          <Text style={styles.progressText}>{totalProgress}%</Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      <FlatList
        data={words}
        renderItem={renderWordItem}
        keyExtractor={(item) => `word-${item.word_id}`}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
        numColumns={1}
      />
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#FFFBFE" },
  header: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    padding: 16,
    backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    borderBottomColor: "#F3EDF7",
  },
  backButton: {
    width: 40,
    height: 40,
    borderRadius: 12,
    backgroundColor: "#F3EDF7",
    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",
    marginRight: 12,
  },
  headerCenter: { flex: 1 },
  headerTitle: { fontSize: 16, fontWeight: "600", color: "#1C1B1F" },
  headerSub: { fontSize: 11, color: "#49454F", marginTop: 2 },
  progressCircle: {
    width: 44,
    height: 44,
    borderRadius: 22,
    backgroundColor: "#E8DEF8",
    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",
  },
  progressText: { fontSize: 12, fontWeight: "700", color: "#6750A4" },

/**
 * src/screens/DailyEventScreen.tsx
 * Daily Event Center - 🎯 Center UI with 4 Stages
 * Shows: 490% Bank Header + Stage 1 ACTIVE + Stage 2,3,4 LOCKED + Event Cards
 * Each Card: [Icon] [Chapter #ID] [+1%] - No Extra Text
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Pressable,
  RefreshControl,
} from "react-native";
import Icon from "react-native-vector-icons/MaterialCommunityIcons";
import { useNavigation, useFocusEffect } from "@react-navigation/native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import DailyEventCenter, {
  DailyEventState,
} from "../services/EventEngine/DailyEventCenter";
import EventCard from "../components/Cards/EventCard";
import BankManager from "../storage/DailyBank/BankManager";
import WordTable from "../database/WordTable";
import { dailyBankMMKV } from "../storage/MMKVInstance";

export const DailyEventScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const navigation = useNavigation() as any;
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const [eventState, setEventState] = useState<DailyEventState | null>(null);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [chapterWordIds, setChapterWordIds] = useState<number[]>([]);

  const loadEventData = useCallback(async () => {
    // For demo, get all words from first chapter or all
    // In real app, pass chapterWordIds from Home or combine all chapters
    try {
      const [result] = await (await import("../database/SQLiteManager")).default
        .getDB()
        .then((db) => db.executeSql("SELECT word_id FROM WordTable LIMIT 20;"));
      const ids: number[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < result.rows.length; i++) {
        ids.push(result.rows.item(i).word_id);
      }

      if (ids.length === 0) {
        // Fallback mock IDs for demo
        setChapterWordIds([
          101, 102, 103, 104, 105, 106, 107, 108, 109, 110, 111, 112, 113, 114,
          115, 116, 117, 118, 119, 120,
        ]);
      } else {
        setChapterWordIds(ids);
      }

      const state = await DailyEventCenter.initialize(
        ids.length > 0 ? ids : [101, 102, 103, 104, 105]
      );
      setEventState(state);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[DailyEventScreen] Load failed:", error);
      // Fallback with mock
      const mockIds = [101, 102, 103, 104, 105, 106, 107, 108, 109, 110];
      setChapterWordIds(mockIds);
      const state = await DailyEventCenter.initialize(mockIds);
      setEventState(state);
    }
  }, []);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      DailyEventCenter.resetIfNewDay();
      loadEventData();
    }, [loadEventData])
  );

  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await loadEventData();
    setRefreshing(false);
  }, [loadEventData]);

  const handleCardPress = async (cardId: string) => {
    if (!eventState) {
      return;
    }

    // Find card
    const allCards = [
      ...eventState.stages.stage1.cards,
      ...eventState.stages.stage2.cards,
      ...eventState.stages.stage3.cards,
      ...eventState.stages.stage4.cards,
    ];
    const card = allCards.find((c) => c.id === cardId);
    if (!card) {
      return;
    }

    // Navigate to WordDetail for first word in card
    const firstWordId = card.wordIds[0];
    const word = await WordTable.getWordById(firstWordId);

    if (word) {
      navigation.navigate("WordDetail", {
        word_id: word.word_id,
        display_word_id: word.display_word_id,
        chapter_name: word.chapter_name,
        chapter_id: word.chapter_id,
        fromEvent: true,
        eventCardId: cardId,
      });

      // Simulate completion after navigation (in real app, on return)
      // For now, mark as completed when pressed
      const result = await DailyEventCenter.onCardCompleted(card);
      await loadEventData();
    }
  };

  if (!eventState) {
    return (
      <View
        style={[
          styles.container,
          {
            paddingTop: insets.top,
            justifyContent: "center",
            alignItems: "center",
          },
        ]}
      >
        <Text>লোড হচ্ছে...</Text>
      </View>
    );
  }

  const renderStage = (
    stageNum: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4,
    stageData: DailyEventState["stages"][`stage${1 | 2 | 3 | 4}`],
    title: string
  ) => {
    const isLocked = stageData.isLocked;
    const completedCount = stageData.completedCount;
    const totalCount = stageData.totalCount;

    return (
      <View
        key={stageNum}
        style={[styles.stageContainer, isLocked && styles.stageLocked]}
      >
        <View style={styles.stageHeader}>
          <View style={styles.stageTitleRow}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.stageIconBox,
                isLocked ? styles.stageIconLocked : styles.stageIconActive,
              ]}
            >
              <Icon
                name={isLocked ? "lock-outline" : "play-circle-outline"}
                size={18}
                color={isLocked ? "#938F99" : "#FFFFFF"}
              />
            </View>
            <View>
              <Text
                style={[styles.stageTitle, isLocked && styles.stageTitleLocked]}
              >
                {title}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.stageSub}>
                {isLocked
                  ? `পর্যায় ${stageNum - 1} এর সকল টাস্ক সম্পন্ন করুন`
                  : `${completedCount}/${totalCount} সম্পন্ন • ${stageData.creditEarned}% অর্জিত`}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
          <View
            style={[styles.creditBadge, isLocked && styles.creditBadgeLocked]}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.creditBadgeText,
                isLocked && styles.creditBadgeTextLocked,
              ]}
            >
              ৫%
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        {isLocked ? (
          <View style={styles.lockedOverlay}>
            <Icon name="lock-outline" size={32} color="#E7E0EC" />
            <Text style={styles.lockedText}>লকড - আগের পর্যায় শেষ করুন</Text>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <View style={styles.cardsList}>
            {stageData.cards.length === 0 ? (
              <Text style={styles.noCardsText}>
                এই পর্যায়ে কোনো কার্ড নেই - দুর্দান্ত! সব সম্পন্ন
              </Text>
            ) : (
              stageData.cards.map((card) => (
                <EventCard
                  key={card.id}
                  id={card.id}
                  iconName={card.modeIcon}
                  title={card.title}
                  creditPercent={card.creditPercent}
                  stage={card.stage}
                  mode={card.mode}
                  isLocked={false}
                  isCompleted={false}
                  onPress={handleCardPress}
                />
              ))
            )}
          </View>
        )}
      </View>
    );
  };

  return (
    <View style={[styles.container, { paddingTop: insets.top }]}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <Pressable
          onPress={() => navigation.goBack()}
          style={styles.backButton}
        >
          <Icon name="arrow-left" size={22} color="#1C1B1F" />
        </Pressable>
        <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>🎯 ডেইলি ইভেন্ট সেন্টার</Text>
        <Pressable onPress={onRefresh} style={styles.refreshButton}>
          <Icon name="refresh" size={20} color="#49454F" />
        </Pressable>
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        style={styles.scroll}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} />
        }
      >
        {/* Bank Header - 490% Credit */}
        <View style={styles.bankHeader}>
          <View style={styles.bankRow}>
            <Icon name="calendar-check-outline" size={20} color="#6750A4" />
            <Text style={styles.bankTitle}>ডেইলি ব্যাংক টোটাল ব্যালেন্স:</Text>
          </View>
          <Text style={styles.bankBalance}>
            {eventState.bankBalance}% ক্রেডিট
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.bankSub}>
            (আজকের অর্জন: +{eventState.todayEarned}% | মোট উপলব্ধ:{" "}
            {eventState.bankBalance}%)
          </Text>
          <View style={styles.bankProgress}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.bankFill,
                { width: `${Math.min(100, eventState.bankBalance)}%` },
              ]}
            />
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* 4 Stages */}
        {renderStage(1, eventState.stages.stage1, "পর্যায় ১ (ACTIVE) — ৫%")}
        {renderStage(2, eventState.stages.stage2, "পর্যায় ২ (LOCKED) — ৫%")}
        {renderStage(3, eventState.stages.stage3, "পর্যায় ৩ (LOCKED) — ৫%")}
        {renderStage(4, eventState.stages.stage4, "পর্যায় ৪ (LOCKED) — ৫%")}

        <View style={styles.footer}>
          <Text style={styles.footerText}>
            প্রতিদিন ২০% ক্রেডিট উপার্জন করুন (৪ পর্যায় × ৫%)
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.footerSub}>
            দুর্বলতম শব্দগুলো স্বয়ংক্রিয়ভাবে সনাক্ত
          </Text>
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

  header: {

/**
 * src/screens/HomeScreen.tsx
 * Home - Chapter List with Chapter Box Progress (Instant MMKV Read)
 * Shows: Chapter Name + Green Bar + Percentage + Lock/Unlock + Bank Credit Button
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  FlatList,
  Pressable,
  StyleSheet,
  RefreshControl,
} from "react-native";
import Icon from "react-native-vector-icons/MaterialCommunityIcons";
import { useFocusEffect, useNavigation } from "@react-navigation/native";
import ChapterTable from "../database/ChapterTable";
import ChapterBoxKey from "../storage/keys/ChapterBoxKey";
import ChapterEncapsulation from "../services/ProgressEngine/ChapterEncapsulation";
import ChapterExecutive from "../services/ProgressEngine/Guards/ChapterExecutive";
import BankManager from "../storage/DailyBank/BankManager";
import DynamicTarget from "../storage/DailyBank/DynamicTarget";
import { Radius, Spacing } from "../theme/radius";

export interface ChapterCardData {
  chapter_id: number;
  chapter_name: string;
  total_words: number;
  completed_words: number;
  progressPercent: number;
  isUnlocked: boolean;
  targetPercent: number;
  canUseBank: boolean;
  needsBankCredit: number;
  executiveMessage: string;
}

export const HomeScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const navigation = useNavigation() as any;
  const [chapters, setChapters] = useState<ChapterCardData[]>([]);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [bankBalance, setBankBalance] = useState(0);

  const loadChapters = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const allChapters = await ChapterTable.getAllChaptersWithProgress();
      const bank = BankManager.getBalance().totalBalance;
      setBankBalance(bank);

      const cards: ChapterCardData[] = [];

      for (const status of allChapters) {
        const box = ChapterEncapsulation.getChapterBox(status.chapter_id);
        const exec = await ChapterExecutive.check(status.chapter_id);

        cards.push({
          chapter_id: status.chapter_id,
          chapter_name: status.chapter_name,
          total_words: status.total,
          completed_words: status.completed,
          progressPercent: box.totalProgressPercent || status.completedPercent,
          isUnlocked: exec.isUnlocked,
          targetPercent: exec.targetPercent,
          canUseBank: exec.canUseBank,
          needsBankCredit: exec.needsBankCredit,
          executiveMessage: exec.message,
        });
      }

      // Sort by chapter_id
      cards.sort((a, b) => a.chapter_id - b.chapter_id);
      setChapters(cards);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[HomeScreen] Load failed:", error);
    }
  }, []);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      loadChapters();
    }, [loadChapters])
  );

  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await loadChapters();
    setRefreshing(false);
  }, [loadChapters]);

  const handleChapterPress = (chapter: ChapterCardData) => {
    if (!chapter.isUnlocked) {
      return;
    }
    navigation.navigate("Chapter", {
      chapter_id: chapter.chapter_id,
      chapter_name: chapter.chapter_name,
    });
  };

  const handleBankUnlock = async (chapter: ChapterCardData) => {
    const result = await ChapterExecutive.unlockViaBank(chapter.chapter_id);
    if (result.success) {
      await loadChapters();
    }
  };

  const renderChapterCard = ({ item }: { item: ChapterCardData }) => {
    return (
      <Pressable
        onPress={() => handleChapterPress(item)}
        style={({ pressed }) => [
          styles.card,
          item.isUnlocked ? styles.cardUnlocked : styles.cardLocked,
          pressed && item.isUnlocked && { transform: [{ scale: 0.98 }] },
        ]}
        disabled={!item.isUnlocked}
      >
        {/* Header: Icon + Name + Lock State */}
        <View style={styles.cardHeader}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.iconBox,
              { backgroundColor: item.isUnlocked ? "#E8DEF8" : "#F3EDF7" },
            ]}
          >
            <Icon
              name={item.isUnlocked ? "book-open-page-variant" : "lock-outline"}
              size={20}
              color={item.isUnlocked ? "#6750A4" : "#938F99"}
            />
          </View>
          <View style={styles.headerText}>
            <Text style={styles.chapterName} numberOfLines={1}>
              {item.chapter_name}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.chapterMeta}>
              {item.completed_words}/{item.total_words} শব্দ •{" "}
              {item.targetPercent}% প্রয়োজন
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.percentBadge}>
            <Icon
              name="chart-donut-variant"
              size={14}
              color={item.isUnlocked ? "#6750A4" : "#938F99"}
            />
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.percentText,
                !item.isUnlocked && { color: "#938F99" },
              ]}
            >
              {item.progressPercent}%
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Progress Bar - Green Fill */}
        <View style={styles.progressTrack}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.progressFill,
              {
                width: `${item.progressPercent}%`,
                backgroundColor: item.isUnlocked ? "#4CAF50" : "#E7E0EC",
              },
            ]}
          />
        </View>

        {/* Bank Credit Button - Invisible UI Magic */}
        {!item.isUnlocked && item.progressPercent >= 50 && item.canUseBank && (
          <Pressable
            onPress={() => handleBankUnlock(item)}
            style={styles.bankButton}
          >
            <Icon name="lightning-bolt-outline" size={16} color="#FFFFFF" />
            <Text style={styles.bankButtonText}>
              -{item.needsBankCredit}% ব্যাংক ক্রেডিট কেটে আনলক করুন
            </Text>
          </Pressable>
        )}

        {!item.isUnlocked && item.progressPercent < 50 && (
          <Text style={styles.lockHint}>
            আরও {50 - item.progressPercent}% প্রয়োজন ব্যাংক ব্যবহারের জন্য
          </Text>
        )}
      </Pressable>
    );
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {/* Bank Header */}
      <View style={styles.bankHeader}>
        <Icon name="calendar-check-outline" size={20} color="#6750A4" />
        <Text style={styles.bankText}>
          ডেইলি ব্যাংক: {bankBalance}% ক্রেডিট
        </Text>
        <Pressable
          onPress={() => navigation.navigate("DailyEvent")}
          style={styles.eventButton}
        >
          <Text style={styles.eventButtonText}>ইভেন্ট সেন্টার</Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>

      <FlatList
        data={chapters}
        renderItem={renderChapterCard}
        keyExtractor={(item) => `chapter-${item.chapter_id}`}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl
            refreshing={refreshing}
            onRefresh={onRefresh}
            colors={["#6750A4"]}
          />
        }
        ListEmptyComponent={
          <View style={styles.empty}>
            <Icon name="book-outline" size={48} color="#E7E0EC" />
            <Text style={styles.emptyText}>কোনো চ্যাপ্টার নেই</Text>
            <Text style={styles.emptySubText}>
              সেটিংস থেকে ম্যানিফেস্টো ডাউনলোড করুন
            </Text>
          </View>
        }
      />
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#FFFBFE" },
  bankHeader: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
    padding: 12,
    margin: 16,
    borderRadius: 12,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#E7E0EC",
    gap: 8,
  },
  bankText: { flex: 1, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: "600", color: "#1C1B1F" },
  eventButton: {
    backgroundColor: "#6750A4",
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 6,
    borderRadius: 8,
  },
  eventButtonText: { fontSize: 12, color: "#FFFFFF", fontWeight: "600" },
  listContent: { paddingBottom: 20 },
  card: {
    backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
    borderRadius: 16,
    padding: 16,
    marginHorizontal: 16,
    marginVertical: 6,
    borderWidth: 1,
    overflow: "hidden",
  },
  cardUnlocked: { borderColor: "#E7E0EC", elevation: 2 },
  cardLocked: { borderColor: "#F3EDF7", backgroundColor: "#FFFBFE" },
  cardHeader: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 12 },
  iconBox: {
    width: 36,
    height: 36,
    borderRadius: 12,
    justifyContent: "center",
    alignItems: "center",
    marginRight: 12,
  },
  headerText: { flex: 1 },
  chapterName: { fontSize: 15, fontWeight: "600", color: "#1C1B1F" },
  chapterMeta: { fontSize: 11, color: "#49454F", marginTop: 2 },
  percentBadge: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    gap: 4,
    backgroundColor: "#F3EDF7",
    paddingHorizontal: 8,
    paddingVertical: 4,
    borderRadius: 12,
  },
  percentText: { fontSize: 12, fontWeight: "600", color: "#6750A4" },
  progressTrack: {
    height: 6,
    backgroundColor: "#F3EDF7",
    borderRadius: 3,
    overflow: "hidden",
  },
  progressFill: { height: "100%", borderRadius: 3 },
  bankButton: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    backgroundColor: "#6750A4",
    padding: 10,
    borderRadius: 12,
    marginTop: 12,
    gap: 6,
    justifyContent: "center",
  },
  bankButtonText: { fontSize: 12, color: "#FFFFFF", fontWeight: "600" },
  lockHint: {
    fontSize: 11,
    color: "#938F99",
    marginTop: 8,
    fontStyle: "italic",
  },
  empty: { alignItems: "center", padding: 40, marginTop: 40 },
  emptyText: {
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: "600",
    color: "#49454F",
    marginTop: 12,
  },
  emptySubText: { fontSize: 12, color: "#938F99", marginTop: 4 },
});

export default HomeScreen;

/**
 * src/screens/SettingsScreen.tsx
 * Settings Screen - Combines all Settings Panels
 * Manifesto Downloader + TTS Slider + Control Panel 1 & 2 + Theme Selector
 * Final Assembly as per Architecture
 */

import React, { useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { View, Text, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Alert } from "react-native";
import Icon from "react-native-vector-icons/MaterialCommunityIcons";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useNavigation } from "@react-navigation/native";
import { Pressable } from "react-native";

import ManifestoDownloader, {
  ManifestoItem,
} from "../components/Settings/ManifestoDownloader";
import TTSControlSlider from "../components/Settings/TTSControlSlider";
import ControlPanel1 from "../components/Settings/ControlPanel1";
import {
  ControlPanel2Both,
  PlayModeFilters,
} from "../components/Settings/ControlPanel2";
import ThemeSelector, {
  ThemeMode,
  LanguageMode,
} from "../components/Settings/ThemeSelector";
import { ttsBrainMMKV } from "../storage/MMKVInstance";

// Fixed Manifesto JSON (Example - In real app, fetch from server or bundle)
const FIXED_MANIFESTO: ManifestoItem[] = [
  {
    manifesto_id: 1,
    chapter_id: 1,
    chapter_name: "অপরিচিতা",
    is_downloaded: 0,
  },
  { manifesto_id: 2, chapter_id: 2, chapter_name: "বিলাসী", is_downloaded: 0 },
  { manifesto_id: 3, chapter_id: 3, chapter_name: "হৈমন্তী", is_downloaded: 0 },
  { manifesto_id: 4, chapter_id: 4, chapter_name: "নববর্ষ", is_downloaded: 0 },
  {
    manifesto_id: 5,
    chapter_id: 5,
    chapter_name: "সহযাত্রী",
    is_downloaded: 0,
  },
];

export const SettingsScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const navigation = useNavigation() as any;
  const [activeFilters, setActiveFilters] = useState<Record<string, any>>({});

  const handleFilterChange = useCallback((mode: number, filter: any) => {
    setActiveFilters((prev) => ({ ...prev, [`mode_${mode}`]: filter }));
    // Save to MMKV for use in Chapter/Word screens
    ttsBrainMMKV.set(`filter_mode_${mode}`, JSON.stringify(filter));
    console.log(`[Settings] Mode ${mode} filter:`, filter);
  }, []);

  const handlePlayFilterChange = useCallback(
    (mode: 2 | 5, filters: PlayModeFilters) => {
      setActiveFilters((prev) => ({ ...prev, [`play_mode_${mode}`]: filters }));
      ttsBrainMMKV.set(`play_filter_mode_${mode}`, JSON.stringify(filters));
      console.log(`[Settings] Play Mode ${mode} filters:`, filters);
    },
    []
  );

  const handleThemeChange = (theme: ThemeMode) => {
    console.log(`[Settings] Theme changed to: ${theme}`);
    // In real app, update ThemeContext
  };

  const handleLanguageChange = (lang: LanguageMode) => {
    console.log(`[Settings] Language changed to: ${lang}`);
  };

  return (
    <View style={[styles.container, { paddingTop: insets.top }]}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <Pressable
          onPress={() => navigation.goBack()}
          style={styles.backButton}
        >
          <Icon name="arrow-left" size={22} color="#1C1B1F" />
        </Pressable>
        <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>সেটিংস</Text>
        <View style={styles.headerRight} />
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        style={styles.scroll}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      >
        {/* 1. Manifesto Download List */}
        <ManifestoDownloader
          manifestData={FIXED_MANIFESTO}
          onDownloadComplete={(chapter_id) => {
            Alert.alert(
              "ডাউনলোড সম্পন্ন",
              `চ্যাপ্টার ${chapter_id} ডাউনলোড হয়েছে`
            );
          }}
          onDeleteComplete={(chapter_id) => {
            Alert.alert(
              "ডিলিট সম্পন্ন",
              `চ্যাপ্টার ${chapter_id} ডিলিট হয়েছে`
            );
          }}
        />

        {/* 2. TTS Control Slider + Time Gap */}
        <TTSControlSlider />

        {/* 3. First Control Panel (m1, m3, m4) */}
        <ControlPanel1 onFilterChange={handleFilterChange} />

        {/* 4. Second Control Panel (m2 & m5 Independent) */}
        <ControlPanel2Both onFilterChange={handlePlayFilterChange} />

        {/* 5. Theme & Language Selector */}
        <ThemeSelector
          onThemeChange={handleThemeChange}
          onLanguageChange={handleLanguageChange}
        />

        {/* Debug Info */}
        <View style={styles.debugContainer}>
          <Text style={styles.debugTitle}>ডিবাগ তথ্য</Text>
          <Text style={styles.debugText}>
            Active Filters: {Object.keys(activeFilters).length}
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.debugText}>
            MMKV Keys: {ttsBrainMMKV.getAllKeys().length}
          </Text>
          <Pressable
            onPress={() => {
              Alert.alert("ক্যাশ ক্লিয়ার", "সব ফিল্টার রিসেট করবেন?", [
                { text: "বাতিল", style: "cancel" },
                {
                  text: "রিসেট",
                  style: "destructive",
                  onPress: () => {
                    ttsBrainMMKV.clearAll();
                    setActiveFilters({});
                    Alert.alert("রিসেট সম্পন্ন");
                  },
                },
              ]);
            }}
            style={styles.resetButton}
          >
            <Icon name="delete-sweep-outline" size={18} color="#BA1A1A" />
            <Text style={styles.resetText}>সব ফিল্টার রিসেট</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <View style={{ height: 40 }} />
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
};

/**
 * src/screens/SplashScreen.tsx
 * App Launch Splash - Uses W Logo (Book W)
 * Android Native Splash Style, Premium Minimalist
 */

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  Image,
  StyleSheet,
  Animated,
  StatusBar,
} from "react-native";

export interface SplashScreenProps {
  onFinish?: () => void;
}

export const SplashScreen: React.FC<SplashScreenProps> = ({ onFinish }) => {
  const fadeAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const scaleAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(0.8)).current;

  useEffect(() => {
    Animated.parallel([
      Animated.timing(fadeAnim, {
        toValue: 1,
        duration: 800,
        useNativeDriver: true,
      }),
      Animated.spring(scaleAnim, {
        toValue: 1,
        tension: 10,
        friction: 5,
        useNativeDriver: true,
      }),
    ]).start();
  }, [fadeAnim, scaleAnim]);

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <StatusBar barStyle="dark-content" backgroundColor="#FFFFFF" />
      <Animated.View
        style={[
          styles.logoContainer,
          {
            opacity: fadeAnim,
            transform: [{ scale: scaleAnim }],
          },
        ]}
      >
        <Image
          source={require("../assets/images/splash_logo.png")}
          style={styles.logo}
          resizeMode="contain"
        />
      </Animated.View>

      <View style={styles.footer}>
        <Text style={styles.appName}>Word Learning</Text>
        <Text style={styles.tagline}>অফলাইন শব্দ শেখা</Text>
        <View style={styles.dots}>
          <View style={[styles.dot, styles.dotActive]} />
          <View style={styles.dot} />
          <View style={styles.dot} />
        </View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    flex: 1,

/**
 * src/screens/WordDetailScreen.tsx
 * Word Detail - 5 Modes Card Layout
 * Implements: 40% Top Header, 20% Core Content, 20% Bottom Padding, 20% Empty for m2/m5
 * TTS Control: m1/m4 Card Tap = Play/Stop, m2/m5 Empty Space = Pause/Resume
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import { View, Text, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Pressable } from "react-native";
import Icon from "react-native-vector-icons/MaterialCommunityIcons";
import { useRoute, useNavigation } from "@react-navigation/native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import ModeCard from "../components/Cards/ModeCard";
import WordProgressKey from "../storage/keys/WordProgressKey";
import ChapterEncapsulation from "../services/ProgressEngine/ChapterEncapsulation";
import WordTable from "../database/WordTable";
import TTSController from "../services/TTSEngine/TTSController";

export const WordDetailScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const route = useRoute() as any;
  const navigation = useNavigation() as any;
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { word_id, display_word_id, chapter_name, chapter_id } = route.params;

  const [content, setContent] = useState<string>("");
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(
    WordProgressKey.getWordProgress(word_id)
  );

  const loadContent = useCallback(async () => {
    const word = await WordTable.getWordById(word_id);
    if (word) {
      setContent(
        word.content || `Content for ${chapter_name} #${display_word_id}`
      );
    }
  }, [word_id, chapter_name, display_word_id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadContent();
    // Navigation Exit = Global Stop if not background
    return () => {
      TTSController.onNavigationExit();
    };
  }, [loadContent]);

  const handleProgressUpdate = useCallback(
    async (wId: number, mode: number) => {
      const newProgress = await WordProgressKey.incrementMode(
        wId,
        mode as any,
        1
      );
      setProgress(newProgress);

      // Update Chapter Box
      const chapterWords = await WordTable.getWordsByChapter(chapter_id);
      const wordIds = chapterWords.map((w) => w.word_id);
      ChapterEncapsulation.saveChapterProgress(chapter_id, wordIds);
    },
    [chapter_id]
  );

  return (
    <View style={[styles.container, { paddingTop: insets.top }]}>
      {/* 40% Top Header & SubHeader */}
      <View style={styles.topHeader}>
        <Pressable
          onPress={() => navigation.goBack()}
          style={styles.backButton}
        >
          <Icon name="arrow-left" size={22} color="#1C1B1F" />
        </Pressable>
        <View style={styles.headerCenter}>
          <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>
            {chapter_name} #{display_word_id}
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.headerSub}>
            5 Modes • {progress.completedModesCount}/5 Completed
          </Text>
        </View>
        <View style={styles.headerRight}>
          <Icon
            name={progress.isCompleted ? "check-circle" : "progress-clock"}
            size={22}
            color={progress.isCompleted ? "#4CAF50" : "#938F99"}
          />
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Scrollable Content */}
      <ScrollView
        style={styles.scrollView}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      >
        {/* Mode Cards - Each has 40/20/20 internal layout */}
        <View style={styles.cardsContainer}>
          {/* m1 - Sound */}
          <ModeCard
            word_id={word_id}
            display_word_id={display_word_id}
            chapter_name={chapter_name}
            content={content}
            mode={1}
            fontSize={16}
            onProgressUpdate={handleProgressUpdate}
          />

          {/* m2 - Play 1 (with empty space control) */}
          <ModeCard
            word_id={word_id}
            display_word_id={display_word_id}
            chapter_name={chapter_name}
            content={content}
            mode={2}
            fontSize={16}
            onProgressUpdate={handleProgressUpdate}
          />

          {/* m3 - Default */}
          <ModeCard
            word_id={word_id}
            display_word_id={display_word_id}
            chapter_name={chapter_name}
            content={content}
            mode={3}
            fontSize={16}
            onProgressUpdate={handleProgressUpdate}
          />

          {/* m4 - Practice */}
          <ModeCard
            word_id={word_id}
            display_word_id={display_word_id}
            chapter_name={chapter_name}
            content={content}
            mode={4}
            fontSize={16}
            onProgressUpdate={handleProgressUpdate}
          />

          {/* m5 - Play 2 (with empty space control) */}
          <ModeCard
            word_id={word_id}
            display_word_id={display_word_id}
            chapter_name={chapter_name}
            content={content}
            mode={5}
            fontSize={16}
            onProgressUpdate={handleProgressUpdate}
          />
        </View>

        {/* 20% Bottom Padding - Security Space */}
        <View style={styles.bottomPadding}>
          <Text style={styles.bottomText}>
            5 Modes Complete = view_id=1 Unlock
          </Text>
          <View style={styles.progressDots}>
            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((m) => {
              const done = (progress as any)[`m${m}`] > 0;
              return (
                <View key={m} style={[styles.dot, done && styles.dotDone]} />
              );
            })}
          </View>
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#FFFBFE" },
  topHeader: {
    height: "12%", // 40% of conceptual top but actual header compact
    minHeight: 60,
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",

  },

=====================================
FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/services/DynamicEngine/ThirtyDayAlgo.ts
=====================================
/**
 * src/services/DynamicEngine/ThirtyDayAlgo.ts
 * Advanced & Non-Linear Dynamic Percentage Generation Algorithm
 * Production wrapper over src/storage/DailyBank/DynamicTarget.ts
 * Adds Android Login Integration, MMKV Persistence, and Chapter-Specific Targets
 */

import { MMKV } from "react-native-mmkv";
import DynamicTarget, {
  ThirtyDaysMetrics,
} from "../../storage/DailyBank/DynamicTarget";
import { dynamicTargetMMKV } from "../../storage/MMKVInstance";
import SQLiteManager from "../../database/SQLiteManager";

export interface AndroidLoginData {
  thirtyDaysActivity: number[]; // [1,0,1,1...] 1=Active, 0=Missed - From Android
  dailyCompletedCounts: number[]; // [5,12,0,8...] - Content completed per day
  lastLoginTimestamp: number;
  streakDays: number;
}

export interface DynamicTargetResult {
  chapterId: number;
  targetPercentage: number; // 50-100%
  metrics: ThirtyDaysMetrics;
  calculatedAt: number;
  isFromCache: boolean;
}

class ThirtyDayAlgoEngine {
  private readonly CACHE_EXPIRY_MS = 1000 * 60 * 60 * 6; // 6 hours cache

  /**
   * Main: Generate Dynamic Target for a Chapter
   * Uses 30-Day Activity from Android Login
   */
  async generateForChapter(
    chapterId: number,
    androidData?: AndroidLoginData
  ): Promise<DynamicTargetResult> {
    // Check cache first
    const cached = this.getCachedTarget(chapterId);
    if (cached && !this.isCacheExpired(cached.calculatedAt)) {
      console.log(
        `[ThirtyDayAlgo] Chapter ${chapterId} using cached target: ${cached.targetPercentage}%`
      );
      return { ...cached, isFromCache: true };
    }

    // Get Android data
    let activity = androidData?.thirtyDaysActivity;
    let counts = androidData?.dailyCompletedCounts;

    if (!activity || !counts) {
      // Load from MMKV or Android Native Module
      const loaded = await DynamicTarget.loadFromAndroid();
      activity = loaded.activity;
      counts = loaded.counts;
    }

    // Generate using core algorithm
    const target = DynamicTarget.generateComplexDynamicTarget(activity, counts);
    const metrics = DynamicTarget.getMetrics();

    // Save chapter-specific target to ChapterTable
    await this.saveToChapterTable(chapterId, target);

    // Save to cache
    const result: DynamicTargetResult = {
      chapterId,
      targetPercentage: target,
      metrics: metrics || this.getFallbackMetrics(target),
      calculatedAt: Date.now(),
      isFromCache: false,
    };

    this.saveToCache(result, activity, counts);

    console.log(`[ThirtyDayAlgo] Chapter ${chapterId} new target: ${target}%`);

    return result;
  }

  private getFallbackMetrics(target: number): ThirtyDaysMetrics {
    return {
      totalActiveDays: 0,
      consistencyRate: 0,
      last7ActiveRate: 0,
      prev23ActiveRate: 0,
      momentum: 0,
      maxGap: 0,
      penaltyFactor: 1,
      avgVolumePerDay: 0,
      volumeScore: 0,
      rawScore: 0,
      finalTarget: target,
    };
  }

  private getCacheKey(chapterId: number): string {
    return `dynamic_target_chapter_${chapterId}`;
  }

  private getCachedTarget(chapterId: number): DynamicTargetResult | null {
    const raw = dynamicTargetMMKV.getString(this.getCacheKey(chapterId));
    if (!raw) {
      return null;
    }
    try {
      return JSON.parse(raw) as DynamicTargetResult;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  private saveToCache(
    result: DynamicTargetResult,
    activity: number[],
    counts: number[]
  ): void {
    dynamicTargetMMKV.set(
      this.getCacheKey(result.chapterId),
      JSON.stringify(result)
    );
    dynamicTargetMMKV.set(
      `thirty_days_activity_chapter_${result.chapterId}`,
      JSON.stringify(activity)
    );
    dynamicTargetMMKV.set(
      `daily_counts_chapter_${result.chapterId}`,
      JSON.stringify(counts)
    );
  }

  private isCacheExpired(calculatedAt: number): boolean {
    return Date.now() - calculatedAt > this.CACHE_EXPIRY_MS;
  }

  private async saveToChapterTable(
    chapterId: number,
    target: number
  ): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
        "UPDATE ChapterTable SET target_percentage = ? WHERE chapter_id = ?;",
        [target, chapterId]
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(
        `[ThirtyDayAlgo] Failed to save target to ChapterTable ${chapterId}:`,
        error
      );
    }
  }

  /**
   * Get current target for chapter (instant MMKV read)
   */
  getCurrentTarget(chapterId: number): number {
    const cached = this.getCachedTarget(chapterId);
    if (cached) {
      return cached.targetPercentage;
    }
    return DynamicTarget.getCurrentTarget();
  }

  /**
   * Recalculate all chapters (daily cron)
   */
  async recalculateAllChapters(
    chapterIds: number[],
    androidData?: AndroidLoginData
  ): Promise<Record<number, DynamicTargetResult>> {
    const results: Record<number, DynamicTargetResult> = {};

    for (const chapterId of chapterIds) {
      results[chapterId] = await this.generateForChapter(
        chapterId,
        androidData
      );
    }

    return results;
  }

  /**
   * Simulate different user behaviors (for testing / demo)
   * high: 28/30 days active, 15+ per day => 95-100% target
   * medium: 15/30 active => 60-75% target
   * low: 8/30 active => 50-55% target (safe zone)
   * churn: 5 day gap => penalty drops to 50%
   */
  simulateAndSave(
    chapterId: number,
    type: "high" | "medium" | "low" | "churn"
  ): DynamicTargetResult {
    const target = DynamicTarget.simulateUser(type);
    const metrics =
      DynamicTarget.getMetrics() || this.getFallbackMetrics(target);

    const result: DynamicTargetResult = {
      chapterId,
      targetPercentage: target,
      metrics,
      calculatedAt: Date.now(),
      isFromCache: false,
    };

    this.saveToCache(result, [], []);
    this.saveToChapterTable(chapterId, target);

    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Get Android Login Data Stub (Replace with NativeModules)
   * In real app:
   * import { NativeModules } from 'react-native';
   * const data = await NativeModules.LoginTracker.getThirtyDaysActivity();
   */
  async getAndroidLoginData(): Promise<AndroidLoginData> {
    try {
      // TODO: Replace with actual native module
      // const nativeData = await NativeModules.LoginTracker.getThirtyDaysActivity();
      // return nativeData;

      // Fallback to MMKV stored data
      const activityRaw = dynamicTargetMMKV.getString("thirty_days_activity");
      const countsRaw = dynamicTargetMMKV.getString("daily_completed_counts");

      if (activityRaw && countsRaw) {
        return {
          thirtyDaysActivity: JSON.parse(activityRaw),
          dailyCompletedCounts: JSON.parse(countsRaw),
          lastLoginTimestamp: Date.now(),
          streakDays: 0,
        };
      }

      // No data - return empty (will trigger baseline 50%)
      return {
        thirtyDaysActivity: Array(30).fill(0),
        dailyCompletedCounts: Array(30).fill(0),
        lastLoginTimestamp: Date.now(),
        streakDays: 0,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[ThirtyDayAlgo] getAndroidLoginData failed:", error);
      return {
        thirtyDaysActivity: Array(30).fill(0),
        dailyCompletedCounts: Array(30).fill(0),
        lastLoginTimestamp: Date.now(),
        streakDays: 0,
      };
    }
  }

  /**
   * Clear cache (on logout)
   */
  clearCache(): void {
    const keys = dynamicTargetMMKV.getAllKeys();
    keys.forEach((key) => {
      if (
        key.startsWith("dynamic_target_chapter_") ||
        key.startsWith("thirty_days_") ||
        key.startsWith("daily_counts_")
      ) {
        dynamicTargetMMKV.delete(key);
      }
    });
  }
}

export const ThirtyDayAlgo = new ThirtyDayAlgoEngine();
export default ThirtyDayAlgo;
