FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/services/EventEngine/DailyEventCenter.ts
=====================================
/**
 * src/services/EventEngine/DailyEventCenter.ts
 * Daily Event Center - Master Event Dashboard Logic
 * Handles: 4 Stages, 490% Bank, 20% Daily Package, Lock/Unlock, Credit Rewards
 * UI Structure:
 * [Header: 490% Bank]
 * [Stage 1 ACTIVE - 5%]
 * [Stage 2 LOCKED - 5% (Stage 1 Complete Requirement)]
 * [Stage 3 LOCKED]
 * [Stage 4 LOCKED]
 */

import WeaknessAnalyzer, {
  StageNumber,
  FullWeaknessAnalysis,
} from "./WeaknessAnalyzer";
import MappingPipeline, {
  EventCard,
  DailyEventPipelineResult,
} from "./MappingPipeline";
import BankManager from "../../storage/DailyBank/BankManager";
import { dailyBankMMKV } from "../../storage/MMKVInstance";
import WordProgressKey from "../../storage/keys/WordProgressKey";

export interface DailyEventState {
  bankBalance: number;
  todayEarned: number;
  stages: {
    stage1: {
      isLocked: boolean;
      cards: EventCard[];
      completedCount: number;
      totalCount: number;
      creditEarned: number;
    };
    stage2: {
      isLocked: boolean;
      cards: EventCard[];
      completedCount: number;
      totalCount: number;
      creditEarned: number;
    };
    stage3: {
      isLocked: boolean;
      cards: EventCard[];
      completedCount: number;
      totalCount: number;
      creditEarned: number;
    };
    stage4: {
      isLocked: boolean;
      cards: EventCard[];
      completedCount: number;
      totalCount: number;
      creditEarned: number;
    };
  };
  totalCreditAvailable: number; // 20%
  totalCreditEarnedToday: number;
  isAllCompleted: boolean;
}

class DailyEventCenterSingleton {
  /**
   * Initialize Daily Event Center - Build all stages from chapter wordIds
   * Called on DailyEventScreen mount
   */
  async initialize(chapterWordIds: number[]): Promise<DailyEventState> {
    const bank = BankManager.getBalance();
    const pipelineResult: DailyEventPipelineResult =
      await MappingPipeline.buildDailyEventCards(chapterWordIds);

    // Check completed cards from MMKV
    const completedCardIds = this.getCompletedCardIds();

    // Build stage states
    const stage1Completed = pipelineResult.stage1Cards.filter((c) =>
      completedCardIds.includes(c.id)
    ).length;
    const stage2Completed = pipelineResult.stage2Cards.filter((c) =>
      completedCardIds.includes(c.id)
    ).length;
    const stage3Completed = pipelineResult.stage3Cards.filter((c) =>
      completedCardIds.includes(c.id)
    ).length;
    const stage4Completed = pipelineResult.stage4Cards.filter((c) =>
      completedCardIds.includes(c.id)
    ).length;

    const stage1Locked = false; // Stage 1 always ACTIVE
    const stage2Locked =
      stage1Completed < pipelineResult.stage1Cards.length &&
      pipelineResult.stage1Cards.length > 0;
    const stage3Locked = !WeaknessAnalyzer.getCompletedStages().includes(2);
    const stage4Locked = !WeaknessAnalyzer.getCompletedStages().includes(3);

    // If stage 1 all completed, unlock stage 2
    if (
      !stage2Locked &&
      pipelineResult.stage1Cards.length > 0 &&
      stage1Completed === pipelineResult.stage1Cards.length
    ) {
      WeaknessAnalyzer.markStageCompleted(1);
    }

    const state: DailyEventState = {
      bankBalance: bank.totalBalance,
      todayEarned: bank.todayEarned,
      stages: {
        stage1: {
          isLocked: stage1Locked,
          cards: pipelineResult.stage1Cards,
          completedCount: stage1Completed,
          totalCount: pipelineResult.stage1Cards.length,
          creditEarned: stage1Completed * 1,
        },
        stage2: {
          isLocked: stage2Locked,
          cards: pipelineResult.stage2Cards,
          completedCount: stage2Completed,
          totalCount: pipelineResult.stage2Cards.length,
          creditEarned: stage2Completed * 1,
        },
        stage3: {
          isLocked: stage3Locked,
          cards: pipelineResult.stage3Cards,
          completedCount: stage3Completed,
          totalCount: pipelineResult.stage3Cards.length,
          creditEarned: stage3Completed * 1,
        },
        stage4: {
          isLocked: stage4Locked,
          cards: pipelineResult.stage4Cards,
          completedCount: stage4Completed,
          totalCount: pipelineResult.stage4Cards.length,
          creditEarned: stage4Completed * 1,
        },
      },
      totalCreditAvailable: pipelineResult.totalCreditAvailable,
      totalCreditEarnedToday:
        stage1Completed + stage2Completed + stage3Completed + stage4Completed,
      isAllCompleted:
        pipelineResult.stage1Cards.length +
          pipelineResult.stage2Cards.length +
          pipelineResult.stage3Cards.length +
          pipelineResult.stage4Cards.length ===
        stage1Completed + stage2Completed + stage3Completed + stage4Completed,
    };

    console.log(
      `[DailyEventCenter] Initialized: Bank ${bank.totalBalance}%, Stage1 ${stage1Completed}/${pipelineResult.stage1Cards.length}`
    );

    return state;
  }

  /**
   * On Event Card Click - Navigate to WordDetail and increment mode
   * After completion, mark card as completed and add +1% to bank
   */
  async onCardCompleted(
    card: EventCard
  ): Promise<{ newBankBalance: number; shouldUnlockNextStage: boolean }> {
    // Mark card as completed in MMKV
    const completedIds = this.getCompletedCardIds();
    if (!completedIds.includes(card.id)) {
      completedIds.push(card.id);
      dailyBankMMKV.set("completed_event_cards", JSON.stringify(completedIds));

      // Add +1% to bank
      const newBank = await BankManager.addCredit(
        card.creditPercent,
        `event_card_${card.id}`
      );

      // Check if stage completed
      const shouldUnlockNext = await this.checkStageCompletion(card.stage);

      console.log(
        `[DailyEventCenter] Card ${card.id} completed, +${card.creditPercent}% | Bank: ${newBank.totalBalance}%`
      );

      return {
        newBankBalance: newBank.totalBalance,
        shouldUnlockNextStage: shouldUnlockNext,
      };
    }

    return {
      newBankBalance: BankManager.getBalance().totalBalance,
      shouldUnlockNextStage: false,
    };
  }

  private async checkStageCompletion(stage: StageNumber): Promise<boolean> {
    const completedStages = WeaknessAnalyzer.getCompletedStages();
    const isAlreadyCompleted = completedStages.includes(stage);

    if (isAlreadyCompleted) {
      return false;
    }

    // For stage 1, if all its cards completed, mark stage completed and unlock next
    if (stage === 1) {
      WeaknessAnalyzer.markStageCompleted(1);
      return true;
    } else if (stage === 2) {
      WeaknessAnalyzer.markStageCompleted(2);
      return true;
    } else if (stage === 3) {
      WeaknessAnalyzer.markStageCompleted(3);
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  }

  private getCompletedCardIds(): string[] {
    const raw = dailyBankMMKV.getString("completed_event_cards");
    if (!raw) {
      return [];
    }
    try {
      return JSON.parse(raw) as string[];
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  }

  /**
   * Reset daily event (new day)
   * Keeps bank balance but resets completed cards if new day
   */
  resetIfNewDay(): boolean {
    const lastReset = dailyBankMMKV.getString("last_event_reset_date");
    const today = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];

    if (lastReset !== today) {
      // New day - reset completed cards
      dailyBankMMKV.delete("completed_event_cards");
      dailyBankMMKV.set("completed_event_stages", JSON.stringify([1])); // Reset to only stage 1 active
      dailyBankMMKV.set("last_event_reset_date", today);
      console.log("[DailyEventCenter] New day - reset event cards");
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  }

  /**
   * Get Bank Display for UI Header
   * "ডেইলি ব্যাংক টোটাল ব্যালেন্স: ৪৯০% ক্রেডিট (আজকের অর্জন: +২০% | মোট উপলব্ধ: ৪৯০%)"
   */
  getBankDisplay(): { total: number; today: number; displayText: string } {
    const bank = BankManager.getBalance();
    return {
      total: bank.totalBalance,
      today: bank.todayEarned,
      displayText: `ডেইলি ব্যাংক টোটাল ব্যালেন্স: ${bank.totalBalance}% ক্রেডিট (আজকের অর্জন: +${bank.todayEarned}% | মোট উপলব্ধ: ${bank.totalBalance}%)`,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Full reset (for testing)
   */
  async fullReset(): Promise<void> {
    dailyBankMMKV.delete("completed_event_cards");
    dailyBankMMKV.delete("completed_event_stages");
    dailyBankMMKV.delete("last_event_reset_date");
    await BankManager.reset();
    console.log("[DailyEventCenter] Full reset done");
  }
}

export const DailyEventCenter = new DailyEventCenterSingleton();
export default DailyEventCenter;


=====================================
FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/services/EventEngine/MappingPipeline.ts
=====================================
/**
 * src/services/EventEngine/MappingPipeline.ts
 * Mapping Pipeline: MMKV Primary Keys (word_id) -> SQLite Display Data (display_word_id, chapter_name)
 * Converts raw weakness word_ids to enriched event card data: "অপরিচিতা #৩৫, #৭৮"
 * Uses Batch IN Query for performance
 */

import {
  fetchDisplayDataFromPrimaryKeys,
  toEventCardDisplay,
  EnrichedDisplayData,
} from "../../database/queries/BatchFetch";
import WeaknessAnalyzer, {
  FullWeaknessAnalysis,
  StageNumber,
  WeaknessStage,
} from "./WeaknessAnalyzer";
import { ModeNumber } from "../../storage/keys/WordProgressKey";

export interface EventCard {
  id: string; // e.g., "stage1_m1"
  stage: StageNumber;
  mode: ModeNumber;
  modeIcon: string; // volume-high, play-circle-outline etc (No Emoji)
  title: string; // e.g., "অপরিচিতা #৩৫, #৭৮, #৮৮, #৮৯"
  displayIds: number[]; // [35,78,88,89]
  wordIds: number[]; // Internal [67,896...]
  enriched: EnrichedDisplayData[];
  creditPercent: number; // +1%
  isCompleted: boolean;
  chapterName: string;
}

export interface DailyEventPipelineResult {
  stage1Cards: EventCard[]; // 5 cards max (m1-m5)
  stage2Cards: EventCard[];
  stage3Cards: EventCard[];
  stage4Cards: EventCard[];
  allCards: EventCard[];
  totalCreditAvailable: number; // 20% (4 stages * 5%)
  totalUniqueWords: number;
}

class MappingPipelineSingleton {
  private readonly MODE_ICONS: Record<
    ModeNumber,
    { icon: string; label: string }
  > = {
    1: { icon: "volume-high", label: "Sound" }, // m1 🔊 -> volume-high
    2: { icon: "play-circle-outline", label: "Play1" }, // m2 ▶️ -> play-circle-outline
    3: { icon: "book-open-page-variant", label: "Default" }, // m3 📖 -> book
    4: { icon: "lightbulb-on-outline", label: "Practice" }, // m4 😊 -> lightbulb
    5: { icon: "play-speed", label: "Play2" }, // m5 🎯 -> play-speed
  };

  /**
   * Core: Convert Weakness Analysis to Event Cards with Display Data
   * Input: wordIds of a chapter (e.g., 20 words)
   * Output: Enriched Event Cards ready for UI
   */
  async buildDailyEventCards(
    wordIds: number[]
  ): Promise<DailyEventPipelineResult> {
    // 1. Analyze weakness into 4 stages
    const completedStages = WeaknessAnalyzer.getCompletedStages();
    const analysis: FullWeaknessAnalysis = WeaknessAnalyzer.analyze(
      wordIds,
      completedStages
    );

    // 2. Collect all word_ids from all stages for batch fetch (optimize)
    const allWordIdsToFetch = new Set<number>();
    [
      analysis.stage1_1ModeLeft,
      analysis.stage2_2ModesLeft,
      analysis.stage3_3ModesLeft,
      analysis.stage4_4ModesLeft,
    ].forEach((stage) => {
      Object.values(stage.groups).forEach((group) => {
        group.forEach((id) => allWordIdsToFetch.add(id));
      });
    });

    // 3. Batch fetch all display data at once (single IN query)
    const { enriched: allEnriched, chapterGrouped } =
      await fetchDisplayDataFromPrimaryKeys(Array.from(allWordIdsToFetch));

    // Create lookup map for fast access
    const enrichedMap = new Map<number, EnrichedDisplayData>();
    allEnriched.forEach((e) => enrichedMap.set(e.wordId, e));

    // 4. Build cards per stage
    const stage1Cards = this.buildStageCards(
      analysis.stage1_1ModeLeft,
      enrichedMap
    );
    const stage2Cards = this.buildStageCards(
      analysis.stage2_2ModesLeft,
      enrichedMap
    );
    const stage3Cards = this.buildStageCards(
      analysis.stage3_3ModesLeft,
      enrichedMap
    );
    const stage4Cards = this.buildStageCards(
      analysis.stage4_4ModesLeft,
      enrichedMap
    );

    const allCards = [
      ...stage1Cards,
      ...stage2Cards,
      ...stage3Cards,
      ...stage4Cards,
    ];
    const totalCredit = allCards.reduce(
      (sum, card) => sum + card.creditPercent,
      0
    );
    const uniqueWords = new Set(allCards.flatMap((c) => c.wordIds)).size;

    console.log(
      `[MappingPipeline] Built ${allCards.length} cards, ${totalCredit}% total credit, ${uniqueWords} unique words`
    );

    return {
      stage1Cards,
      stage2Cards,
      stage3Cards,
      stage4Cards,
      allCards,
      totalCreditAvailable: totalCredit,
      totalUniqueWords: uniqueWords,
    };
  }

  private buildStageCards(
    stage: WeaknessStage,
    enrichedMap: Map<number, EnrichedDisplayData>
  ): EventCard[] {
    const cards: EventCard[] = [];

    // Each mode (m1-m5) that has words becomes a card
    (Object.entries(stage.groups) as Array<[string, number[]]>).forEach(
      ([modeKey, wordIds]) => {
        if (wordIds.length === 0) {
          return;
        }

        const mode = Number(modeKey.replace("m", "")) as ModeNumber;
        const enriched = wordIds
          .map((id) => enrichedMap.get(id))
          .filter(Boolean) as EnrichedDisplayData[];

        if (enriched.length === 0) {
          return;
        }

        // Group by chapter for display (usually same chapter, but handle multi)
        const byChapter = enriched.reduce((acc, cur) => {
          if (!acc[cur.chapterName]) {
            acc[cur.chapterName] = [];
          }
          acc[cur.chapterName].push(cur);
          return acc;
        }, {} as Record<string, EnrichedDisplayData[]>);

        // Create one card per chapter group (or combine if same chapter)
        Object.entries(byChapter).forEach(([chapterName, chapterEnriched]) => {
          const displayIds = chapterEnriched.map((e) => e.displayWordId);
          const title = `${chapterName} ${displayIds
            .map((id) => `#${id}`)
            .join(", ")}`;
          const iconInfo = this.MODE_ICONS[mode];

          cards.push({
            id: `stage${stage.stage}_m${mode}_${chapterName}`,
            stage: stage.stage,
            mode,
            modeIcon: iconInfo.icon,
            title,
            displayIds,
            wordIds: chapterEnriched.map((e) => e.wordId),
            enriched: chapterEnriched,
            creditPercent: 1, // Each card +1%
            isCompleted: false,
            chapterName,
          });
        });
      }
    );

    return cards;
  }

  /**
   * Helper: Get cards for a specific stage (for lazy loading)
   */
  async getStageCards(
    wordIds: number[],
    stage: StageNumber
  ): Promise<EventCard[]> {
    const completedStages = WeaknessAnalyzer.getCompletedStages();
    const analysis = WeaknessAnalyzer.analyze(wordIds, completedStages);

    const stageData = {
      1: analysis.stage1_1ModeLeft,
      2: analysis.stage2_2ModesLeft,
      3: analysis.stage3_3ModesLeft,
      4: analysis.stage4_4ModesLeft,
    }[stage];

    const allIds = Object.values(stageData.groups).flat();
    const { enriched } = await fetchDisplayDataFromPrimaryKeys(allIds);

    const enrichedMap = new Map<number, EnrichedDisplayData>();
    enriched.forEach((e) => enrichedMap.set(e.wordId, e));

    return this.buildStageCards(stageData, enrichedMap);
  }

  /**
   * Convert event cards to UI display format (No Emoji, Vector Icons Only)
   * For DailyEventScreen: [Icon] [Title] [+1%]
   */
  toUIDisplay(
    cards: EventCard[]
  ): Array<{ icon: string; text: string; credit: string; cardId: string }> {
    return cards.map((card) => ({
      icon: card.modeIcon, // volume-high, play-circle-outline etc
      text: card.title, // "অপরিচিতা #৩৫, #৭৮"
      credit: `+${card.creditPercent}%`,
      cardId: card.id,
    }));
  }
}

export const MappingPipeline = new MappingPipelineSingleton();
export default MappingPipeline;


=====================================
FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/services/EventEngine/WeaknessAnalyzer.ts
=====================================
/**
 * src/services/EventEngine/WeaknessAnalyzer.ts
 * 4-Stage Weakness Analyzer - Most Neglected Words Detection
 * Stage 1: 1 Mode Left (4 done, easiest)
 * Stage 2: 2 Modes Left (3 done, medium)
 * Stage 3: 3 Modes Left (2 done, deep study)
 * Stage 4: 4 Modes Left (1 done, weakness remover)
 * Input: 20*5=100 MMKV Keys, Output: 4 Stages grouped by missing modes
 */

import WordProgressKey, {
  ModeNumber,
} from "../../storage/keys/WordProgressKey";
import { dailyBankMMKV } from "../../storage/MMKVInstance";

export type StageNumber = 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;

export interface WeaknessStage {
  stage: StageNumber;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  creditPercent: number; // 5% per stage
  groups: {
    m1: number[]; // word_ids where mode 1 is missing
    m2: number[];
    m3: number[];
    m4: number[];
    m5: number[];
  };
  totalUniqueWords: number;
  totalCards: number; // Sum of all missing slots
  isLocked: boolean;
  unlockRequirement: string;
}

export interface FullWeaknessAnalysis {
  stage1_1ModeLeft: WeaknessStage; // ACTIVE - Easiest
  stage2_2ModesLeft: WeaknessStage; // LOCKED initially
  stage3_3ModesLeft: WeaknessStage; // LOCKED
  stage4_4ModesLeft: WeaknessStage; // LOCKED - Weakest
  overallStats: {
    totalWords: number;
    completedWords: number;
    averageModesCompleted: number;
    mostNeglectedMode: ModeNumber;
  };
}

class WeaknessAnalyzerSingleton {
  /**
   * Core: Analyze wordIds (e.g., Chapter 5's 20 words) into 4 stages
   */
  analyze(
    wordIds: number[],
    completedStages: StageNumber[] = [1]
  ): FullWeaknessAnalysis {
    if (!wordIds || wordIds.length === 0) {
      return this.getEmptyAnalysis();
    }

    const stage1 = {
      m1: [] as number[],
      m2: [] as number[],
      m3: [] as number[],
      m4: [] as number[],
      m5: [] as number[],
    };
    const stage2 = {
      m1: [] as number[],
      m2: [] as number[],
      m3: [] as number[],
      m4: [] as number[],
      m5: [] as number[],
    };
    const stage3 = {
      m1: [] as number[],
      m2: [] as number[],
      m3: [] as number[],
      m4: [] as number[],
      m5: [] as number[],
    };
    const stage4 = {
      m1: [] as number[],
      m2: [] as number[],
      m3: [] as number[],
      m4: [] as number[],
      m5: [] as number[],
    };

    let totalCompleted = 0;
    const modeCompletionCount = { m1: 0, m2: 0, m3: 0, m4: 0, m5: 0 };

    for (const wordId of wordIds) {
      const progress = WordProgressKey.getWordProgress(wordId);

      if (progress.isCompleted) {
        totalCompleted++;
        // Count for stats
        Object.keys(modeCompletionCount).forEach((key) => {
          const mode = key as keyof typeof modeCompletionCount;
          modeCompletionCount[mode]++;
        });
        continue;
      }

      const missing: ModeNumber[] = [];
      if (progress.m1 === 0) {
        missing.push(1);
      } else {
        modeCompletionCount.m1++;
      }
      if (progress.m2 === 0) {
        missing.push(2);
      } else {
        modeCompletionCount.m2++;
      }
      if (progress.m3 === 0) {
        missing.push(3);
      } else {
        modeCompletionCount.m3++;
      }
      if (progress.m4 === 0) {
        missing.push(4);
      } else {
        modeCompletionCount.m4++;
      }
      if (progress.m5 === 0) {
        missing.push(5);
      } else {
        modeCompletionCount.m5++;
      }

      const missingCount = missing.length;

      if (missingCount === 0) {
        totalCompleted++;
      } else if (missingCount === 1) {
        // Stage 1: 1 Mode Left - 4 modes done, easiest
        missing.forEach((m) =>
          stage1[`m${m}` as keyof typeof stage1].push(wordId)
        );
      } else if (missingCount === 2) {
        // Stage 2: 2 Modes Left - 3 done
        missing.forEach((m) =>
          stage2[`m${m}` as keyof typeof stage2].push(wordId)
        );
      } else if (missingCount === 3) {
        // Stage 3: 3 Modes Left - 2 done
        missing.forEach((m) =>
          stage3[`m${m}` as keyof typeof stage3].push(wordId)
        );
      } else if (missingCount === 4) {
        // Stage 4: 4 Modes Left - 1 done, most neglected
        missing.forEach((m) =>
          stage4[`m${m}` as keyof typeof stage4].push(wordId)
        );
      } else if (missingCount === 5) {
        // All 5 missing - also Stage 4 (never touched)
        missing.forEach((m) =>
          stage4[`m${m}` as keyof typeof stage4].push(wordId)
        );
      }
    }

    // Find most neglected mode
    let mostNeglected: ModeNumber = 1;
    let minCount = modeCompletionCount.m1;
    (
      Object.entries(modeCompletionCount) as Array<
        [keyof typeof modeCompletionCount, number]
      >
    ).forEach(([key, count]) => {
      if (count < minCount) {
        minCount = count;
        mostNeglected = Number(key.replace("m", "")) as ModeNumber;
      }
    });

    const totalWords = wordIds.length;
    const avgModesCompleted =
      totalWords > 0
        ? Object.values(modeCompletionCount).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) /
          totalWords
        : 0;

    const createStage = (
      stageNum: StageNumber,
      groups: typeof stage1,
      isLocked: boolean
    ): WeaknessStage => {
      const unique = new Set([
        ...groups.m1,
        ...groups.m2,
        ...groups.m3,
        ...groups.m4,
        ...groups.m5,
      ]);
      const totalCards =
        groups.m1.length +
        groups.m2.length +
        groups.m3.length +
        groups.m4.length +
        groups.m5.length;

      const titles = {
        1: "পর্যায় ১ (ACTIVE) — ৫%",
        2: "পর্যায় ২ (LOCKED) — ৫%",
        3: "পর্যায় ৩ (LOCKED) — ৫%",
        4: "পর্যায় ৪ (LOCKED) — ৫%",
      };

      const descs = {
        1: "সবচেয়ে সহজ - ১টি Mode বাকি",
        2: "মাঝারি - ২টি Mode বাকি (পর্যায় ১ শেষ করুন)",
        3: "গভীর পড়া - ৩টি Mode বাকি",
        4: "দুর্বলতা দূরীকরণ - ৪টি Mode বাকি",
      };

      return {
        stage: stageNum,
        title: titles[stageNum],
        description: descs[stageNum],
        creditPercent: 5,
        groups,
        totalUniqueWords: unique.size,
        totalCards,
        isLocked,
        unlockRequirement:
          stageNum === 1
            ? "Unlocked"
            : `পর্যায় ${stageNum - 1} এর সকল টাস্ক সম্পন্ন করুন`,
      };
    };

    const isStage2Locked =
      !completedStages.includes(1) ||
      stage1.m1.length +
        stage1.m2.length +
        stage1.m3.length +
        stage1.m4.length +
        stage1.m5.length >
        0;
    const isStage3Locked = !completedStages.includes(2);
    const isStage4Locked = !completedStages.includes(3);

    return {
      stage1_1ModeLeft: createStage(1, stage1, false),
      stage2_2ModesLeft: createStage(2, stage2, isStage2Locked),
      stage3_3ModesLeft: createStage(3, stage3, isStage3Locked),
      stage4_4ModesLeft: createStage(4, stage4, isStage4Locked),
      overallStats: {
        totalWords,
        completedWords: totalCompleted,
        averageModesCompleted: Math.round(avgModesCompleted * 10) / 10,
        mostNeglectedMode: mostNeglected,
      },
    };
  }

  private getEmptyAnalysis(): FullWeaknessAnalysis {
    const emptyGroups = { m1: [], m2: [], m3: [], m4: [], m5: [] };
    const createEmpty = (stage: StageNumber): WeaknessStage => ({
      stage,
      title: `পর্যায় ${stage}`,
      description: "",
      creditPercent: 5,
      groups: { ...emptyGroups },
      totalUniqueWords: 0,
      totalCards: 0,
      isLocked: stage !== 1,
      unlockRequirement:
        stage === 1 ? "Unlocked" : `পর্যায় ${stage - 1} শেষ করুন`,
    });

    return {
      stage1_1ModeLeft: createEmpty(1),
      stage2_2ModesLeft: createEmpty(2),
      stage3_3ModesLeft: createEmpty(3),
      stage4_4ModesLeft: createEmpty(4),
      overallStats: {
        totalWords: 0,
        completedWords: 0,
        averageModesCompleted: 0,
        mostNeglectedMode: 1,
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Get words for a specific mode in a stage (for Event Card rendering)
   * e.g., Stage 1, Mode 1: word_ids where only m1 is left
   */
  getWordsForStageMode(
    analysis: FullWeaknessAnalysis,
    stage: StageNumber,
    mode: ModeNumber
  ): number[] {
    const stageKey = {
      1: "stage1_1ModeLeft",
      2: "stage2_2ModesLeft",
      3: "stage3_3ModesLeft",
      4: "stage4_4ModesLeft",
    }[stage] as keyof FullWeaknessAnalysis;

    const stageData = analysis[stageKey] as WeaknessStage;
    return stageData.groups[`m${mode}` as keyof typeof stageData.groups] || [];
  }

  /**
   * Save completed stages to MMKV (for lock/unlock)
   */
  markStageCompleted(stage: StageNumber): void {
    const key = "completed_event_stages";
    const raw = dailyBankMMKV.getString(key);
    let completed: StageNumber[] = [];
    try {
      if (raw) {
        completed = JSON.parse(raw);
      }
    } catch {}

    if (!completed.includes(stage)) {
      completed.push(stage);
      dailyBankMMKV.set(key, JSON.stringify(completed));
      console.log(`[WeaknessAnalyzer] Stage ${stage} marked completed`);
    }
  }

  getCompletedStages(): StageNumber[] {
    const raw = dailyBankMMKV.getString("completed_event_stages");
    if (!raw) {
      return [1];
    } // Stage 1 always active initially
    try {
      return JSON.parse(raw) as StageNumber[];
    } catch {
      return [1];
    }
  }
}

export const WeaknessAnalyzer = new WeaknessAnalyzerSingleton();
export default WeaknessAnalyzer;


=====================================
FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/services/ProgressEngine/ChapterEncapsulation.ts
=====================================
/**
 * src/services/ProgressEngine/ChapterEncapsulation.ts
 * Chapter Box Encapsulation - Saves per-mode progress into separate MMKV keys
 * Key: chapter_{id}_mode_{n}_progress
 * Purpose: Drawer instant read without recalculation
 */

import { chapterBoxMMKV } from "../../storage/MMKVInstance";
import PerModeCalculator, { PerModeResult } from "./PerModeCalculator";
import WordProgressKey from "../../storage/keys/WordProgressKey";

export interface ChapterBox {
  chapter_id: number;
  mode1: number;
  mode2: number;
  mode3: number;
  mode4: number;
  mode5: number;
  totalCompletedWords: number;
  totalWords: number;
  totalProgressPercent: number;
  lastUpdated: number;
}

class ChapterEncapsulationSingleton {
  private getModeKey(chapter_id: number, mode: number): string {
    return `chapter_${chapter_id}_mode_${mode}_progress`;
  }

  private getTotalKey(chapter_id: number): string {
    return `chapter_${chapter_id}_total_progress`;
  }

  private getLastUpdatedKey(chapter_id: number): string {
    return `chapter_${chapter_id}_last_updated`;
  }

  private getCompletedWordsKey(chapter_id: number): string {
    return `chapter_${chapter_id}_completed_words`;
  }

  /**
   * Core: saveChapterProgress(chapterId, chapterWordIds)
   * Called after PerModeCalculator calculation
   * This is the encapsulation layer from your architecture
   */
  saveChapterProgress(chapter_id: number, wordIds: number[]): ChapterBox {
    const result: PerModeResult =
      PerModeCalculator.calculateAllModesProgress(wordIds);

    // Save each mode as separate key for instant drawer read
    chapterBoxMMKV.set(this.getModeKey(chapter_id, 1), result.mode1);
    chapterBoxMMKV.set(this.getModeKey(chapter_id, 2), result.mode2);
    chapterBoxMMKV.set(this.getModeKey(chapter_id, 3), result.mode3);
    chapterBoxMMKV.set(this.getModeKey(chapter_id, 4), result.mode4);
    chapterBoxMMKV.set(this.getModeKey(chapter_id, 5), result.mode5);
    chapterBoxMMKV.set(
      this.getTotalKey(chapter_id),
      result.totalProgressPercent
    );
    chapterBoxMMKV.set(
      this.getCompletedWordsKey(chapter_id),
      result.totalCompletedWords
    );
    chapterBoxMMKV.set(this.getLastUpdatedKey(chapter_id), Date.now());

    const box: ChapterBox = {
      chapter_id,
      mode1: result.mode1,
      mode2: result.mode2,
      mode3: result.mode3,
      mode4: result.mode4,
      mode5: result.mode5,
      totalCompletedWords: result.totalCompletedWords,
      totalWords: wordIds.length,
      totalProgressPercent: result.totalProgressPercent,
      lastUpdated: Date.now(),
    };

    console.log(
      `[ChapterEncapsulation] Saved chapter_${chapter_id}: ${result.totalProgressPercent}%`
    );

    return box;
  }

  /**
   * Instant read for Drawer - No calculation
   * chapter_5_mode_1_progress = 80
   */
  getChapterBox(chapter_id: number): ChapterBox {
    return {
      chapter_id,
      mode1: chapterBoxMMKV.getNumber(this.getModeKey(chapter_id, 1)) || 0,
      mode2: chapterBoxMMKV.getNumber(this.getModeKey(chapter_id, 2)) || 0,
      mode3: chapterBoxMMKV.getNumber(this.getModeKey(chapter_id, 3)) || 0,
      mode4: chapterBoxMMKV.getNumber(this.getModeKey(chapter_id, 4)) || 0,
      mode5: chapterBoxMMKV.getNumber(this.getModeKey(chapter_id, 5)) || 0,
      totalCompletedWords:
        chapterBoxMMKV.getNumber(this.getCompletedWordsKey(chapter_id)) || 0,
      totalWords: 0,
      totalProgressPercent:
        chapterBoxMMKV.getNumber(this.getTotalKey(chapter_id)) || 0,
      lastUpdated:
        chapterBoxMMKV.getNumber(this.getLastUpdatedKey(chapter_id)) || 0,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Get total progress for Drawer Green Bar
   * chapter_5_total_progress
   */
  getTotalProgress(chapter_id: number): number {
    return chapterBoxMMKV.getNumber(this.getTotalKey(chapter_id)) || 0;
  }

  /**
   * Incremental update after single word increment
   * Called from WordProgressKey.incrementMode()
   */
  incrementalUpdate(chapter_id: number, wordIds: number[]): ChapterBox {
    return this.saveChapterProgress(chapter_id, wordIds);
  }

  /**
   * Save multiple chapters at once (Home Screen)
   */
  saveMultipleChapters(
    chapterWordMap: Record<number, number[]>
  ): Record<number, ChapterBox> {
    const result: Record<number, ChapterBox> = {};
    for (const [idStr, wordIds] of Object.entries(chapterWordMap)) {
      const chapterId = Number(idStr);
      result[chapterId] = this.saveChapterProgress(chapterId, wordIds);
    }
    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Delete chapter box (Manifesto delete)
   */
  deleteChapterBox(chapter_id: number): void {
    for (let mode = 1; mode <= 5; mode++) {
      chapterBoxMMKV.delete(this.getModeKey(chapter_id, mode));
    }
    chapterBoxMMKV.delete(this.getTotalKey(chapter_id));
    chapterBoxMMKV.delete(this.getCompletedWordsKey(chapter_id));
    chapterBoxMMKV.delete(this.getLastUpdatedKey(chapter_id));
  }

  /**
   * Debug log - Shows 100 key encapsulation
   */
  debug(chapter_id: number): void {
    const box = this.getChapterBox(chapter_id);
    console.log(`=== Chapter ${chapter_id} Encapsulated Box ===`);
    console.log(`chapter_${chapter_id}_mode_1_progress = ${box.mode1}%`);
    console.log(`chapter_${chapter_id}_mode_2_progress = ${box.mode2}%`);
    console.log(`chapter_${chapter_id}_mode_3_progress = ${box.mode3}%`);
    console.log(`chapter_${chapter_id}_mode_4_progress = ${box.mode4}%`);
    console.log(`chapter_${chapter_id}_mode_5_progress = ${box.mode5}%`);
    console.log(
      `chapter_${chapter_id}_total_progress = ${box.totalProgressPercent}%`
    );
  }
}

export const ChapterEncapsulation = new ChapterEncapsulationSingleton();
export default ChapterEncapsulation;

FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/services/ProgressEngine/Guards/ChapterExecutive.ts

/**
 * src/services/ProgressEngine/Guards/ChapterExecutive.ts
 * 80% Manager - Chapter Executive that decides final unlock
 * Logic:
 * - Natural Unlock: completed >= target (80% or dynamic 50-100%)
 * - Bank Unlock: 50% + Bank Credit = 80%
 * - Says "চমৎকার! ৮০% পূরণ হয়েছে" on auto unlock
 * Handles both Default 80% and Dynamic Target from 30-Day Algo
 */

import ChapterTable, {
  ChapterUnlockStatus,
} from "../../../database/ChapterTable";
import BankManager from "../../../storage/DailyBank/BankManager";
import DefaultGuard from "./DefaultGuard";
import { eventsMMKV } from "../../../storage/MMKVInstance";
import DynamicTarget from "../../../storage/DailyBank/DynamicTarget";

export interface ExecutiveResult {
  isUnlocked: boolean;
  unlockType: "natural" | "bank" | "locked";
  completedPercent: number;
  targetPercent: number; // 80 or dynamic from 30-Day Algo
  needsBankCredit: number;
  canUseBank: boolean;
  bankBalance: number;
  message: string;
  celebrationMessage?: string; // "চমৎকার! ৮০% পূরণ হয়েছে"
}

class ChapterExecutiveSingleton {
  /**
   * Core: Check if chapter should be unlocked
   * This is the final decision maker - 80% Manager
   */
  async check(chapter_id: number): Promise<ExecutiveResult> {
    const bankBalance = BankManager.getBalance().totalBalance;
    const dynamicTarget = DynamicTarget.getCurrentTarget(); // 50-100% from 30-Day Algo

    // Get status with current bank balance
    const status: ChapterUnlockStatus =
      await ChapterTable.checkChapterUnlockStatus(chapter_id, bankBalance);

    // Override target with dynamic if available
    const targetPercent =
      dynamicTarget > 50 ? dynamicTarget : status.targetPercent;

    // Recalculate with dynamic target
    const completedPercent = status.completedPercent;
    const isNaturalUnlock = completedPercent >= targetPercent;
    const needsBankCredit = Math.max(0, targetPercent - completedPercent);
    const is50Passed = completedPercent >= 50;
    const canUseBank = is50Passed && bankBalance >= needsBankCredit;
    const isBankUnlockPossible = is50Passed && canUseBank;

    const isUnlocked =
      status.isUnlocked ||
      isNaturalUnlock ||
      (is50Passed &&
        completedPercent + Math.min(bankBalance, needsBankCredit) >=
          targetPercent);

    let unlockType: ExecutiveResult["unlockType"] = "locked";
    let message = "";
    let celebration: string | undefined;

    if (isNaturalUnlock) {
      unlockType = "natural";
      message = `Natural unlock: ${completedPercent}% >= ${targetPercent}%`;
      celebration = `চমৎকার! ${targetPercent}% পূরণ হয়েছে`;
    } else if (isUnlocked && is50Passed) {
      unlockType = "bank";
      message = `Bank unlock: ${completedPercent}% + ${needsBankCredit}% Bank = ${targetPercent}%`;
      celebration = `ব্যাংক ক্রেডিট দিয়ে আনলক! ${completedPercent}% + ${needsBankCredit}% = ${
        completedPercent + needsBankCredit
      }%`;
    } else {
      message = `Locked: ${completedPercent}% / ${targetPercent}% - Need ${needsBankCredit}% more`;
    }

    return {
      isUnlocked,
      unlockType,
      completedPercent,
      targetPercent,
      needsBankCredit,
      canUseBank,
      bankBalance,
      message,
      celebrationMessage: celebration,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Attempt to unlock via Bank Credit (Manual button click)
   * User clicks [ -30% ব্যাংক ক্রেডিট কেটে আনলক করুন ]
   */
  async unlockViaBank(chapter_id: number): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    executiveResult: ExecutiveResult;
    message: string;
  }> {
    const guardResult = await DefaultGuard.check(chapter_id);

    if (!guardResult.passed) {
      const exec = await this.check(chapter_id);
      return {
        success: false,
        executiveResult: exec,
        message: `50% Guard not passed. Need 50%, have ${guardResult.completedPercent}%`,
      };
    }

    const exec = await this.check(chapter_id);

    if (exec.isUnlocked && exec.unlockType === "natural") {
      return {
        success: true,
        executiveResult: exec,
        message: "Already naturally unlocked",
      };
    }

    if (!exec.canUseBank) {
      return {
        success: false,
        executiveResult: exec,
        message: `Insufficient bank. Need ${exec.needsBankCredit}%, have ${exec.bankBalance}%`,
      };
    }

    // Deduct and unlock
    const deductResult = await BankManager.deductCredit(
      exec.needsBankCredit,
      `chapter_${chapter_id}_unlock`
    );

    if (!deductResult.success) {
      return {
        success: false,
        executiveResult: exec,
        message: deductResult.message,
      };
    }

    // Unlock in DB
    const unlocked = await ChapterTable.unlockChapter(chapter_id);

    if (unlocked) {
      // Emit celebration event
      eventsMMKV.set(`chapter_${chapter_id}_unlocked_via_bank`, true);
      eventsMMKV.set(`chapter_${chapter_id}_unlocked_at`, Date.now());

      const newExec = await this.check(chapter_id);

      return {
        success: true,
        executiveResult: newExec,
        message:
          newExec.celebrationMessage ||
          `Unlocked via Bank! Deducted ${exec.needsBankCredit}%`,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: false,
      executiveResult: exec,
      message: "Unlock failed due to DB error",
    };
  }

  /**
   * Auto unlock check (called after word completion)
   * If natural 80% reached, auto unlock without bank
   */
  async checkAutoUnlock(chapter_id: number): Promise<boolean> {
    const exec = await this.check(chapter_id);

    if (exec.unlockType === "natural" && !exec.isUnlocked) {
      const unlocked = await ChapterTable.unlockChapter(chapter_id);
      if (unlocked) {
        eventsMMKV.set(`chapter_${chapter_id}_auto_unlocked`, true);
        eventsMMKV.set(`chapter_${chapter_id}_unlocked_at`, Date.now());
        console.log(
          `[ChapterExecutive] ${exec.celebrationMessage} - Chapter ${chapter_id} auto-unlocked!`
        );
        return true;
      }
    }

    return exec.isUnlocked;
  }

  /**
   * Get all chapters executive status (Home Screen)
   */
  async checkAllChapters(
    chapterIds: number[]
  ): Promise<Record<number, ExecutiveResult>> {
    const result: Record<number, ExecutiveResult> = {};

    for (const chapterId of chapterIds) {
      result[chapterId] = await this.check(chapterId);
    }

    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Get display text for Chapter Card
   * Shows: "৭৫% সম্পন্ন | ৮০% প্রয়োজন" or "৫০% + ৩০% ব্যাংক = ৮০% আনলক"
   */
  getDisplayText(execResult: ExecutiveResult): {
    primary: string;
    secondary: string;
    showBankButton: boolean;
  } {
    if (execResult.isUnlocked) {
      return {
        primary: `${execResult.completedPercent}% সম্পন্ন`,
        secondary:
          execResult.unlockType === "natural" ? "আনলকড" : "ব্যাংক দিয়ে আনলকড",
        showBankButton: false,
      };
    }

    if (execResult.completedPercent >= 50) {
      return {
        primary: `${execResult.completedPercent}% সম্পন্ন | ${execResult.targetPercent}% প্রয়োজন`,
        secondary: `[-${execResult.needsBankCredit}% ব্যাংক ক্রেডিট কেটে আনলক করুন]`,
        showBankButton: true,
      };
    }

    return {
      primary: `${execResult.completedPercent}% সম্পন্ন | ${execResult.targetPercent}% প্রয়োজন`,
      secondary: `আরও ${
        50 - execResult.completedPercent
      }% প্রয়োজন ব্যাংক ব্যবহারের জন্য`,
      showBankButton: false,
    };
  }
}

export const ChapterExecutive = new ChapterExecutiveSingleton();
export default ChapterExecutive;

FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/services/ProgressEngine/Guards/DefaultGuard.ts

/**
 * src/services/ProgressEngine/Guards/DefaultGuard.ts
 * 50% Guard - Default Guard that allows Bank Credit usage
 * Logic: User must achieve 50% naturally, then can use Bank to reach 80%
 * Invisible UI Magic Trigger: When 50% passed, show [-30% Bank Credit] button
 */

import ChapterTable, {
  ChapterUnlockStatus,
} from "../../../database/ChapterTable";
import { eventsMMKV } from "../../../storage/MMKVInstance";
import BankManager from "../../../storage/DailyBank/BankManager";

export interface GuardResult {
  passed: boolean;
  completedPercent: number;
  requiredPercent: number; // 50
  message: string;
  canUseBank: boolean;
  needsBankCredit: number;
  bankBalance: number;
}

class DefaultGuardSingleton {
  private readonly REQUIRED_PERCENT = 50;

  /**
   * Check 50% Guard
   * Called when checking chapter unlock status
   */
  async check(chapter_id: number): Promise<GuardResult> {
    const bankBalance = BankManager.getBalance().totalBalance;
    const status: ChapterUnlockStatus =
      await ChapterTable.checkChapterUnlockStatus(chapter_id, bankBalance);

    const passed = status.is50GuardPassed;
    const canUseBank = passed && bankBalance >= status.needsBankCredit;

    return {
      passed,
      completedPercent: status.completedPercent,
      requiredPercent: this.REQUIRED_PERCENT,
      message: passed
        ? `50% Guard PASSED (${status.completedPercent}%) - Can use Bank Credit`
        : `50% Guard FAILED - Need ${this.REQUIRED_PERCENT}%, have ${status.completedPercent}%`,
      canUseBank,
      needsBankCredit: status.needsBankCredit,
      bankBalance,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Emit event when 50% guard passed
   * This triggers Invisible UI Magic: Show bank button in Chapter Card
   */
  emitGuardPassed(chapter_id: number, completedPercent: number): void {
    eventsMMKV.set(`chapter_${chapter_id}_50_guard_passed`, true);
    eventsMMKV.set(`chapter_${chapter_id}_guard_event_at`, Date.now());
    eventsMMKV.set(`chapter_${chapter_id}_completed_percent`, completedPercent);

    console.log(
      `[DefaultGuard] Chapter ${chapter_id} 50% PASSED at ${completedPercent}% - Invisible Magic Triggered`
    );
  }

  /**
   * Check if guard passed for UI rendering
   * Instant MMKV read for Chapter Card
   */
  isGuardPassedInstant(chapter_id: number): boolean {
    return (
      eventsMMKV.getBoolean(`chapter_${chapter_id}_50_guard_passed`) || false
    );
  }

  /**
   * Get guard status for all chapters (Home Screen)
   */
  async checkAllChapters(
    chapterIds: number[]
  ): Promise<Record<number, GuardResult>> {
    const result: Record<number, GuardResult> = {};

    for (const chapterId of chapterIds) {
      result[chapterId] = await this.check(chapterId);
    }

    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Reset guard (when chapter reset)
   */
  resetGuard(chapter_id: number): void {
    eventsMMKV.delete(`chapter_${chapter_id}_50_guard_passed`);
    eventsMMKV.delete(`chapter_${chapter_id}_guard_event_at`);
    eventsMMKV.delete(`chapter_${chapter_id}_completed_percent`);
  }
}

export const DefaultGuard = new DefaultGuardSingleton();
export default DefaultGuard;

FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/services/ProgressEngine/PerModeCalculator.ts

/**
 * src/services/ProgressEngine/PerModeCalculator.ts
 * Single Loop (20*5=100) Progress Calculator
 * Calculates all 5 modes progress in ONE loop, not 5 separate loops
 * Performance: O(n) instead of O(5n)
 */

import WordProgressKey from "../../storage/keys/WordProgressKey";
import { chapterBoxMMKV } from "../../storage/MMKVInstance";

export interface PerModeResult {
  mode1: number; // 0-100%
  mode2: number;
  mode3: number;
  mode4: number;
  mode5: number;
  totalCompletedWords: number;
  totalWords: number;
  totalProgressPercent: number; // view_id=1 percentage
  perModeCompleted: {
    m1Done: number;
    m2Done: number;
    m3Done: number;
    m4Done: number;
    m5Done: number;
  };
}

class PerModeCalculatorSingleton {
  /**
   * Core: Single Loop Calculator
   * Input: wordIds = [12,45,89,103,156,...985] (20 words)
   * Instead of:
   *   loop1: count m1 >0
   *   loop2: count m2 >0 ... (5 loops = 100 iterations)
   * Do:
   *   single loop: check m1,m2,m3,m4,m5 in one pass (20 iterations)
   */
  calculateAllModesProgress(wordIds: number[]): PerModeResult {
    if (!wordIds || wordIds.length === 0) {
      return {
        mode1: 0,
        mode2: 0,
        mode3: 0,
        mode4: 0,
        mode5: 0,
        totalCompletedWords: 0,
        totalWords: 0,
        totalProgressPercent: 0,
        perModeCompleted: {
          m1Done: 0,
          m2Done: 0,
          m3Done: 0,
          m4Done: 0,
          m5Done: 0,
        },
      };
    }

    let m1Done = 0;
    let m2Done = 0;
    let m3Done = 0;
    let m4Done = 0;
    let m5Done = 0;
    let totalCompleted = 0;

    // SINGLE LOOP - As per your architecture
    for (const wordId of wordIds) {
      const progress = WordProgressKey.getWordProgress(wordId);

      // Check each mode >0 in same iteration
      if (progress.m1 > 0) {
        m1Done++;
      }
      if (progress.m2 > 0) {
        m2Done++;
      }
      if (progress.m3 > 0) {
        m3Done++;
      }
      if (progress.m4 > 0) {
        m4Done++;
      }
      if (progress.m5 > 0) {
        m5Done++;
      }

      if (progress.isCompleted) {
        totalCompleted++;
      }
    }

    const total = wordIds.length;

    return {
      mode1: Math.round((m1Done / total) * 100),
      mode2: Math.round((m2Done / total) * 100),
      mode3: Math.round((m3Done / total) * 100),
      mode4: Math.round((m4Done / total) * 100),
      mode5: Math.round((m5Done / total) * 100),
      totalCompletedWords: totalCompleted,
      totalWords: total,
      totalProgressPercent: Math.round((totalCompleted / total) * 100),
      perModeCompleted: { m1Done, m2Done, m3Done, m4Done, m5Done },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Calculate for multiple chapters at once (for Home Screen)
   * Input: { chapter_5: [12,45...], chapter_6: [...] }
   */
  calculateMultiChapters(
    chapterWordMap: Record<number, number[]>
  ): Record<number, PerModeResult> {
    const result: Record<number, PerModeResult> = {};

    for (const [chapterIdStr, wordIds] of Object.entries(chapterWordMap)) {
      const chapterId = Number(chapterIdStr);
      result[chapterId] = this.calculateAllModesProgress(wordIds);
    }

    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Get Weakness Data for Event Engine (4 Stages)
   * Uses WordProgressKey.analyzeWeakness()
   */
  getWeaknessAnalysis(wordIds: number[]) {
    return WordProgressKey.analyzeWeakness(wordIds);
  }

  /**
   * Quick Percentage for a single mode (for Drawer instant)
   */
  getSingleModePercentage(wordIds: number[], mode: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5): number {
    if (wordIds.length === 0) {
      return 0;
    }

    let done = 0;
    for (const wordId of wordIds) {
      const count = WordProgressKey.getModeCount(wordId, mode);
      if (count > 0) {
        done++;
      }
    }

    return Math.round((done / wordIds.length) * 100);
  }
}

export const PerModeCalculator = new PerModeCalculatorSingleton();
export default PerModeCalculator;


=====================================
FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/services/ProgressEngine/WordCompletion.ts
=====================================
/**
 * src/services/ProgressEngine/WordCompletion.ts
 * Word Completion Checker: m1>0 && m2>0 && m3>0 && m4>0 && m5>0 => view_id=1
 * Triggers SQLite update and Chapter Guards
 */

import WordProgressKey, {
  WordModeProgress,
} from "../../storage/keys/WordProgressKey";
import { updateDatabaseViewId } from "../../database/queries/UnlockQuery";
import SQLiteManager from "../../database/SQLiteManager";
import ChapterTable from "../../database/ChapterTable";
import { eventsMMKV } from "../../storage/MMKVInstance";

export interface CompletionResult {
  word_id: number;
  isCompleted: boolean;
  wasNewlyCompleted: boolean; // true if view_id changed 0->1 now
  progress: WordModeProgress;
}

class WordCompletionSingleton {
  /**
   * Core check: All 5 modes >0 ?
   * From architecture: checkWordCompletionAndTrigger(wordId)
   */
  checkWordCompletion(word_id: number): CompletionResult {
    const progress = WordProgressKey.getWordProgress(word_id);
    return {
      word_id,
      isCompleted: progress.isCompleted,
      wasNewlyCompleted: false, // Will be set after DB check
      progress,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Check and trigger SQLite view_id=1 + Chapter Guards
   * Called on every mode increment
   */
  async checkAndTrigger(word_id: number): Promise<CompletionResult> {
    const progress = WordProgressKey.getWordProgress(word_id);
    const isCompleted = progress.isCompleted;

    if (!isCompleted) {
      return {
        word_id,
        isCompleted: false,
        wasNewlyCompleted: false,
        progress,
      };
    }

    // Check current view_id in SQLite to avoid unnecessary write
    try {
      const [result] = await SQLiteManager.executeSql(
        "SELECT view_id, chapter_id FROM WordTable WHERE word_id = ?;",
        [word_id]
      );

      if (result.rows.length === 0) {
        return {
          word_id,
          isCompleted: true,
          wasNewlyCompleted: false,
          progress,
        };
      }

      const currentViewId = result.rows.item(0).view_id as number;
      const chapter_id = result.rows.item(0).chapter_id as number;

      if (currentViewId === 1) {
        // Already completed
        return {
          word_id,
          isCompleted: true,
          wasNewlyCompleted: false,
          progress,
        };
      }

      // New completion! Update to 1
      const updated = await updateDatabaseViewId(word_id, 1);

      if (updated) {
        console.log(`[WordCompletion] Word ${word_id} completed! view_id 0->1`);

        // Trigger chapter check
        await this.onWordCompleted(chapter_id);

        return {
          word_id,
          isCompleted: true,
          wasNewlyCompleted: true,
          progress,
        };
      }

      return { word_id, isCompleted: true, wasNewlyCompleted: false, progress };
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[WordCompletion] Failed for ${word_id}:`, error);
      return { word_id, isCompleted: true, wasNewlyCompleted: false, progress };
    }
  }

  /**
   * After word completes, check chapter guards (50% & 80%)
   */
  private async onWordCompleted(chapter_id: number): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const status = await ChapterTable.checkChapterUnlockStatus(chapter_id, 0);

      // Auto unlock if 80% reached
      if (status.is80ManagerPassed && !status.isUnlocked) {
        await ChapterTable.unlockChapter(chapter_id);
        console.log(
          `[WordCompletion] Chapter ${chapter_id} auto-unlocked via 80%`
        );
      }

      // Emit 50% guard event for Invisible UI Magic
      if (status.is50GuardPassed) {
        eventsMMKV.set(`chapter_${chapter_id}_50_guard_passed`, true);
        eventsMMKV.set(`chapter_${chapter_id}_guard_event_at`, Date.now());
        eventsMMKV.set(
          `chapter_${chapter_id}_completed_percent`,
          status.completedPercent
        );
        console.log(
          `[WordCompletion] Chapter ${chapter_id} 50% Guard PASSED (${status.completedPercent}%) - Show Bank Button`
        );
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[WordCompletion] onWordCompleted failed:", error);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Batch check for multiple words (Daily Event)
   */
  async batchCheck(wordIds: number[]): Promise<{
    newlyCompleted: number[];
    alreadyCompleted: number[];
    incomplete: number[];
  }> {
    const newlyCompleted: number[] = [];
    const alreadyCompleted: number[] = [];
    const incomplete: number[] = [];

    for (const wordId of wordIds) {
      const result = await this.checkAndTrigger(wordId);
      if (!result.isCompleted) {
        incomplete.push(wordId);
      } else if (result.wasNewlyCompleted) {
        newlyCompleted.push(wordId);
      } else {
        alreadyCompleted.push(wordId);
      }
    }

    return { newlyCompleted, alreadyCompleted, incomplete };
  }

  /**
   * Reset word (for re-study)
   */
  async resetWord(word_id: number): Promise<void> {
    WordProgressKey.resetWord(word_id);
    await updateDatabaseViewId(word_id, 0);
    console.log(`[WordCompletion] Word ${word_id} reset to 0`);
  }
}

export const WordCompletion = new WordCompletionSingleton();
export default WordCompletion;

FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/services/TTSEngine/AutoPlayEngine.ts

/**
 * Src/services/TTSEngine/AutoPlayEngine.ts & src/services/TTSEngine/AutoPlayEngine.ts
 * Auto-Play Engine - m2 & m5 Sequential Play with Gap + Repeat + Random/Parallel
 * Features: Single Card Repeat, Card-to-Card Repeat, Random, Parallel, Background
 */

import TTSController, { TTSMode } from "./TTSController";
import TTSStateKey from "../../storage/keys/TTSStateKey";
import { ttsBrainMMKV } from "../../storage/MMKVInstance";
import { PlayModeFilters } from "../../types/progress.types";
import WordProgressKey from "../../storage/keys/WordProgressKey";

export interface AutoPlayQueueItem {
  word_id: number;
  display_word_id: number;
  chapter_name: string;
  content: string;
}

export interface AutoPlayConfig {
  mode: TTSMode; // m2 or m5
  queue: AutoPlayQueueItem[];
  filters: PlayModeFilters;
  gapMs: number;
  speechRate: number;
}

class AutoPlayEngineSingleton {
  private isAutoPlaying = false;
  private currentIndex = 0;
  private queue: AutoPlayQueueItem[] = [];
  private config: AutoPlayConfig | null = null;
  private shouldStop = false;

  /**
   * Start Auto-Play Sequentially
   * Implements: Play Icon 1 & Play Icon 2 with all filter logics
   */
  async start(config: AutoPlayConfig): Promise<void> {
    if (this.isAutoPlaying) {
      await this.stop();
    }

    this.config = config;
    this.queue = this.buildQueue(config);
    this.currentIndex = 0;
    this.isAutoPlaying = true;
    this.shouldStop = false;

    console.log(
      `[AutoPlayEngine] Starting ${config.mode} with ${this.queue.length} items, gap ${config.gapMs}ms`
    );

    await this.playNext();
  }

  private buildQueue(config: AutoPlayConfig): AutoPlayQueueItem[] {
    let queue = [...config.queue];
    const filters = config.filters;

    // Filter: Single Card Repeat
    if (filters.singleRepeat && filters.singleRepeatNumber !== null) {
      const targetId = filters.singleRepeatNumber;
      const filtered = queue.filter(
        (item) => item.display_word_id === targetId
      );
      if (filtered.length > 0 && filters.singleRepeatChapter) {
        // Also filter by chapter name if provided
        const byChapter = filtered.filter((item) =>
          item.chapter_name.includes(filters.singleRepeatChapter)
        );
        queue = byChapter.length > 0 ? byChapter : filtered;
      } else {
        queue = filtered.length > 0 ? filtered : queue;
      }
      // Repeat single card
      if (queue.length === 1) {
        // Keep single, will loop
        console.log(
          `[AutoPlayEngine] Single Repeat Mode: ${queue[0].chapter_name} #${queue[0].display_word_id}`
        );
      }
    }
    // Filter: Card-to-Card Repeat
    else if (
      filters.cardToCardRepeat &&
      filters.cardToCardStart !== null &&
      filters.cardToCardEnd !== null
    ) {
      const min = Math.min(filters.cardToCardStart, filters.cardToCardEnd);
      const max = Math.max(filters.cardToCardStart, filters.cardToCardEnd);
      queue = queue.filter(
        (item) => item.display_word_id >= min && item.display_word_id <= max
      );
      if (filters.cardToCardChapter) {
        queue = queue.filter((item) =>
          item.chapter_name.includes(filters.cardToCardChapter)
        );
      }
      console.log(
        `[AutoPlayEngine] Card-to-Card Repeat: ${min}-${max}, ${queue.length} cards`
      );
    }

    // Parallel vs Random
    if (filters.random) {
      // Shuffle
      queue = this.shuffleArray(queue);
      console.log(
        `[AutoPlayEngine] Random Mode: Shuffled ${queue.length} cards`
      );
    } else if (filters.parallel) {
      // Keep sequential (normal)
      console.log(
        `[AutoPlayEngine] Parallel Mode: Sequential ${queue.length} cards`
      );
    }

    return queue;
  }

  private shuffleArray<T>(array: T[]): T[] {
    const shuffled = [...array];
    for (let i = shuffled.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
      const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
      [shuffled[i], shuffled[j]] = [shuffled[j], shuffled[i]];
    }
    return shuffled;
  }

  private async playNext(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.shouldStop || !this.config || this.queue.length === 0) {
      this.isAutoPlaying = false;
      return;
    }

    if (this.currentIndex >= this.queue.length) {
      // Check if repeat modes - loop
      if (
        this.config.filters.singleRepeat ||
        this.config.filters.cardToCardRepeat
      ) {
        this.currentIndex = 0;
        console.log("[AutoPlayEngine] Looping queue...");
      } else {
        console.log("[AutoPlayEngine] Auto-play completed");
        this.isAutoPlaying = false;
        return;
      }
    }

    const currentItem = this.queue[this.currentIndex];
    const gapMs = this.config.gapMs;

    console.log(
      `[AutoPlayEngine] Playing ${this.currentIndex + 1}/${
        this.queue.length
      }: #${currentItem.display_word_id}`
    );

    try {
      // Set TTS Mode
      TTSStateKey.setActiveMode(this.config.mode);

      // Play with TTSController - sequential
      await TTSController.playSequentialWord(
        currentItem.word_id,
        currentItem.content,
        this.config.mode
      );

      // Wait for TTS to complete + gap
      await this.waitForCompletion(currentItem.word_id);

      // Gap delay
      if (gapMs > 0) {
        await this.delay(gapMs);
      }

      // Mark progress
      const modeNum = this.config.mode === "m2" ? 2 : 5;
      await WordProgressKey.incrementMode(
        currentItem.word_id,
        modeNum as any,
        1
      );

      this.currentIndex++;

      if (!this.shouldStop) {
        await this.playNext();
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[AutoPlayEngine] PlayNext failed:", error);
      this.isAutoPlaying = false;
    }
  }

  private async waitForCompletion(wordId: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      const checkInterval = setInterval(() => {
        const currentState = TTSStateKey.getCurrentState();
        if (currentState === "STOPPED" || this.shouldStop) {
          clearInterval(checkInterval);
          resolve();
        }
      }, 100);

      // Timeout after 30 seconds
      setTimeout(() => {
        clearInterval(checkInterval);
        resolve();
      }, 30000);
    });
  }

  private delay(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
  }

  async stop(): Promise<void> {
    this.shouldStop = true;
    this.isAutoPlaying = false;
    await TTSController.stopImmediately("USER_STOP");
    console.log("[AutoPlayEngine] Stopped");
  }

  async pause(): Promise<void> {
    await TTSController.pause(
      (TTSController as any).getResumePosition ? 0 : 0,
      0
    );
    console.log("[AutoPlayEngine] Paused");
  }

  async resume(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.config && this.currentIndex < this.queue.length) {
      await TTSController.resume("", 0);
      console.log("[AutoPlayEngine] Resumed");
    }
  }

  isPlaying(): boolean {
    return this.isAutoPlaying;
  }

  getCurrentIndex(): number {
    return this.currentIndex;
  }

  getQueue(): AutoPlayQueueItem[] {
    return this.queue;
  }
}

export const AutoPlayEngine = new AutoPlayEngineSingleton();
export default AutoPlayEngine;

/**
 * Src/services/TTSEngine/BackgroundAudio.ts & src/services/TTSEngine/BackgroundAudio.ts
 * Background Audio Engine - Handles App Background/Inactive State
 * Rule: m2 & m5 can play in background if background_enabled=true
 * m1,m3,m4 stop on Navigation Exit or Background
 */

import TTSController, { TTSMode } from "./TTSController";
import TTSStateKey from "../../storage/keys/TTSStateKey";
import { AppState, AppStateStatus } from "react-native";
import { ttsBrainMMKV } from "../../storage/MMKVInstance";

class BackgroundAudioSingleton {
  private appStateSubscription: any = null;
  private isBackgroundEnabled = false;

  /**
   * Initialize Background Listener
   */
  initialize(): void {
    this.isBackgroundEnabled = TTSStateKey.isBackgroundEnabled();

    this.appStateSubscription = AppState.addEventListener(
      "change",
      this.handleAppStateChange.bind(this)
    );

    console.log(
      `[BackgroundAudio] Initialized, background_enabled=${this.isBackgroundEnabled}`
    );
  }

  private handleAppStateChange(nextAppState: AppStateStatus): void {
    const activeMode = TTSStateKey.getActiveMode() as TTSMode | null;
    const currentState = TTSStateKey.getCurrentState();

    console.log(
      `[BackgroundAudio] AppState changed to ${nextAppState}, mode=${activeMode}, state=${currentState}`
    );

    if (nextAppState.match(/inactive|background/)) {
      // App going to background
      this.onAppBackground(activeMode, currentState);
    } else if (nextAppState === "active") {
      // App returning to foreground
      this.onAppForeground();
    }
  }

  private onAppBackground(
    activeMode: TTSMode | null,
    currentState: string
  ): void {
    const bgEnabled = TTSStateKey.isBackgroundEnabled();

    // Rule: Only m2 & m5 can continue in background if enabled
    if (!bgEnabled) {
      console.log("[BackgroundAudio] Background disabled, stopping TTS");
      TTSController.onNavigationExit();
      return;
    }

    if (activeMode !== "m2" && activeMode !== "m5") {
      console.log(
        `[BackgroundAudio] Mode ${activeMode} cannot play in background, stopping`
      );
      TTSController.onNavigationExit();
      return;
    }

    // m2/m5 + background_enabled = continue playing
    console.log(`[BackgroundAudio] Mode ${activeMode} continues in background`);
    // Keep playing, don't stop
  }

  private onAppForeground(): void {
    console.log("[BackgroundAudio] App returned to foreground");
    // Optionally resume if paused due to background
  }

  /**
   * Set Background Enabled (From Settings ControlPanel2)
   */
  setBackgroundEnabled(enabled: boolean): void {
    this.isBackgroundEnabled = enabled;
    TTSStateKey.setBackgroundEnabled(enabled);
    ttsBrainMMKV.set("tts_background_enabled", enabled);
    console.log(`[BackgroundAudio] Background enabled set to ${enabled}`);
  }

  isBackgroundEnabledState(): boolean {
    return TTSStateKey.isBackgroundEnabled();
  }

  /**
   * Should Continue In Background?
   */
  shouldContinueInBackground(mode: TTSMode): boolean {
    if (!this.isBackgroundEnabled) {
      return false;
    }
    return mode === "m2" || mode === "m5";
  }

  /**
   * Cleanup
   */
  cleanup(): void {
    if (this.appStateSubscription) {
      this.appStateSubscription.remove();
      this.appStateSubscription = null;
    }
  }
}

export const BackgroundAudio = new BackgroundAudioSingleton();
export default BackgroundAudio;

FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/services/TTSEngine/HighlightingEngine.ts

/**
 * src/services/TTSEngine/HighlightingEngine.ts
 * TTS UI Highlighting - Yellow + Primary Color Dynamic Glow
 * No Hardcode Theme, Border Glow, Real-time Highlight
 * Dependencies: @react-native-material/core theme context
 */

import { StyleSheet, TextStyle, ViewStyle } from "react-native";

// --- Theme Interface (No Hardcode) ---
export interface ThemeColors {
  primary: string;
  primaryLight: string;
  background: string;
  surface: string;
  text: string;
  textSecondary: string;
  border: string;
  highlightYellow: string; // Dynamic from theme, fallback #FFEB3B
  highlightPrimaryGlow: string; // Primary with opacity
}

export interface HighlightState {
  isHighlighted: boolean;
  word_id: number | null;
  startChar: number;
  endChar: number;
  mode: "m1" | "m2" | "m3" | "m4" | "m5" | null;
}

// Default fallback (Will be overridden by theme context)
const FALLBACK_THEME: ThemeColors = {
  primary: "#6750A4",
  primaryLight: "#E8DEF8",
  background: "#FFFBFE",
  surface: "#FFFFFF",
  text: "#1C1B1F",
  textSecondary: "#49454F",
  border: "#E7E0EC",
  highlightYellow: "#FFEB3B",
  highlightPrimaryGlow: "rgba(103, 80, 164, 0.15)",
};

class HighlightingEngineSingleton {
  private static instance: HighlightingEngineSingleton;
  private theme: ThemeColors = FALLBACK_THEME;
  private listeners: Array<(state: HighlightState) => void> = [];
  private currentState: HighlightState = {
    isHighlighted: false,
    word_id: null,
    startChar: 0,
    endChar: 0,
    mode: null,
  };

  private constructor() {}

  static getInstance(): HighlightingEngineSingleton {
    if (!HighlightingEngineSingleton.instance) {
      HighlightingEngineSingleton.instance = new HighlightingEngineSingleton();
    }
    return HighlightingEngineSingleton.instance;
  }

  /**
   * Update theme dynamically (No Hardcode Theme)
   * Called from ThemeContext or Material Core provider
   */
  setTheme(theme: Partial<ThemeColors>): void {
    this.theme = { ...this.theme, ...theme };
  }

  getTheme(): ThemeColors {
    return this.theme;
  }

  /**
   * Subscribe to highlight state changes
   */
  subscribe(listener: (state: HighlightState) => void): () => void {
    this.listeners.push(listener);
    return () => {
      this.listeners = this.listeners.filter((l) => l !== listener);
    };
  }

  private notify(): void {
    this.listeners.forEach((listener) => listener(this.currentState));
  }

  /**
   * Start Highlighting - Called when TTS starts reading a segment
   */
  startHighlight(
    word_id: number,
    startChar: number,
    endChar: number,
    mode: HighlightState["mode"]
  ): void {
    this.currentState = {
      isHighlighted: true,
      word_id,
      startChar,
      endChar,
      mode,
    };
    this.notify();
  }

  /**
   * Update Highlight during TTS Progress (Real-time)
   */
  updateHighlight(word_id: number, currentChar: number, length: number): void {
    this.currentState = {
      ...this.currentState,
      word_id,
      startChar: currentChar,
      endChar: currentChar + length,
      isHighlighted: true,
    };
    this.notify();
  }

  /**
   * End Highlighting - Auto revert to normal when TTS finishes
   */
  endHighlight(): void {
    this.currentState = {
      isHighlighted: false,
      word_id: null,
      startChar: 0,
      endChar: 0,
      mode: null,
    };
    this.notify();
  }

  /**
   * Get Card Container Style with Border Glow (Dynamic Height, Fixed Width)
   * Implements: Border Radius 12-16px, overflow hidden, dynamic glow
   */
  getCardContainerStyle(isActive: boolean, isHighlighted: boolean): ViewStyle {
    const outerRadius = 16; // As per Architecture: 12-16px perfect

    return {
      borderRadius: outerRadius,
      overflow: "hidden", // Smooth Corners for Android
      backgroundColor: this.theme.surface,
      borderWidth: isHighlighted ? 1.5 : 1,
      borderColor: isHighlighted ? this.theme.primary : this.theme.border,
      // Visual Depth with Primary Shade
      shadowColor: isHighlighted ? this.theme.primary : "#000",
      shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: isHighlighted ? 4 : 2 },
      shadowOpacity: isHighlighted ? 0.15 : 0.06,
      shadowRadius: isHighlighted ? 8 : 4,
      elevation: isHighlighted ? 6 : 2,
      // Card Spacing Architecture
      marginVertical: 6, // Half of 12px gap
      marginHorizontal: 16, // Fixed margin for responsive width
      padding: 16, // Inner breathing space
    } as ViewStyle;
  }

  /**
   * Get Highlighted Text Style - Yellow + Primary Adjusted
   */
  getHighlightedTextStyle(isHighlightedWord: boolean): TextStyle {
    if (!isHighlightedWord) {
      return {
        color: this.theme.text,
        backgroundColor: "transparent",
      };
    }

    return {
      color: this.theme.text,
      backgroundColor: this.theme.highlightYellow, // Yellow highlight
      borderRadius: 4, // Inner radius = outer - padding logic
      paddingHorizontal: 2,
      // Primary glow effect via text shadow
      textShadowColor: this.theme.highlightPrimaryGlow,
      textShadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 0 },
      textShadowRadius: 8,
    } as TextStyle;
  }

  /**
   * Get Inner Element Radius (Inner vs Outer Radius Rule)
   * Inner Radius = Outer Radius - Padding
   */
  getInnerRadius(outerRadius: number = 16, padding: number = 16): number {
    return Math.max(4, outerRadius - padding / 2); // Minimum 4px
  }

  /**
   * Render Text with Highlighting - Utility to split text
   * Used in ModeCard component
   */
  getHighlightedSegments(
    fullText: string,
    highlightState: HighlightState
  ): Array<{ text: string; highlighted: boolean }> {
    if (!highlightState.isHighlighted || highlightState.word_id === null) {
      return [{ text: fullText, highlighted: false }];
    }

    const { startChar, endChar } = highlightState;

    if (startChar >= fullText.length) {
      return [{ text: fullText, highlighted: false }];
    }

    const safeEnd = Math.min(endChar, fullText.length);

    return [
      { text: fullText.substring(0, startChar), highlighted: false },
      { text: fullText.substring(startChar, safeEnd), highlighted: true },
      { text: fullText.substring(safeEnd), highlighted: false },
    ].filter((seg) => seg.text.length > 0);
  }

  /**
   * Get Progress Indicator Style for Active TTS
   */
  getProgressBarStyle(progress: number): ViewStyle {
    return {
      height: 3,
      backgroundColor: this.theme.primary,
      width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(0, progress))}%`,
      borderRadius: 1.5,
    } as ViewStyle;
  }
}

export const HighlightingEngine = HighlightingEngineSingleton.getInstance();
export default HighlightingEngine;

// --- Stylesheet Factory (No Hardcode, Theme Aware) ---
export const createHighlightStyles = (theme: ThemeColors) =>
  StyleSheet.create({
    cardContainer: {
      borderRadius: 16,
      overflow: "hidden",
      backgroundColor: theme.surface,
      borderWidth: 1,
      borderColor: theme.border,
      marginVertical: 6,
      marginHorizontal: 16,
      padding: 16,
    },
    cardHighlighted: {
      borderColor: theme.primary,
      borderWidth: 1.5,
      shadowColor: theme.primary,
      shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 4 },
      shadowOpacity: 0.15,
      shadowRadius: 8,
      elevation: 6,
    },
    textNormal: {
      color: theme.text,
      fontSize: 16,
      lineHeight: 24,
    },
    textHighlighted: {
      color: theme.text,
      backgroundColor: theme.highlightYellow,
      borderRadius: 4,
      textShadowColor: theme.highlightPrimaryGlow,
      textShadowRadius: 8,
    },
    innerButton: {
      borderRadius: 12, // Inner = Outer - Padding (16 - 4)
      overflow: "hidden",
    },
  });

FILE: /data/data/com.termux/files/home/WordApp/src/services/TTSEngine/PauseResumeEngine.ts
